Natural Dodge [Heritage, Fast]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to, once per
Reset, negate a single effect, delivered by Weapon or
Packet, by which they are struck, as per the Skill
Dodge. This Skill does not count as a prerequisite for
any other Skill. The Call for this ability is "Dodge".
Natural Fortification [Heritage, Survivor]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may grant themselves a
"Physical Fortify 5" effect. Once per Reset, the
Character may Rest in order to restore all uses of this
Skill.
Natural Magic [Heritage, Magical]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to cast a
single Spell below 4th level once per Reset from
Memory. This Skill does not count as a prerequisite
for any other Skill.
Natural Parry [Heritage, Martial]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may negate
any attack delivered, by weapon or packet, as
<Effect>-Strike or Physical by which they are struck.
The call for this ability is "Parry".
If the Character has the Bodyguard Skill they can
use this ability to negate an applicable attack which
strikes another target within their Weapon's reach.
This Skill does not count as a prerequisite for any
other Skill.
Natural Remedy [Heritage, Wild]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill may spend
sixty(60) seconds examining the target, role playing
this examination involves checking their eyes, pallor,
and other physical features while gathering
ingredients from the surrounding area or their own
person. At the end of the minute the Character states
"Natural Remedy. Poison Dispel Poison.". This ability
is usable at will.
The Character is able to use this Skill on
themselves, and is even able to do so while they are
under an effect which prevents them from using
Skills.
Natural Ritualist [Heritage, Magical]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character may participate in the casting
of Ritual Magic as a Secondary Caster. This does not
grant the Character the ability to cast any Rituals,
only add one(1) to the count of total casters. If the
Character also has the Skill Ritual Magic, the number
of Casters the Character counts as is increased by
one(1). This Skill does not increase the number of
Invocations a Character can cast per Reset.
Natural Strength [Heritage, Strong]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character increases all of their Weapon
Damage by one(1). This Skill does not count as a
prerequisite for any other Skill. The effects of this Skill
stacks with all other Skills and effects including
Spells.
Natural Toughness [Heritage, Tough]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains five(5) additional Hit
Points.
Psychic Awaken [Heritage, Psychic]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver, by Touch or
Packet, a "Magic Dispel Compulsion". This ability
may also be used on themselves on a ten(10)-count
even if they were in a state where they normally
wouldn't be able to cast Spells.
Psychic Charm [Heritage, Psychic]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver, by Touch or
Packet, a "Magic Charm" effect.
Quickness [Heritage, Fast]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill gains ten(10)
points of Avoidance Armour.
Rapid Refit: Avoidance [Heritage, Fast]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may, on a three(3)-Count,
deliver a Physical Refit Avoidance Armour, to
themselves.
Resist Break [Heritage, Skilled]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single Break
effect by which they are struck. The call for this ability
is "Resist".
Resist Compulsion [Heritage, Willful]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single
Compulsion effect by which they are struck. The call
for this ability is "Resist".
Resist Curse [Heritage, Spiritual]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single Curse
effect by which they are struck. The call for this ability
is "Resist".
Resist Fumble [Heritage, Martial]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single Fumble
effect by which they are struck. The call for this ability
is "Resist".
Resist Magic [Heritage, Magical]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset the Character may negate a
single effect delivered as Magic(-Strike) by which
they are struck.
If the Character has the Skill Absorb Magic, they
also benefit from a "Magic Refit Arcane Armour". The
call for this ability is "Resist".
Resist Physical [Heritage, Tough]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset the Character may negate a
single effect delivered as Physical(-Strike) by which
they are struck. The call for this ability is "Resist".
Resist Poison [Heritage, Tough]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single effect
delivered as Poison(-Strike) by which they are struck.
The call for this ability is "Resist".
Sense Magic [Heritage, Skilled]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to spend a
sixty(60)-count to Detect the presence of magic on
any item they are touching.
If the Character has both this Skill and the Skill
Magical Aptitude they may identify what School(s) of
Magic are present on the item.
Sense Poison [Heritage, Survivor]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to spend a
sixty(60)-count to Detect the presence of Poison or
Alchemy on, or within, any item they are touching.
If the Character has both this Skill and either the
Skill Apothecary or Chemist they may Identify what
effect is present.
Vanish [Heritage, Stealthy]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may grant
themselves a "Lesser Physical Aegis" effect. The
Character must be in shadows or darkness to use
this ability.
Vorpal Strike [Heritage, Strong]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may grant themselves a
"Physical Vorpal 5" effect.
Weapon Familiarity [Heritage, Martial]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill gains Weapon Proficiency in a single One-Handed Weapon, or
Archery Weapon of their choice.
Chapter 4: Skill Systems
The Skill System
In Crucible, Skills represent a Character's natural
abilities and training in skills both mundane and
supernatural. A Character uses Skills to interact with
the game world in many different ways as well as to
represent a nebulous concept such as a profession or
vocational training. A Character's ability to perform
magic, create items, wield weapons and defend
themselves against various effects are all Skills. Skills
are purchased through the expenditure Skill Points
which a Character accumulates automatically as they
gain Experience Points. A Character can purchase
any Skill for which they meet the requirements and
have enough unspent, or "Free", Skill Points to pay
for the chosen Skill. The nature of these prerequisite
requirements vary greatly, some require a Character
to have one or more Skills, a requirement to purchase
Skills in a specific order, or even a Character Level
requirement limiting the number of times a Skill can
be bought until the Character meets the stated
Requirements.
Skills marked by an asterisk " * " can be purchased
more than once.
Skill Points and Levels
A Character's Level is equal to their total number of
Skill Points, divided by ten(10) and rounded down,
minus five(5). A new, first level, Player Character
starts with sixty(60) Skill Points. A Character obtains
experience not through combat but by simply
attending an Event. At the end of each Event, every
Player Character receives a number of Experience
Points equal to the total number of Skill Points
possessed by the Character for each Day of the
Event. For example, a Character with 100 Skill Points
would receive 200 Experience Points after attending
a normal two-day Event.
As a Character increases in level the number of
Experience Points needed to obtain Skill Points
slowly increases. A Character who is 1st level will
obtain significantly more Skill Points after an Event
than one who is 50th level.
Skill Pyramids
Buying Skills with the "Δ" requirement follows
special rules. This is found in Alchemy, Martial Arts
and Spells. It denotes that the Skills must be bought
in a "pyramid" fashion. A Character begins by
purchasing first level Slots. As the Character
purchases more Slots they must "build a pyramid". In
order to purchase Slots of any level they must have
at least two(2) more Slots of the level below and must
have a number of Slots above no less than two fewer
of the level above.
Skill Pyramids: Columning
Once a Character has purchased their 4th 1st
Level Slot they are no longer required to purchase
additional 1st level Slots. They may instead purchase
additional 2nd, 3rd, 4th, and so on, Slots until they
have four(4) ranks in each level. They may also
purchase additional 1st level Slots but still cannot
have a number of Slots of a particular level which is
greater than the number of Slots one level higher plus
two(2). See Table 4.2 for Skill progression up to a
"Four Column".
Skill Pyramids: Pyramiding
Alternatively, the Character may continue to
purchase Skills in a Pyramid fashion buying as many
low-level Slots as possible. This is done to maximise
the number of spells, or to improve utility over power.
There are a number of reasons why a Character may
choose to build in this way. Regardless of their
reasons, once the Character has a single 9th level
Slot it follows the same progression as purchasing
additional Skill Columns. See Table 4.3 for Skill
progression up to a "Pyramid".

Skill Tables
The following is the list of all Skills available for
purchase. This table is primarily for quick reference
and Character planning. Please refer to the individual
Skill Descriptions for a full list of requirements for the
use and purchase of all listed Skills. Generally, it is a
good idea to familiarise yourself with the various Skills
you encounter often, either because you have
purchased them for your Character or because they
are frequently used on, or against, your Character. It
is suggested that all Players read the Skills;
Diagnose, First Aid, Create Potion and Create Scroll.
Skill Priorities
There are four(4) Groups of Skills available to
Player Characters. These Groups are; Fighting,
Expertise, Magic, and Crafting. There are four(4)
Priorities which are particular Skill Groups can have.
These priorities, from highest to lowest are; A, B, C,
and D. The higher the Skill Groups' Priority, the lower
the Skill Point Cost for the Character to purchase the
Skill.
At the time of creation, the Character's Skill Group
Priorities are assigned. A Character can have no more
than one(1) Skill Group at each Priority level.
Additionally, if the Character has a Heritage
Drawback, they face limitations on how they can
assign their Skill Group Priorities.

Table 4.9 – Background Skills
Skill Descriptions
The following is a description of the Skills available
to Player Characters. Characters are able to use
Skills at any time unless they are under an effect
which prevents their use, such as Drain. There are
generally two types of Skill benefits. These are
passive benefits such as Weapon Training, while
others have limited benefits being usable a limited
number of times per Reset, such as Resist
Compulsion, or when the Character performs some
special counted action to use them, such as
Chemistry. Generally speaking, the use of any Skill,
even a passive one, during a Counted Action will
cause the action to be interrupted. This means that
you cannot use a Shield to block attacks while
delivering a Killing Blow, nor can you use Defensive
Abilities while casting a Spell.
The one exception to the above is the Skill Read
and Write. This is a special Skill which costs zero(0)
Skill Points and is always usable regardless of any
effect the Character is under unless the effect
explicitly states otherwise in its description. The use
of this Skill never interrupts any action counted or
otherwise.
Skill Name [Skill Group(s)]* *Skills Marked with
"*" can be purchased multiple times.
Requirements: Any Requirements which must be
met prior to the purchase of the Skill. When a Skill
lists another followed by a number, this indicates the
number of ranks in the given Skill the Character must
have in order to purchase that Skill. Some Skills even
list a number of Ranks in another Skill required for
every Rank the Character wants to purchase.
Benefit: The benefits the Skill grants the Character
as well as an explanation for how often it can be used
and any Delivery Verbal or Call which must be made
when using the Skill.
Skill Description List
Absorb Magic [Magic]
Requirements: Arcane Armour 1
Benefit: The Character may Absorb Spells which
they Touch Cast, from memory, on themselves.
These Spells, instead of their normal effect, restore
an amount of Arcane Armour equal to five(5) times
the level of the expended Spell Slot. The Spell's
effect must be one which is Delivered as Magic. The
call for this use is "<Incantation> <Delivery Verbal>,
Absorb".
If the Character has the Resist Magic Skill, they
may also use it to restore their Arcane Armour to full
value. The call for this specific use is "Resist, Magic
Refit Arcane Armour".
Absorb Poison [Crafting]
Requirements: Slime Armour 1
Benefit: The Character may Absorb Alchemy which
they Touch Cast, from memory, on themselves. This
Alchemy, instead of its normal effect, restores an
amount of Natural Armour equal to five(5) times the
level of the expended Alchemy Slot. The Alchemy's
effect must be one which is Delivered as Poison. The
call for this use is "<Delivery Verbal>, Absorb".

Academic [Background]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains an additional Rank in
the Academic Skill See Page 199 for information on
this Skill.
Acid Bomb [Expertise]*
Requirements: Acid Grenade 3 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a Packet attack
causing "20 Physical Acid" damage to the target. The
Character may throw any number of Packets when
using this Skill but must throw all Packets with the
same hand.
Acid Grenade [Expertise]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a Packet attack
causing "20 Physical Acid" damage to the target.
Alternatively, the Character may expend a single
use of this Skill in order to deliver a Packet attack
which causes a "Physical Break <Item>" effect.

Acid Trap [Expertise]*
Requirements: Acid Grenade 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may lay down an Acid Trap,
represented by a small yellow coloured cloth, or
similar marker, with an area of no more than 1m
2
which can be of any shape but may not contain voids.
Once the rep has been placed the Character must
spend ten(10) seconds to arm the Trap during which
time they must keep at least one hand on the Trap.
Once activated anyone who steps or touches the
Trap takes "20 Physical Acid" damage.
A Trap can be disarmed by striking it with a Break
or Destroy effect or by its creator spending ten(10)
seconds touching it in order to disarm it. Disarming a
Trap does not trigger its effects. A disarmed Trap is
considered Destroyed.
A Character may have no more than one(1) Trap,
of any type, placed at a time.
Alchemical Tradition [Crafting]
Requirements: Chemistry
Benefit: The Character is able to learn, and gain the
effects of, a single Alchemical Tradition. See Page 91
for more information on Alchemical Traditions.
Alchemical Slot [Crafting]*
Requirements: Chemistry, See Text
Benefit: This Character gains one Alchemy Slot of
the appropriate level as detailed under Pyramid Skills
on Page 32.
Alkahestry [Crafting]
Requirements: Level 9 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
cast Ritual Alchemy. Upon gaining this Skill the
Character can act as the Primary Caster, or as a
Secondary Caster, for any Ritual Alchemy.
Additionally, the Character gains the ability to cast
Invocations, powerful Alchemical effects tied to their
ability to perform Alkahestries. See Chapter 8: Rituals
for more information on casting Ritual Alchemy and
for the rules on casting Invocations.
The Character can also use this Skill to Identify
any number of Ritual Components on a sixty(60)-
count.
Ambush [Heritage, Stealthy]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may grant themselves the
following effects; "Physical Vorpal 3, Physical
Ambush 3".
Arcane Armour [Magic]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains five(5) points of Arcane
Armour for each time this Skill is bought. A Character
is still limited by their Wear Total which limits the
maximum number of Armour Points of any type from
which the Character may benefit.
Arcane Armour is represented by the Character
wearing a number of Runes equal to the total Armour
Point value of the armour divided by ten(10), rounded
up. These runes must be represented by and
inscribed upon worn accessories which are visible on
the Character.
If the Character is under an effect which prevents
them from using Skills, such as the Drain effect, they
are unable to Refit this armour using the Refit Armour
counted action until that effect ends.
Archery [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield
Bows and Crossbows in combat. The Character also
has the ability to use these Weapons to block
Weapon Attacks.
In order to fire a shot from their (Cross)Bow the
Character must remove an arrow, represented by an
Archery Packet, from their Quiver. Then touch the
Packet to the hand holding the (Cross)Bow and then
role-play the drawing of the arrow back before
throwing the Packet. For information on the
requirements for the Physical Representations for
Bows and Quivers, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Unlike most packet attacks, Archery Packets can
be blocked by Weapons and Shields. For more
information on attacking with Archery Packets see
Chapter 6: Combat.
Armour Mastery [Fighting]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character's familiarity with Physical
Armour grants them additional protection when
wearing it. For every time this Skill is bought, the
number of points of Physical Armour the Character
gains from wearing Physical Armour is increased by
five(5). The maximum bonus from this Skill is equal to
the Armour Points of the Physical Representation of
the suit of Physical Armour the Character is wearing,
or their maximum Wear Total, whichever is lower.
For example, a Character wearing a Physical
Representation worth fifteen(15) points is able to
benefit from no more than fifteen(15) additional
Armour Points from this Skill. Even if the armour
under the Enhance Armour effect.
Augment Potion [Crafting]
Requirements: Create Potion 20
Benefit: The Character may expend one use of a
single Spellshape or Mutation Skill. The Character
may imbue a single Potion with the effects of the
chosen Skill. Upon use, the Potion's effect, modified
by the imbued Spellshape or Mutation, is triggered.
If the Character has access to a Potion Laboratory,
they may target two(2) Potions with this ability.
The name of the imbued Spellshape or Mutation
must be included on the Potion's label. No Potion
may benefit from more than one Spellshape or
Mutation effect.
Augment Scroll [Crafting]
Requirements: Create Scroll 20
Benefit: The Character may expend one use of a
single Spellshape Skill. The Character may imbue a
single Scroll with the effects of the chosen
Spellshape. Upon use, the Scroll's effect, modified by
the imbued Spellshape, is triggered.
If the Character has access to a Scroll Laboratory,
they may target two(2) Scrolls with this ability.
The name of the imbued Spellshape must be
included on the Scroll. No Scroll may benefit from
more than one Spellshape effect.
Avoidance Armour [Expertise]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains five(5) points of
Avoidance Armour for each time this Skill is bought. A
Character is still limited by their Wear Total which
limits the maximum number of Armour Points of any
type from which the Character may benefit.
Avoidance Armour is represented by the Character
wearing a number of feathers equal to the total
Armour Point value of the armour divided by ten(10),
rounded up. These feathers must be attached to a
single accessory which is worn by, and visible upon,
the Character.
If the Character is under an effect which prevents
them from using Skills, such as the Drain effect, they
are unable to Refit this armour using the Refit Armour
counted action until that effect ends.
Axe Mastery [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield all
Axes in combat. When wielding a Two-Handed
Weapon, the Character may block attacks while
holding the Weapon with one hand but cannot attack,
or use the Weapon to deliver any Skill, until they have
both hands on the Two-Handed Weapon. At the time
this Skill is bought, the Character may "sell back" any
Skills which are included in its benefit. For information
of the requirements for the Physical Representations
for this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5:
Equipment.
Bodyguard [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
guard another. The Character may, once per Reset,
designate a Character as their charge. A Character
may have only one(1) charge at a time but may
change it as often as they Rest.
Once per Reset, the Character may negate any
Weapon Attack, or attack delivered, by Weapon or
Packet, as <Effect>-Strike or Physical by which their
charge is struck. The charge must be within
Weapon's reach of the Character to be targeted by
this effect. The call for this ability is "Parry".
If the Character has any uses of the Parry Skill, the
Character can use this ability to negate an applicable
attack which strikes their Charge so long as they are
within their Weapon's reach.
Break Compulsion [Heritage, Psychic]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill may spend
sixty(60) seconds, role playing with another creature.
This can either be in quiet concentration or by
speaking with the target. At the end of the minute, the
Character states "Break Compulsion. Arcane Dispel
Compulsion.". This ability is usable at will.
Alternatively, the Character may expend this Skill,
becoming unable to use its benefits until Reset, to
instantly negate all Compulsions on any target,
including themselves, by Touch. When used in this
manner the call is "Arcane Dispel Compulsion". When
used in this way the effect may be delivered as Touch
or by Packet. The Skill cannot be recovered by the
Inspiration effect.
Catalyst [Crafting, Mutation]*
Requirements: Level 6 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may double the damage or
healing of a single Alchemy with a Duration of Instant.
Channel Spell [Spellshape, Magic]*
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a single Spell as
an <Effect>-Strike using a Weapon, instead of a
Packet, with which they are proficient. The Delivery
Type used to deliver the effect is determined by the
Delivery Type of the Spell. For example, if the Spell
allows the Character to deliver a Magic Sleep effect,
the call for this ability is "Magic-Strike Sleep". If the
effect causes "10 Elemental Fire" damage, the
Channelled effect is "10 Elemental-Strike Fire". The
Character does not need a free hand to cast the Spell
if they are wielding a Weapon with which they are
Proficient. For more information on Channelling
Spells see Page 100.
Chemistry [Crafting]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character has the ability to Identity
Poisons and their presence in a substance by
spending a sixty(60)-count to examine the substance.
Contingent Slime [Crafting, Mutation]*
Requirements: Level 9 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may cast an Alchemy and have
it triggered upon one of the following conditions;
Dead, Dying, Berserked, Binding, Compelled,
Cursed, Enthraled, Imprisoned, Poisoned, Sleeping,
or Unconscious, chosen at the time of casting. If the
Character dies before this effect is used it is lost. The
call to first activate this ability is "I Invoke a <Delivery
Verbal> Slime, Contingent upon my <Trigger State>."
A Character may have only one Contingency effect,
from this Skill, at a time. The call when this ability is
triggered is "Contingency: <Delivery Verbal>".
Contingent Spell [Spellshape, Magic]*
Requirements: Level 9 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may cast a Spell and have it
triggered upon one of the following conditions; Dead,
Dying, Berserked, Binding, Compelled, Cursed,
Enthraled, Imprisoned, Poisoned, Sleeping, or
Unconscious, chosen at the time of casting. If the
Character dies before this effect is used it is lost. The
call to first activate this ability is "I Invoke a <Delivery
Verbal> Spell, Contingent upon my <Trigger
State>."A Character may have only one Contingency
effect, from this Skill, at a time. The call when this
ability is triggered is "Contingency: <Delivery
Verbal>".
Counter [Fighting]*
Requirements: Parry 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate and cause any
attack, delivered by Weapon or Packet, causing
Weapon Damage or effect delivered as <Effect>-
Strike or Physical by which they are struck causing it
to rebound against its source. This ability is also
usable against normal Weapon Damage. In order to
use this Skill the Character must have a Weapon or
Shield in hand. This ability cannot be used if the
Character is under an effect which prevents the
Character from using Weapons or if the Item is
Fumbled. Effects delivered as Arcane-Strike cannot
be avoided by this, or any other, ability. The call for
this ability is "Reflect".
Create Cauldron [Crafting]*
Requirements: Create Flask Create Potion 20
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may create a Cauldron which
contains a single Technique, with a Range of Touch
or Packet, which is an Alchemy or from one of the
following Schools of Magic; Protection, Restoration,
Transmutation. The caster must be able to cast the
stored Technique . The creator may set up the
Cauldron as an immovable object from which anyone
may spend a ten(10)-count to role-play drawing from,
and imbibing a portion of the Cauldron gaining the
benefits of the contained Technique. Any number of
Characters may benefit from the Cauldron but a
Character may benefit from a single Cauldron no
more than once per Reset period. A Cauldron ends if
the Cauldron is Destroyed or its creator sets up a
second Cauldron.
A Cauldron is a container which can hold a
minimum of two(2) litres of liquid.
In order to use this Skill the Character must have
access to a Potion Laboratory. All Cauldrons expire at
the end of the Event.
Create Flask [Crafting]*
Requirements: Create Potion 10
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may expend a single Spell or
Alchemy Slot to create a Flask containing a
Technique, the Character can cast, with a Range of
Touch or Packet, of the same level as the expended
Technique Slot. The Technique must have a Range of
Touch or Packet. A Flask has five(5) Charges upon
creation. The Character may then expending one(1)
Charge from the Flask in order to deliver the chosen
Technique against a target by having the target spend
a three(3)-count role-playing drinking from the Flask.
A Flask must be marked with the Technique it
contains, as well as the number of charges it
contains, and must be a container which appears to
hold one-hundred(100) to one-thousand(1000)
millilitres of liquid.
In order to use this Skill the Character must have
access to a Potion Laboratory. All Flasks expire at the
end of the Event.
Create Potion [Crafting]*
Requirements: Level 1 Spell or Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may expend a single Spell or
Alchemy Slot to create a Potion containing a
Technique, the Character can cast, with a Range of
Touch or Packet, of the same level as the expended
Technique Slot. Once a Technique has been put into
a Potion, any Character may imbibe the Potion, a
process taking a three(3)-count, to receive the effects
of the stored Technique. A consumed Potion follows
all rules for a Touch Cast Technique. Each Potion is
usable only once.
When a Technique has multiple effects, such as
Blood Bolt, the Character must choose which of the
effects are contained within the Potion.
A Potion must be marked with the Technique it
contains, as well as the Delivery Verbal, and must be
a container which appears to hold twenty(20) to one-
hundred(100) millilitres of liquid. Though the physical
representation of each Potion may be reused, each
Potion is usable only once.
If the Character has access to a Potion Laboratory
they may instead create two(2) Potions for each
consumed Technique Slot. All Potions expire at the
end of the Event.
Create Scroll [Crafting]*
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may expend a single Spell Slot
to create a Scroll containing a Spell, the Character
can cast, with a Range of Touch or Packet, the same
level as the expended Spell Slot. Once a Spell has
been put into a Scroll, any Character with access to a
School of Magic which includes the inscribed Spell
may cast the stored Spell, consuming the Scroll. To
do so, the user must touch a Packet to the Scroll and
speak the Spell's full Incantation and Delivery Verbal.
Spells contained within Scroll cannot be modified by
Spellshapes unless they were included in the Scroll's
creation. Each Scroll is usable only once.
When a Spell has multiple effects, such as Blood
Bolt, the Character must choose which of the effects
are contained within the Scroll.
A Scroll must be marked with the Spell it contains,
as well as the Incantation and Delivery Verbal, and
must be a piece of paper with an area of at least
40cm
2
. Though the physical representation of each
Scroll may be reused, each Scroll is usable only
once.
If the Character has access to a Scroll Laboratory
they may instead create two(2) Scrolls for each
consumed Spell Slot. All Scrolls expire at the end of
the Event.
Create Wand [Crafting]*
Requirements: Create Scroll 10
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may expend a single Spell Slot
to create a Wand containing a Spell, the Character
can cast, with a Range of Touch or Packet, of the
same level as the expended Spell Slot. Once a Spell
has been put into a Wand, its creator may use the
wand to deliver the stored Spell up to five(5) times.
A Wand must be marked with the Spell it contains,
as well as the number of charges it contains, and
must measure between 10 and 100cm.
In order to use this Skill the Character must have
access to a Scroll Laboratory. All Wands expire at the
end of the Event.
Deadly Strike [Expertise]*
Requirements: Penetrating Strike 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver an attack causing
"100 Physical-Strike <Weapon Type>" damage. In
order to use this Skill the Character must be standing
behind their target and able to see both of their
shoulder blades. This ability is also usable if the
Character expends a charge from any Ambush effect
they have on themselves.
Defensive Roll [Expertise]*
Requirements: Avoidance Armour 5 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, as long as the Character has at least one(1)
Armour Point of Avoidance Armour available, they
may expend all remaining Avoidance Armour Points
to allow them to "Dodge" a single effect delivered by
Weapon or Packet. The call for this ability is "Absorb.
Dodge.". A Character must Rest in between uses of
this Skill.
Diagnose [Background]
Requirements: First Aid
Benefit: This Skill allows the Character to determine
the state of the target. In order to use this Skill, the
Character must touch a willing, or helpless creature
and state "Diagnose" followed by one of the following
questions; "Are you Sleeping?", "Are you
Unconscious?", "Are you Dying?", "Are you Dead?",
"Are you Paralyzed?", "Are you Poisoned?", "Are you
Regenerating?", "How long until you dissipate?",
"How long until you wake-up?", "How long until you
die?", "How long until you become Unparalyzed?",
"How long until you finish Regenerating?". "How
many Hit Points are you missing?". It is not possible
to use this Skill on a target without their knowledge
and an unwilling target can simply say "I Block you." A
Helpless target is unable to stop this Skill from being
used on them.
The Character cannot ask another question until
the first is answered and this Skill takes as long as it
takes for the Character to ask the question and the
target to answer. It cannot, normally, be used against
non-humanoid creatures, or those that have no, or
alien metabolisms.
Die Hard [Heritage, Survivor]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, while the Character is in
their Death Count they may, at any time, grant
themselves a "Physical Life".
Disrupt Magic [Heritage, Wild]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a "Magic Remove
Magic" effect by Packet or Touch.
Dodge [Expertise]*
Requirements: Stealth Training 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate any effect
delivered by Weapon or Packet by which they are
struck. Effects delivered as Arcane(-Strike) cannot be
avoided by this, or any other, ability. The call for this
ability is "Dodge".
Echo Slime [Crafting, Mutation]*
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a copy of the last
Alchemy they cast. This ability must be activated
within ten(10) seconds of the initial Alchemy. The
copy includes all the same options, including any and
all Mutations and other abilities used on the first
Alchemy. The call for this ability is "Echo: <Delivery
Verbal of copied Alchemy>".
Echo Spell [Spellshape, Magic]*
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a copy of the last
Spell they cast. The Spell must be delivered from
memory to use this Skill. This ability must be
activated within ten(10) seconds of the initial Spell.
The copy is delivered without Incantation and
includes all the same options, including any and all
Spellshapes and other abilities used on the first Spell.
The call for this ability is "Echo: <Delivery Verbal of
copied Spell>".
Empower Spell [Spellshape, Magic]*
Requirements: Level 6 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may double the damage or
healing of a single Spell with a Duration of Instant.
Envenom Weapon [Crafting]
Requirements: Level 3 Alchemy Slot, Slimy Coating
5
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
deliver all 1st, 2nd, and 3rd level Alchemy, the Character
casts from memory, as a <Effect>-Strike with their
Weapon instead of a Packet. The Delivery Type used
to deliver the effect is determined by the Delivery
Type of the Alchemy. For example, if the Alchemy
allows the Character to deliver a Poison Sleep effect,
the call for this ability is "Poison-Strike Sleep". If the
effect causes "10 Physical Acid" damage, the
Channelled effect is "10 Physical-Strike Acid". They
do not need a free hand to use Alchemy if they are
wielding a Weapon with which they are Proficient.
They may even Touch Cast Alchemy with their
Weapon by placing the Weapon on the target during
the Delivery Verbal.
Envenom Weapon, Greater [Crafting]
Requirements: Level 6 Alchemy Slot, Envenom
Weapon
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
deliver all 4th, 5th, and 6th level Alchemy, the Character
casts from memory, as a <Effect>-Strike with their
Weapon instead of a Packet. They do not need a free
hand to use Alchemy if they are wielding a Weapon
with which they are Proficient. They may even Touch
Cast Alchemy with their Weapon by placing the
Weapon on the target during the Delivery Verbal.
Envenom Weapon, Superior [Crafting]
Requirements: Level 9 Alchemy Slot, Greater
Envenom Weapon
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
deliver all 7th, 8th, and 9th level Alchemy, the Character
casts from memory, as a <Effect>-Strike with their
Weapon instead of a Packet. They do not need a free
hand to use Alchemy if they are wielding a Weapon
with which they are Proficient. They may even Touch
Cast Alchemy with their Weapon by placing the
Weapon on the target during the Delivery Verbal.
Additionally, the Character may expend a use of
the Slimy Coating Skill in order to allow themselves to
deliver a single copy of any Alchemy they have
delivered as <Effect>-Strike using this ability if the
initial swing failed to strike any target. The call for this
copy is "Echo: <Delivery Verbal>".
Escape Binding [Heritage, Strong]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a "Physical Dispel
Binding" effect on themselves on a three(3)-count.
The call for this ability is "I rip free 1, I rip free 2, I rip
free 3. Physical Dispel Binding."
Evasion [Expertise]*
Requirements: Dodge 3 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate any effect by
which they would be affected. Effects delivered as
Arcane(-Strike) cannot be avoided by this, or any
other, ability. The call for this ability is "Evasion".
Expert Combatant [Expertise]*
Requirements: Level 9 Martial Arts Slot per 2 Ranks
Benefit: The Character gains one(1) additional
Martial Art Slot of each level for every time this Skill is
bought.
Expose Weakness [Expertise]*
Requirements: Stealth Training 1 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought the Character may grant themselves the
effects of an Ambush 5, allowing them to use their
damage bonus from Stealth Training, for their next
five(5) landed hits, even if they aren't behind their
target. The call for this ability is "Physical Ambush 5".
Additionally, if the Character has exhausted all
uses of this Skill for the current Reset period, the
character may Rest in order to recover a single use of
this Skill.
Extra Trap [Expertise]*
Requirements: Any Trap
Benefit: For every Rank a Character has in this Skill,
they may place, and arm, a single additional Trap.
This Skill even allows a Character to have multiple
Traps of the same type in place at once. Normally a
Character is limited to one(1) Trap of each type at a
time. It does not grant any additional uses of any Trap
Skill.
Favoured By Destiny [Background]
Requirements: Any Intervention Skill
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
choose, and use, an additional Intervention Skill each
Event but never more than once per Reset. For more
information on Interventions, See page 193.
Field Repair [Heritage, Skilled]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character may, on a sixty(60)-count,
Repair any number of mundane items without the use
of a Portable Forge or expending any levels of
Smithing. Once this ability is used, the Character
cannot use it again until they return to Town and Rest
to acquire replacement materials and maintain their
tools, a process requiring access to a Forge and
five(5) minutes. See page 66 for more information on
Smithing abilities.
If the Character has the Tinkerer Sub-Skill, they
may use this ability an additional time before needing
to Rest.
Fieldwork [Heritage, Trader]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character does not require a Crafting
Table to use the special abilities from Create Potion,
Create Scroll, and Smithing. The Character still
requires a Crafting Table in order to use the abilities
granted from other Skills such as Augment Potion, or
Create Wand.
Fifth School of Magic [Magic]
Requirements: Fourth School of Magic
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character access to
their fifth School of Magic. The Character is able to
expend their Spell Slots to use the Spells which
belong to any schools they know. See Chapter 7:
Magic for more information on the Schools of Magic.
Fire Bomb [Expertise]*
Requirements: Fire Grenade 3 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a Packet attack
causing "20 Physical Fire" damage to the target. The
Character may throw any number of Packets when
using this Skill but must throw all Packets with the
same hand.
Fire Grenade [Expertise]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a Packet attack
causing "20 Physical Fire" damage to the target.
Alternatively, the Character may expend a single
use of this Skill in order to deliver a Packet attack
which causes a "Physical Fumble <Item>" effect.
Fire Trap [Expertise]*
Requirements: Fire Grenade 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may lay down an Fire Trap,
represented by a small red coloured cloth, or similar
marker, with an area of no more than 1m
2
which can
be of any shape but may not contain voids. Once the
rep has been placed the Character must spend
ten(10) seconds to arm the Trap during which time
they must keep at least one hand on the Trap. Once
activated anyone who steps or touches the Trap
takes "20 Physical Fire" damage.
A Trap can be disarmed by striking it with a Break
or Destroy effect or by its creator spending ten(10)
seconds touching it in order to disarm it. Disarming a
Trap does not trigger its effects. A disarmed Trap is
considered Destroyed.
A Character may have no more than one(1) Trap,
of any type, placed at a time.
First Aid [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: This Skill allows the Character to Stabilise a
Dying target. In order to use this Skill, the Character
must touch the target and state "First Aid". If the
target is not Dying, or immune to First Aid, the target
must immediately state "No Effect". Applying First Aid
is a sixty(60) second Counted Action.
Once First Aid has begun, the target immediately
pauses their Dying Count. it remains paused until
either the First Aid count is finished, or interrupted. In
the latter case the dying creature immediately
resumes their count where it left off.
At the end of the First Aid count, the target is
Stabilised and at zero(0) Hit Points. After one(1)
minute the target regains Consciousness at one(1)
Hit Point.
This Skill is modified by the Physician Background
Skill. For more information on how these Skills
interact, See page 203.
First Martial Arts School [Fighting]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character access to
their first Martial Arts School. The Character is able to
expend their Martial Arts Slots to use the Martial Arts
which belong to this school. See Chapter 7: Magic for
more information on Martial Arts.
First School of Magic [Magic]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character access to
their first School of Magic. The Character is able to
expend their Spell Slots to use the Spells which
belong to this school. See Chapter 7: Magic for more
information on the Schools of Magic.
Flanking [Expertise]*
Requirements: Stealth Training 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may Invoke this ability against
a single target. The Character gains a bonus to all
Weapon Damage on their attacks against the target
equal to their ranks in Stealth Training. This effect
ends at the end of Combat. The call for this ability is
"I Invoke Flanking against you."
Florentine [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield a
Short Weapon, with which they are proficient, in their
off-hand at the same time they wield a Weapon in
their main hand. This Character's Weapon Damage
call with this weapon cannot be increased above the
Weapon's Base Damage.
Focus [Heritage, Willful]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may ignore Disruption for the
casting of a single Spell. Once the Spell is cast, the
Character must state "Focus" if they were subject to
an effect which normally would have Disrupted the
casting. This allows a Character to even use
Defensive Skills such as Resist Curse or Dodge
during the Casting of, or maintain Concentration on, a
Spell after taking Hit Point damage.
This ability does not allow a Character to cast
Spells while Concentrating or Sustaining another
Spell. The Character's Spell Casting can still be
Disrupted if they are subjected to an effect which
causes them to enter a state where they are unable
to cast Spells such as Unconsciousness or Drain.
Fourth School of Magic [Magic]
Requirements: Third School of Magic
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character access to
their fourth School of Magic. The Character is able to
expend their Spell Slots to use the Spells which
belong to any schools they know. See Chapter 7:
Magic for more information on the Schools of Magic.
Freezing Bomb [Expertise]*
Requirements: Freezing Grenade 3 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a Packet attack
causing a "Physical Bind" to affect the target. The
Character may throw any number of Packets when
using this Skill but must throw all Packets with the
same hand.
Freezing Grenade [Expertise]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a Packet attack
causing a "Physical Bind" to affect the target.
Alternatively, the Character may expend a single
use of this Skill in order to deliver a Packet attack
which causes a "Physical Pin" effect.
Freezing Trap [Expertise]*
Requirements: Freezing Grenade 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may lay down a Freezing Trap,
represented by a small blue coloured cloth, or similar
marker, with an area of no more than 1m
2
which can
be of any shape but may not contain voids. Once the
rep has been placed the Character must spend
ten(10) seconds to arm the Trap during which time
they must keep at least one hand on the Trap. Once
activated anyone who steps or touches the Trap is
affected by a "Physical Web" effect.
A Trap can be disarmed by striking it with a Break
or Destroy effect or by its creator spending ten(10)
seconds touching it in order to disarm it. Disarming a
Trap does not trigger its effects. A disarmed Trap is
considered Destroyed.
A Character may have no more than one(1) Trap,
of any type, placed at a time.
Frenzy [Heritage, Wild]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may either Resist a single
Berserk effect by which they are struck, the call for
which is "Resist. Physical Vorpal 5", or choose to
activate this Skill at any time. When this Skill is
activated at the Character's discretion the call is
"Physical Vorpal 5".
Giant Trap [Expertise]*
Requirements: Any Trap
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may lay down a Trap whose
representation has a maximum area of 4m
2
.
Harden Magic [Magic]*
Requirements: Level 5 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may Resist an effect which
would Remove, or Dispel, one or more Magic effects
including Arcane Armour. This ability is usable even if
the Character is inside of an Imprison allowing them
to prevent their Imprison from being removed. It
cannot be used against Nullify or Abolishment effects.
Harvester [Heritage, Trader]
Requirements: None
Benefit: At the start of each Event, the Character
receives one of the following items for each Game
Day of the Event; one(1) Adamantine Ingot, one(1)
Iron Ingot, one(1) Silver Ingot, or a single strength
one(1) Ritual Component. The Ritual Component
Type is random though Event Staff may
accommodate requests for a specific type at their
discretion.
Innate Spellcasting [Magic]
Requirements: Level 3 Spell Slot, Quicken Spell 5
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
deliver all 1st, 2nd, and 3rd level Spells, the Character
casts from memory, without their Incantation.
Additionally, they may cast these Spells while unable
to speak, such as while under the effects of a
Silence, or without a free hand.
Innate Spellcasting, Greater [Magic]
Requirements: Level 6 Spell Slot, Innate
Spellcasting
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
deliver all 4th, 5th, and 6th level Spells, the Character
casts from memory, without their Incantation.
Additionally, they may cast these Spells while unable
to speak, such as while under the effects of a
Silence, or without a free hand.
Innate Spellcasting, Superior [Magic]
Requirements: Level 9 Spell Slot, Greater Innate
Spellcasting
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
deliver all 7th, 8th, and 9th level Spells, the Character
casts from memory, without their Incantation.
Additionally, they may cast these Spells while unable
to speak, such as while under the effects of a
Silence, or without a free hand.
Additionally, the Character may expend a use of
the Quicken Spell Skill in order to instantly regain the
ability to cast a single Spell, delivered from memory,
which they would have been able to recover by
Meditation.
Invoker [Background]
Requirements: Level 5 Alchemy Slot, Level 5 Spell
Slot, or Smithing 5.
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may perform a
single Invocation of any type for which the meet this
Skill's Requirements. Level 5 Alchemy Slots allow a
single Alchemy Invocation. Level 5 Spell Slots allow a
Magic Invocation. Five(5) Ranks in the Skill Smithing
allows a Smithing Invocation.
A Character who meets multiple requirements does
not gain the ability to perform multiple Invocations
with this Skill but expands their options for the one(1)
they may perform. This Skill does not count towards
the Requirements of any other Skills or abilities.
Last Words [Heritage, Spiritual]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Whenever the Character enters their Death
Count, they may say up to ten(10) words at a normal
speaking volume.
If the Character has Rank 4 of the Seer Skill, they
may use this ability while Manifested.
If the Character has Rank 10 of the Seer Skill, they
may once per Reset, touch a target and deliver a
"Lesser Arcane Imbue Skill: Last Words" allowing the
touched target to use this Skill for ten(10) seconds.
Legendary Invention [Crafting, Intervention]*
Requirements: 20 Character Levels per Rank.
Benefit: This Skill allows the Character to reveal an
invention of legendary ability beyond the limits of
what should be possible. A Character can have only
one Intervention Skill for every twenty(20) Character
Levels they possess. Each Intervention Skill allows
the Character to perform one(1) Intervention per
Event. These uses can never be recovered. For more
information on Interventions, see page 193.
Lifebind [Heritage, Spiritual]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may deliver
a "Magic Tarry" effect by Touch. Alternatively, they
may activate this ability on themselves at any time
during their Death Count.
If the Character has Rank 4 of the Seer Skill, this
Skill can be expended to increase the time they may
be Manifested by five(5) minutes.
If the Character has Rank 10 of the Seer Skill, they
may once per Reset, touch a target and deliver a
"Lesser Arcane Imbue Skill: Last Words" allowing the
touched target to use this Skill for ten(10) seconds.
Long Axe [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield a
Long Axe in combat. For information of the
requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Long Mace [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield a
Long Mace in combat. For information of the
requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Long Mastery [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield all
Long Weapons in combat. At the time this Skill is
bought, the Character may "sell back" any Skills
which are included in its benefit. For information of
the requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Long Sword [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield a
Long Sword in combat. For information of the
requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Mace Mastery [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield all
Maces in combat. When wielding a Two-Handed
Weapon, the Character may block attacks while
holding the Weapon with one hand but cannot attack
or use any Skill until they have both hands on the
Two-Handed Weapon. At the time this Skill is bought,
the Character may "sell back" any Skills which are
included in its benefit. For information of the
requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Magical Aptitude [Magic]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character is able to learn how to cast
Spells. Additionally the Character may perform any of
the following actions on a sixty(60)-count; 1) Detect
the presence of, but no other information about the,
magic on an item they are touching, 2) Identify the
Spells contained in any number of Scrolls, or 3)
Identify the Spells contained in any number of
Potions.
If the Character has both this Skill and the Skill
Sense Magic they may identify what School(s) of
Magic are present on the item when using the Detect
portion of this Skill.
Magical Intervention [Magic, Intervention]*
Requirements: 20 Character Levels per Rank.
Benefit: This Skill allows the Character to perform an
incredible act of magical prowess beyond the limits of
what should be possible. A Character can have only
one Intervention Skill for every twenty(20) Character
Levels they possess. Each Intervention Skill allows
the Character to perform one(1) Intervention per
Event. These uses can never be recovered. For more
information on Interventions, see page 193.
Magical Tradition [Magic]
Requirements: Magical Aptitude
Benefit: The Character is able to learn, and gain the
effects of, a single Magical Tradition. See Page 101
for more information on Magical Traditions.
Martial Arts Slot [Fighting]*
Requirements: Weapon Training or Stealth Training,
See Text
Benefit: This Character gains one Martial Art Slot of
the appropriate level as detailed under Pyramid Skills
on Page 33.
Martial Intervention [Fighting, Intervention]*
Requirements: 20 Character Levels per Rank.
Benefit: This Skill allows the Character to perform an
incredible act of martial prowess beyond the limits of
what should be possible. A Character can have only
one Intervention Skill for every twenty(20) Character
Levels they possess. Each Intervention Skill allows
the Character to perform one(1) Intervention per
Event. These uses can never be recovered. For more
information on Interventions, see page 193.
Martial Kata [Fighting]*
Requirements: Level 9 Martial Art Slot
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
perform a Martial Arts Kata granting them, or their
equipment a special benefit. For more information on
what Kata are available, see Chapter 8: Rituals.
Martial Secret [Fighting]*
Requirements: Martial Kata
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character to perform,
without the need of a Martial Treatise, a single Martial
Kata of their choice. This Skill cannot be used to
learn any Martial Kata which is marked as Limited in
its description.
Martial Tradition [Fighting]
Requirements: Any Weapon Proficiency
Benefit: The Character is able to learn, and gain the
effects of, a single Martial Tradition. See Page 136 for
more information on Martial
Traditions.
Master Alchemist [Crafting]*
Requirements: Alkahestry
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character one of the
following two options;
Practised Alchemist: The Character counts as an
additional Caster for the purposes of all Ritual
Alchemy. Additionally, the number of Invocations the
Character may cast each Reset is increased by
one(1). Each additional time this option is taken the
Character acts as an additional Caster and can cast
an additional Invocation each Reset.
Eschew Components: The Character chooses a
single Component type, other than Power. The
Character reduces the total number of Components
needed to cast the Ritual by one(1) to a minimum of
one(1). Each additional time this option is taken the
Character must choose a new Component type. If A
Character has already taken this Skill for every
component type they can no longer take this branch
of the Skill.
Master Ritualist [Magic]*
Requirements: Ritual Magic
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character one(1) of the
following two(2) options;
Practised Ritualist: The Character counts as an
additional Caster for the purposes of all Ritual Magic.
Additionally, the number of Invocations the Character
may cast each Reset is increased by one(1). Each
additional time this option is taken the Character acts
as an additional Caster and can cast an additional
Invocation each Reset.
Eschew Components: The Character chooses a
single Component type, other than Power. The
Character reduces the total number of Components
needed to cast the Ritual by one(1) to a minimum of
one(1). Each additional time this option is taken the
Character must choose a new Component type. If A
Character has already taken this Skill for every
component type they can no longer take this branch
of the Skill.
Master's Secret [Crafting]*
Requirements: Grand Master Smithing
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character to perform,
without the need of a Schematic, a single Ritual
Smithing of their choice. This Skill cannot be used to
learn any Ritual Smithing which is marked as
"Limited" in its description.
Memorise Recipe [Alchemy]*
Requirements: Alkahestry
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character to perform,
without the need of a Recipe Scroll, a single Ritual
Alchemy of their choice. This Skill cannot be used to
learn any Ritual Alchemy which is marked as
"Limited" in its description.
Memorise Ritual [Magic]*
Requirements: Ritual Magic
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character to perform,
without the need of a Ritual Scroll, a single Ritual
Magic of their choice. This Skill cannot be used to
learn any Ritual Magic which is marked as Limited in
its description.
Mercantile Connections [Heritage, Trader]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains five(5) entries to the
Merchant Draw each Event. For more information on
the Merchant Draw, See Page 210.
Merchant [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains one(1) Rank in the
Merchant Skill as well as a single Merchant Sub-Skill
for which they meet the Requirements. Additionally,
for each Rank the Character has in this Skill they gain
a single entry for the Merchant Draw. See Page 210
for more information.
Mind Over Body [Heritage, Willful]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character remains
Conscious while Dying. This Skill does not grant the
Character the ability to use any Skills while in this
state, but allows the Character to move at a normal
walking rate ignoring all further damage. This effect
ends when the Character is healed to one(1) or more
Hit Points or when they enter their Death Count.
This effect does not grant Immunity to any effects
other than Weapon Damage.
Mix Poison [Crafting, Mutation]*
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may mix an Alchemy, with a
Range other than Self, they deliver from Memory, into
food or drink. This process requires a three(3)-count
and requires the Character to role-play mixing the
poison into the food and mark the poisoned food's
container with the name of the Alchemy it contains.
It is possible to add multiple Alchemical effects to a
single target but requires the count to be repeated for
each additional effect. All effects expire at the end of
the current Reset period.
The first Character who consumes any amount of
the poisoned food is affected by all effects bypassing
Automatic Defenses.
Natural Cunning [Heritage, Stealthy]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains Cunning 2, increasing
their Weapon Damage, when standing behind their
target and able to see both shoulder blades, by
two(2). This Skill does not count as a prerequisite for
any other Skill. The Cunning from this Skill stacks
with that granted by all effects including Spells.
Natural Dodge [Heritage, Fast]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to, once per
Reset, negate a single effect, delivered by Weapon or
Packet, by which they are struck, as per the Skill
Dodge. This Skill does not count as a prerequisite for
any other Skill. The Call for this ability is "Dodge".
Natural Fortification [Heritage, Survivor]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may grant themselves a
"Physical Fortify 5" effect. Once per Reset, the
Character may Rest in order to restore all uses of this
Skill.
Natural Magic [Heritage, Magical]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to, once per
Reset, cast a single Spell below 4th level of any
School from Memory. This Skill does not count as a
prerequisite for any other Skill.
Natural Parry [Heritage, Martial]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may negate
any attack delivered, by weapon or packet, as
<Effect>-Strike or Physical by which they are struck.
The call for this ability is "Parry".
If the Character has the Bodyguard Skill they can
use this ability to negate an applicable attack which
strikes another target within their Weapon's reach.
This Skill does not count as a prerequisite for any
other Skill.
Natural Remedy [Heritage, Wild]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill may spend
sixty(60) seconds examining the target, role playing
this examination involves checking their eyes, pallor,
and other physical features while gathering
ingredients from the surrounding area or their own
person. At the end of the minute the Character states
"Natural Remedy. Poison Dispel Poison.". This ability
is usable at will.
The Character is able to use this Skill on
themselves, and is even able to do so while they are
under an effect which prevents them from using
Skills.
Natural Ritualist [Heritage, Magical]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character may participate in the casting
of Ritual Magic as a Secondary Caster. This does not
grant the Character the ability to cast any Rituals,
only add one(1) to the count of total casters. If the
Character also has the Skill Ritual Magic, the number
of Casters the Character counts as is increased by
one(1). This Skill does not increase the number of
Invocations a Character can cast per Reset.
Natural Strength [Heritage, Strong]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character increases all of their Weapon
Damage by one(1). This Skill does not count as a
prerequisite for any other Skill. The effects of this Skill
stacks with all other Skills and effects including
Spells.
Natural Toughness [Heritage, Tough]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains five(5) additional Hit
Points.
Open Lock [Expertise]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character may, if they have a set of
lockpicks, attempt to open any in-game lock. If no
lock is present, the Character needs to role-play the
process as a sixty(60)-count. This Skill can also be
used to disarm, and Destroy a Trap. The Character
needs to role-play the process as a sixty(60)-count.
If the Character has the Locksmith Sub-Skill of the
Merchant Background Skill, they reduce the time
needed to open a lock to ten(10) seconds. If the lock
has a limited number of tries, they gain one(1)
additional attempt.
If the Character has the Spy Background Skill, they
reduce the time needed to disarm a trap to ten(10)
seconds. If the Trap has a limited number of tries,
they gain one(1) additional attempt.
Parry [Fighting]*
Requirements: Weapon Training 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate any attack
delivered, by weapon or packet, as <Effect>-Strike or
Physical by which they are struck. In order to use this
Skill the Character must have a Weapon or Shield in
hand. This ability cannot be used if the Character is
under an effect which prevents the Character from
using Weapons or if the Item is Fumbled. Effects
delivered as Arcane-Strike cannot be avoided by this,
or any other, ability. The call for this ability is "Parry".
If the Character has the Bodyguard Skill they can
use this ability to negate an applicable attack which
strikes the target of their Bodyguard Skill when they
are within their Weapon's reach.
Penance [Fighting]*
Requirements: Level 7 Martial Art Slot or Weapon
Training 5
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, as long as the Character has at least one(1)
Armour Point of Physical Armour available they may
expend all remaining Physical Armour Points to grant
themselves a "Physical Dispel Curse" effect. The call
for this ability is "Absorb. Physical Dispel Curse". This
ability may be used event if the Character is under an
effect which prevents them from using Skills such as
Drain or Paralysis.
Penetrating Strike [Expertise]*
Requirements: Precise Strike 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver an attack causing
"50 Physical-Strike <Weapon Type>" damage. In
order to use this Skill the Character must be standing
behind their target and able to see both of their
shoulder blades. This ability is also usable if the
Character expends a charge from any Ambush effect
they have on themselves.
Physician [Background]*
Requirements: Diagnose and First Aid
Benefit: The Character gains an additional rank in
the Physician Skill See Page 203 for information on
this Skill.
Piercing Slime [Crafting, Mutation]*
Requirements: Level 7 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a single Alchemy,
with a Range of Packet, as Piercing. The call for this
ability is "<#> Piercing <Delivery Type> <Effect>
<Option>". For example, a Sleep Alchemy would use
the Delivery Verbal "Piercing Poison Sleep" while an
Alchemist's Flask would use the Delivery Verbal "10
Piercing Poison <Type>"
Piercing Spell [Spellshape, Magic]*
Requirements: Level 7 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a single Spell, with
a Range of Packet, as Piercing. The call for this
ability is "<Incantation> <#> Piercing <Delivery Type>
<Effect> <Option>". For example, a Sleep Spell
would use the Delivery Verbal "Piercing Magic Sleep"
while an Elemental Bolt would use the Delivery
Verbal "10 Piercing Elemental <Type>".
Precise Strike [Expertise]*
Requirements: Stealth Training 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver an attack causing
"20 Physical-Strike <Weapon Type>" damage. In
order to use this Skill the Character must be standing
behind their target and able to see both of their
shoulder blades. This ability is also usable if the
Character expends a charge from any Ambush effect
they have on themselves.
Psychic Awaken [Heritage, Psychic]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver, by Touch or
Packet, a "Magic Dispel Compulsion". This ability
may also be used on themselves on a ten(10)-count
even if they were in a state where they normally
wouldn't be able to cast Spells.
Psychic Charm [Heritage, Psychic]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver, by Touch or
Packet, a "Magic Charm" effect.
Quicken Spell [Spellshape, Magic]*
Requirements: Level 3 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a single Spell
without its Incantation. Additionally, they may cast this
Spell while unable to speak, such as while under the
effects of a Silence, or without a free hand.
Quickness [Heritage, Fast]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill gains ten(10)
points of Avoidance Armour. A Character is still limited
by their Wear Total which limits the maximum number
of Armour Points of any type from which the
Character may benefit.
Avoidance Armour is represented by the Character
wearing a number of feathers equal to the total
Armour Point value of the armour divided by ten(10).
These feathers must be attached to a single
accessory which is worn by, and visible upon, the
Character.
Rapid Refit: Avoidance [Heritage, Fast]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may, on a three(3)-Count,
deliver a Physical Refit Avoidance Armour, to
themselves.
Read and Write [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to read and
write in all languages they know. Without this Skill the
Character is unable to read or recognize even the
simplest of symbols.
Please note that this does not mean a Character
without this Skill is stupid or unable to perform maths,
merely that they are unable to understand any form of
written media.
Read and Write is not Considered a Skill for any
effect or action which limits the use of Skills. Meaning
it is possible to read while Meditating, Resting or
even while casting Ritual Magic.
Resist Break [Heritage, Skilled]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single Break
effect by which they are struck. The call for this ability
is "Resist".
Resist Compulsion [Heritage, Willful]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single
Compulsion effect by which they are struck. The call
for this ability is "Resist".
Resist Curse [Heritage, Spiritual]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single Curse
effect by which they are struck. The call for this ability
is "Resist".
Resist Fumble [Heritage, Martial]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single Fumble
effect by which they are struck. The call for this ability
is "Resist".
Resist Magic [Heritage, Magical]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset the Character may negate a
single effect delivered as Magic(-Strike) by which
they are struck. The call for this ability is "Resist".
If the Character has the Skill Absorb Magic, they
also benefit from a "Magic Refit Arcane Armour". The
call for this ability, in this latter case, is "Resist. Magic
Refit Arcane Armour".
Resist Physical [Heritage, Tough]
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset the Character may negate a
single effect delivered as Physical(-Strike) by which
they are struck. The call for this ability is "Resist".
Resist Poison [Heritage, Tough]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may negate a single effect
delivered as Poison(-Strike) by which they are struck.
The call for this ability is "Resist".
Ritual Magic [Magic]
Requirements: Level 9 Spell Slot
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
cast Ritual Magic. Upon gaining this Skill the
Character can act as the Primary Caster, or as a
Secondary Caster, for any Ritual Magic. Additionally,
the Character gains the ability to cast Invocations.
See Chapter 8: Rituals for more information on
casting Ritual Magic and Invocations.
The Character can also use this Skill to Identify
any number of Ritual Components on a sixty(60)-
count.
Rune Carving [Crafting]*
Requirements: Create Wand, Create Scroll 20
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may inscribe a Rune, attuned
to a single Spell they can cast, upon a willing target
or their immediate possessions. The target may
charge the Rune by Absorbing a Touch Cast of the
attuned Spell. Once the Rune has been Charged in
this way, the Character on whom the Rune was
drawn may cast the Spell using the following verbal:
"Activate: <Delivery Verbal>".
A Rune must be a conspicuous mark between 2 to
5cm in one dimension and 4 to 10cm in the second.
Runes last until the end of the Event.
Sailor [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains immunity to many
environmental effects while fighting on, or beneath
the sea. The specific effects are up to the Marshal
who must be informed of this Skill at the start of the
Module.
Additionally, once per Reset the Character may,
when drinking any beverage, grant themselves either
of the following effects; 20 Poison Healing, or Poison
Remove <Delivery Type> of any of the following
Delivery Types; Elemental, Magic, Physical, or
Poison. Once per Reset the Character may Rest to
recover the use of this ability.
Savage Strike [Fighting]*
Requirements: Vital Strike 2 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver an attack causing
"50 Physical-Strike <Weapon Type>" damage.
Second Martial Arts School [Fighting]
Requirements: First Martial Arts School
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character access to
their second Martial Arts School. The Character is
able to expend their Martial Arts Slots to use the
Martial Arts which belong to this school, and any
other School the Character knows. See Chapter 7:
Magic for more information on Martial Arts.
Second School of Magic [Magic]
Requirements: First School of Magic
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character access to
their second School of Magic. The Character is able
to expend their Spell Slots to use the Spells which
belong to any schools they know. See Chapter 7:
Magic for more information on the Schools of Magic.
Secret Technique [Crafting]*
Requirements: Grand Master Smithing
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character once of the
following two options;
Practised Smith: The Character counts as an
additional Caster for the purposes of all Ritual
Smithing. Additionally, the number of Invocations the
Character may cast each Reset is increased by
one(1). Each additional time this option is taken the
Character acts as an additional Caster and can cast
an additional Invocation each Reset.
Eschew Components: The Character chooses a
single Component type, other than Power. The
Character reduces the total number of Components
needed to cast the Ritual by one(1) to a minimum of
one(1). Each additional time this option is taken the
Character must choose a new Component type. If A
Character has already taken this Skill for every
component type they can no longer take this branch
of the Skill.
Seer [Background]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains an additional rank in
the Seer Skill See Page 206 for information on this
Skill.
Sense Magic [Heritage, Skilled]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to spend a
sixty(60)-count to Detect the presence of magic on
any item they are touching.
If the Character has both this Skill and the Skill
Magical Aptitude they may identify what School(s) of
Magic are present on the item.
Sense Poison [Heritage, Survivor]
Requirements: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to spend a
sixty(60)-count to Detect the presence of Poison or
Alchemy on, or within, any item they are touching.
If the Character has both this Skill and either the
Skill Apothecary or Chemist they may Identify what
effect is present.
Shadow Intervention [Expertise,
Intervention]*
Requirements: 20 Character Levels per Rank.
Benefit: This Skill allows the Character to perform an
incredible act of stealth prowess beyond the limits of
what should be possible. A Character can have only
one Intervention Skill for every twenty(20) Character
Levels they possess. Each Intervention Skill allows
the Character to perform one(1) Intervention per
Event. These uses can never be recovered. For more
information on Interventions, see page 193.
Shadow Shot [Expertise]*
Requirements: Weapon Proficiency (Archery or
Thrown)
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may use a Thrown Weapon or
Archery Packet to deliver any weapon delivered
attack, such as a Martial Art, including those which
normally require the Character to be behind their
target, regardless of their position relative to their
target. Additionally, when a Thrown Weapon is used,
the Character may throw a second Weapon at the
same time.
Shield [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield all
Shields and the ability to use a single shield in
combat. There is no way for a Character to wield
more than one(1) Shield at once. For information of
the requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Short Axe [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield a
Short Axe in combat. For information of the
requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Short Mace [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield a
Short Mace in combat. For information of the
requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Short Mastery [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield all
Short Weapons in combat. At the time this Skill is
bought, the Character may "sell back" any Skills
which are included in its benefit. For information of
the requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Short Sword [Weapon Proficiency]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to wield a
Short Sword in combat. For information of the
requirements for the Physical Representations for
this, and other Weapons, see Chapter 5: Equipment.
Shrapnel Bomb [Expertise]*
Requirements: Shrapnel Grenade 3 per Rank
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a Packet attack
causing "50 Physical Normal" damage to the target.
The Character may throw any number of Packets
when using this Skill but must throw all Packets with
the same hand.
Shrapnel Grenade [Expertise]*
Requirements: See Text
Benefit: Once per Reset, for every time this Skill is
bought, the Character may deliver a Packet attack
causing "50 Physical Normal" damage to the target.
Alternatively, the Character may expend a single use
of this Skill in order to deliver a Packet attack which
causes a "20 Physical Silver" damage or "20 Physical
Iron" damage to the target.
A Character may also expend a single use of this
