to use some Skills, a Character must be standing behind
their target and able to see both shoulder blades. This
ability allows a Character to ignore this requirement a
number of times equal to the Character's Charges of
Ambush. This ability also allows a Character to use the
damage bonus from their Stealth Training regardless of
their position around their target. Each time the
Character successfully lands any valid hit on a target, or
uses any other weapon delivered effect, one(1) charge
is consumed. At the end of Combat, all remaining
Charges are lost.
<Weapon Type> Aura – This effect alters the
Weapon Type of the Character's weapon either
against a single target, against whom this effect is
invoked, or against all opponents. In either case, this
effect normally ends at the end of Combat.
Berserk – This effect forces the Character into an
insane killing frenzy forcing them to attack everyone
around them to the best of their Player's ability using the
most effective means at their disposal.
Bind – This Binding effect forces the target's arms to
their sides and keeps them there throughout its duration.
The creature cannot use any Skill which requires the
use of their arms such as using weapons or casting
spells.
Blind- This Curse effect prevents the target from using
any ranged attacks including thrown weapon and packet
attacks.
Block – This ability allows the Character to negate a
single attack causing Weapon Damage by which they
are struck.
<#> <Deliver Type> Body – This effect causes damage
directly to the target's Hit Points bypassing any Armour
Points the Character may have. If the Character has any
Fortification Points the damage is subtracted from these
before it causes Hit Points damage.
Breach <Type> Armour – This effect instantly
reduces any Armour Points the Character has from
the chosen Armour Type to zero(0). It does not
prevent the armour from being Refit normally.
Break <Item> – This effect Breaks the target item. A
Broken item cannot be used until it is Repaired. Certain
creatures, such as Constructs, may be damaged by this
effect. If the target has multiple items in their possession
which match the description of the item designated by
the user it is the target chooses which of their items are
affected. The target cannot choose an item which is
Indestructible unless it is the only item in their
possession which matches the description.
Calm – This Compulsion effect causes the target to
enter an extremely calm and tranquil state. The target
cannot attack themselves or any other creature
though they will not ignore danger. This effect ends if
the target is attacked.
Charm – This Compulsion effect causes the target to
view the caster as a trusted friend listening to their
suggestions and not attacking them. This does not grant
the caster any control over the target. This effect ends if
the caster attacks the target.
Clarity – This effect instantly grants the target the
benefit of having Meditated for one(1) minute.
Claws/Natural Weapons – This refers to a special type
of Conjured Weapon. These are special Weapons
which, unless explicitly stated otherwise, have a Base
Damage of one(1) and use Weapon Type: Normal. All
such items must use Physical Representations which
are marked with a thick red band on the handle, or are,
at least, 50% red in colour. Claws are always Immune to
Break and Destroy <Item>. These weapons are valid
targets for Ritual Effects which target Weapons, as well
as Ritual Effects which target Claws. If the Claws are
subjected to the Ritual Effect Transmuting Weapon, they
may also be the target of Ritual Effects which target
Weapons made of Special Materials. Claws follow
construction rules for swords.
Cleanse – This effect removes all harmful effects from
the target that would be removed by their Death. It is the
decision of the target's Player as to which effects are
harmful.
Confine – This Binding effect forces the target's arms to
their sides and immobilises the target preventing them
from moving or using any Skills while in effect.
Additionally, the target cannot be moved. The target may
still speak.
Conjure <Item> – This effect creates an item which is
usable only by the character with this ability. The item
cannot be taken from its possessor, nor can it be
handed off to another. If the item is a Thrown Weapon,
the item can still be thrown but cannot be used again
until the item is either retrieved, or the Character has
multiple, identical, Physical Representations for the
item. The item must be marked with a thick red band on
the handle and must be mostly, at least 50%, red in
colour.
Control <Type> – This effect allows the caster to control
the actions of a mindless creature. The target will obey
all instructions from the caster, even self destructive
ones. Some powerful creatures can control intelligent
creatures but not Player Characters.
Cowardice – This Compulsion effect prevents the target
from using any non-ranged weapon attacks including
effects which are delivered as <Effect>-Strike.
Cripple – This Curse effect affects a limb of the target. A
Crippled arm cannot be used for any Skills and cannot
hold any items. A Crippled leg cannot be used to walk
and prevents the target from moving faster than a slow,
limping walking pace. The target cannot hop. If both legs
are Crippled the target can only kneel in place. Unlike
most effects, this one stacks with each new Cripple
effect affecting a new limb until the Character has no
remaining limbs.
Cunning <#> – This ability grants the Character "#"
additional points of Weapon Damage when they are
standing behind their target and able to see both of
their shoulder blades. This effect stacks with all
similar effects which increase weapon damage but
multiple effects which grant Cunning do not stack
unless explicitly states otherwise.
When calculating a Character's Cunning, first
determine the points of non-stacking Cunning then
every point of stacking Cunning. For example a Zoan
Character with the Hidden Numbers Pact Tactic has
Cunning 2 from the ability. But when under the effects
of the Greater Predation Spell they gain Cunning 4.
Their total from non-stacking effects is four(4). This
would stack with the Skill Natural Cunning for a total
value of Cunning 6.
Death – This effect instantly causes a living creature
to enter its Death Count.
Death Throes: <Effect> – Upon entering their Death
Count or Dying Count, this creature delivers the
specified effect. This effect must be delivered within
ten(10) seconds of entering the triggered state.
Destroy <Item> – This effect instantly Destroys a
single item specified by the effect rendering it
unusable. Certain creatures, such as Constructs, may
be damaged by this Effect, An item, or effect, which
has been Destroyed cannot be Repaired. A tagged
item which has been Destroyed is lost and the tag
must be turned in at the end of the module, or to
logistics at the player's earliest convenience. At the
discretion of the Event Logistics Marshals, destroyed
tags may have other policies regarding their disposal.
Dispel <Effect> – This effect instantly removes all
effects which match the description specified by the
effect. For example, Dispel Binding would remove all
Binding effects from the target regardless of their
Delivery Method. While Dispel Magic would remove
all effects which were delivered as Magic.
Dominate – This Compulsion effect places the target
under the complete control of the caster. They will
follow all orders, even self destructive ones. Please
note that this spell does not force a player to engage
in any activities they are not comfortable with
performing.
Drain – This Curse effect prevents the target from
running or using any Skills until the effect ends or is
removed.
Elemental Aura – This effect appends the chosen
Damage Type to the Character's Weapon Damage
call. This effect is either against a single target,
against whom this effect is invoked, or against all
opponents. In either case, this effect normally ends at
the end of Combat.
Enhance <Type> Armour <#> – This effect improves
the designated armour type by the amount
designated. The duration of this effect varies based
on the source but generally lasts until the end of the
Day.
Enthral – This powerful effect places the target under
the complete control of the caster. They will follow all
orders, even self destructive ones. This effect is not
removed by the Character's Death. Only a Cleanse
effect or the Character's Resurrection can remove it.
Once a Character has been affected by this effect
they are unable to move, take any action, or even
speak, until one of the following occurs; five(5)
minutes go by without receiving an order from the
source of the Enthral effect, or they are given an
order. Once they have been given an order they are
unable to use any effect on themselves which would
remove the Enthral effect such as casting Cleanse on
themselves unless ordered to do so. Please note
that this spell does not force a player to engage in
any activities they are not comfortable with
performing.
Fabricate – This effect creates a single mundane
item for which the caster has a valid representation.
False Vitality – This effect appears to return a Dead
Character to life at one(1) Hit Point. After five(5)
minutes, the Character will immediately die and
restart their Death count.
Fear – This Compulsion effect causes the target to
immediately flee the source‘s presence attempting to
break line of sight with them. The target cannot use
any Skills while under this effect. Once the target has
broken line of sight with the source for ten(10)
seconds they may again use their Skills but this effect
remains active unless it ends or is removed. Until it
does, if the target returns to the source's line of sight
they again lose access to all Skills and must flee in
an attempt to once again break line of sight.
Fetter – This effect prevents the target from running,
jumping, or moving faster than a normal walking
pace.
Fortify <#> – This effect grants the target a number
of Fortification Points equal to <#>. Effects which
grant Fortification Points do not stack, add together,
unless explicitly stated otherwise in their description.
Frailty – This Curse effect causes the target to take
double damage from all effects. Additionally, the
Character cannot use any form of the Defensive
Ability Resist.
Fumble <Weapon> – This Curse effect prevents the
target from using the named weapon for any Skills.
The weapon cannot be used to attack or even block
weapon attacks. This effect lasts ten(10) seconds.
Healing – This effect restores lost Hit Points. A target
cannot be healed above their maximum Hit Point
total. Creatures which lack a physical form are
usually not affected by healing and a small number of
creatures may even be damaged by this effect.
Imbue Skill <Skill Name> – This effect grants the
target the Skill or ability specified by the effect. For
example, there are a number of spells which grant
the target armour. Effects which grant the same Skill
or ability do not stack. For example a Character who
is granted "Natural Armour 5" and "Natural Armour
10" has ten(10) points of Natural Armour, not
fifteen(15). If the Skill granted by the effect is not
passive, the Character must state Activate prior to the
Delivery Verbal or Activation Call. Unless stated
otherwise, this ability ends at the end of the Day.
Imbue Spell <Spell Name> – This effect grants the
target the ability to cast the designated spell using its
full Incantation and Delivery Verbal. If this effect is
granted by an item, the Character must state Activate
prior to the Incantation. This effect normally ends at
the end of the Day.
<Effect> Immunity – This effect grants Immunity to
the specified effect. If the effect is a Deliver Type, the
target is Immune to all effects delivered as that
Delivery Type. If the effect is a specific effect such as
Fumble, the target is Immune to all Fumble effects
regardless of Delivery Type. The one exception to this
is if the effect is delivered as Arcane(-Strike). In this
case the target's Immunity does not apply.
Imprison – This effect encases the target in a
magical field preventing them from speaking, moving,
being moved, or using any Skills. While inside the
Imprison, the target is immune to all effects except
Dispel <Delivery Type of Imprison> which removes
this effect. The caster may also end the Imprison
effect by simply touching the target and stating
"Imprison down". If the caster is also the target they
may expend one use of the Skill Harden Magic to
negate a single Dispel Magic effect from removing
the Imprison.
The caster can also use the Imprison to deliver a Killing
Blow, which counts as Magic, upon the target. To do so the
caster must touch the target and state "Killing Blow 1,
Killing Blow 2, Killing Blow 3, Magic"
Indestructible – All Items made of Adamantine or
containing one or more Ritual effects are
Indestructible. An Indestructible item is Immune to the
effects of Break and Destroy <Item>. An Item which is
Indestructible can never be chosen as the target item
for a Break or Destroy <Item> effect unless it is the
only Item in the Character's possession which
matches the description specified in the effect's
Delivery Verbal. An Arcane Break will cause the Item
to become unusable until it is Repaired. An Arcane
Destroy <Item> will cause the target to be Destroyed
normally.
Inspiration – This effect allows the Character to
regain a single use of a single Skill which has a
limited number of uses per Reset such as a Spell
Slot. Unless explicitly informed otherwise, a
Character can benefit from this effect no more than
once per Reset.
Lantern – This effect creates a light source which
allows the use of a diffused flash light, lantern, glow
stick or similar light source. A light source may be
disqualified from use if it is too bright. The decision as
to whether or not a light can be used is up to the
Marshal.
Life – This effect restores a Dead creature to Life at
one(1) Hit Point. This effect, usually, only affects
living creatures.
Martial Conversion – This ability allows a Character
to expend a Martial Arts Slot of any level in order to
regain a Martial Arts Slot of any level below that of
the expended Martial Arts Slot.
Mirage <#> – This effect grants the target a Dodge
against the next effect, delivered by Weapon or Packet,
by which it is struck. Unlike the Dodge Skill, the target
does not choose when this ability activates, it is
automatically triggered as soon as the target is struck by
an applicable effect.
Nullify – This effect removes all effects from the target
which would be removed by the target's Death.
Paralysis – This Curse effect completely immobilises
the target preventing them from speaking, moving, or
using any Skills. This effect does not prevent the target
from being moved, and may be carried. While under this
effect the player can choose if any forced motion results
in their pose being changed and maintaining the new
pose, or having any attempt result in them falling limp to
the ground.
Pin – This Binding effect prevents the target from
moving their right foot from where it was at the moment
they were affected by the Pin. The target may still pivot
upon the foot but cannot be lifted or moved in any other
way.
Refit <Type> Armour – This effect instantly restores
the target armour type worn by the target of this effect
to its full Armour Point total. This has no effect on
armour that has been Broken or Destroyed.
<Deliver Type/Effect> Reflection or Reflect <Type>
– This effect automatically negates the next effect, to
which it is applicable, by which the target is struck to
rebound against the attacker. A Character may accept
effects which are Touch Cast through any Reflect(ion)
effect. For example, an Elemental Reflection will
affect any effect which is delivered as Elemental(-
Strike). A Binding Reflection will affect any effect
which is a Binding effect regardless of Delivery Type.
A Lightning Reflection will affect any effect which
causes Lightning damage. Finally a Death Reflection
will affect any effect which causes a Death effect
regardless of Delivery Type. The call for this ability is
"Reflect <Effect>".
Regenerate – This effect causes the target to, after a
sixty(60)-count, regain all lost Hit Points.
Regeneration comes in many forms with some
creatures being interrupted if they take any damage,
particular type of damage, are Killing Blowed, and so
on. There are even some creatures able to
Regenerate from Death requiring special methods to
kill. Regeneration is always visible and the target
should announce, in a normal speaking voice, "Visibly
Regenerating" every ten(10) seconds. A small
number of creatures have the ability to Regenerate in
less than a sixty(60)-count but these are very rare.
Rejuvenate – This effect removes, after a sixty(60)-
count, all harmful effects that would be removed by
the target's Death. It is up to the Character's player to
determine which effects are harmful. Rejuvenate is,
usually, interrupted by the Character taking damage
though some creatures have the ability to continue
Rejuvenating even if they are under attack or even
Dead. A small number of creatures have the ability to
Rejuvenate in less than a sixty(60)-count but these
are very rare.
Remove <Effect> – This effect removes a single
effect which matches the type specified by this effect.
For example a "Remove Curse" would remove a
single Curse effect from the target while a "Remove
Poison" would remove a single effect which was
delivered as Poison from the target. If a target has
multiple effects which match the type specified it is up
to the target as to which is removed.
Renew – This effect restores all uses of all Skills
possessed by the target.
Repair – This effect restores a Broken item to usable
condition. It cannot be used to Repair a Destroyed
item.
Repel – This effect forces the target to immediately
take ten(10) steps away from the source of this effect.
If it is not possible for the Player to safely do so they
must move as far away as possible. For example it
cannot force a Player to walk into a hazardous or
unsafe area, but a Character can be pushed into an
in-game hazard such as a trap or through a portal.
This effect lasts ten(10) seconds.
Resist <Delivery Type/Effect> – This ability may be
activated by the Character to negate a single effect to
which it is applicable. A Resist cannot be used to
negate any effect which is delivered as Piercing
regardless of the examples which follow.. For
example a Resist Poison can negate any effect which
is delivered as Poison(-Strike). A Resist Compulsion
can negate any effect which causes a Compulsion
effect regardless of delivery type. A Resist
Break/Destroy can negate any effect which causes
either a Destroy or Break effect. The Resist Perfect
ability applies to all possible effects. The call for this
ability is "Resist <Effect>".
<Delivery Type/Effect> Resistance – This ability
causes the Character to take reduced effect from
effects to which it is applicable. All effects to which it
applies have their durations reduced to ten(10)
seconds as though they were delivered as Lesser.
Additionally, any damage to which it applies is
reduced by half, rounded down. Effects which are
delivered as Piercing bypass this ability. For example,
a creature with Magic Resistance reduces the
duration of all effects delivered as Magic(-Strike) is
reduced as is all damage which is delivered as
Magic(-Strike). A creature with Curse Resistance
reduces the duration of all Curse effects to ten(10)
seconds. A creature with Fire Resistance reduces the
damage from all Fire damage by half. Perfect
Resistance indicates a creature has Resistance to all
effects. The call for this ability is "Reduced".
Revive – This effect restores the target to maximum
Hit Points, even if the target is Dead.
Safeguard – This Effect magically seals the target
Container preventing any creature from accessing the
affected Container. Creatures which were touching
the target Container at the time of casting may add or
remove items from inside the Safeguard. This Effect
cannot be Dispelled. Characters can never be stored
inside of a Safeguard. The Container targeted by this
ability must be marked with an "S". If this Effect is
cast upon a container within a Sanctuary, the Caster
may choose to cause the target container to become
unmovable. This Effect must be included in the
Marshal Notes for the Room.
Sanctuary – This effect can only affect a room. Once
in effect, this effect magically seals the target Room
preventing any creature from entering, or exiting, the
affected area. Creatures which were inside at the
time of casting may enter or exit the Sanctuary room
at will, and can even "Recognize" others into the
area. Neither weapon, nor packet attacks, can pass
through the Sanctuary and those inside are not
affected by any effect originating from outside the
Sanctuary. This effect cannot be dispelled. The Room
targeted by this ability must be marked with a stylized
"S" or "W" at all doorways into the room. This effect
must be included in the Marshal Notes for the Room.
Seal <Container or Portal> – This effect prevents
the target container or door from being opened or
closed for its duration. It has no effect on a container
or portal which cannot be closed. Though it will
function on a curtain. The target of this ability must be
marked with a stylized "S". This effect must be
included in the Marshal Notes for the Room.
<Deliver Type/Effect> Shield – This effect
automatically negates the next effect, to which it is
applicable, by which the target is struck. A Shield
cannot negate any effect which is delivered as
Piercing regardless of any of the following text. A
Character may accept effects which are Touch Cast
through any Shield effect. For example, a Physical
Shield will negate any effect which is delivered as
Physical(-Strike), as well as any effect causing
Weapon Damage. A Compulsion Shield will negate
any effect which is a Compulsion regardless of
Delivery Type. A Fire Shield will negate any effect
which causes Fire damage. Finally a Sleep Shield will
negate any effect which causes a Sleep effect
regardless of Delivery Type. The call for this ability is
"<Effect> Shield".
Shun – This Compulsion effect prevents the target
from willingly approaching or attacking the Caster.
This effect ends if the Caster attacks the target.
Silence – This Compulsion effect prevents the target
from speaking or casting Spells with an Incantation.
This effect also ends any Spells, with a duration of
Sustained, the target has in effect. Additionally, the
target cannot intentionally create any sound such as
clapping or using a noise making tool.
Sleep – This Compulsion Spell causes the target to
enter into a magical sleep preventing them from
moving or using any Skills. As soon as it is safe to do
so, the target must lay down as if asleep. The target
is unaware of their surroundings and treated as
Unconscious.
Spell Conversion – This ability allows a Character to
expend a Spell Slot of any level in order to regain a
Spell Slot of any level below that of the expended
Spell Slot.
Stabilise – This effect can only affect a Character
who is Dying and in the Bleed Out count. The
Character is healed to zero(0) Hit Points, is no longer
Dying but is still Unconscious. After being
unconscious for one(1) minute, the Character
recovers, returning to Consciousness at one(1) Hit
Point.
Stasis – This effect renders the target completely
immobilised, they are unable to move, or be moved,
cannot use Skills, and all Counts and Counted
actions, such as; Dying, Regenerating, Refit, and
even Killing Blows are frozen while this effect is in
place. A target under the effect of Stasis is Immune to
all effects until the effect ends. Even effects delivered
as Arcane cannot bypass this effect.
Strength <#> – This ability increases all weapon
damage of the Character by an amount equal to <#>.
This effect stacks with all similar effects which
increase weapon damage but multiple effects which
grant Strength do not stack unless explicitly stated
otherwise.
When calculating a Character's Strength, first
determine the points of non-stacking Strength then
every point of stacking Strength. For example a Zoan
Character with the Strength in Unity Pact Tactic has
Strength 1 from the ability. But when under the effects
of the Verdant Strength Spell they gain Strength 1.
Their total from non-stacking effects is one(1). This
would stack with the Skill Natural Strength for a total
value of Strength 2.
Tarry – This effect adds five(5) minutes to the target's
Death Count up to a maximum of ten(10) minutes.
Teleport – This effect allows the target to either
return to town, a designated Circle of their choosing,
or to the last location the target cast this Spell. The
target may take any item or items they can carry, but
cannot take any other creature. This spell does not
allow the target to travel between Planes. Upon being
affected by this effect, the target goes Out-of-Game
and walks to the target destination. Immediately,
upon arrival, the Player repeats the Delivery Verbal
and goes In-Game.
Vanquish <Creature Type> – This effect can
instantly reduce the target to zero(0) Hit Points if it is
the specified creature type. If the target is not the
creature type specified by the effect they are not
harmed by the effect. Though most creatures, of the
specified type, are instantly slain, some are instead
damaged by this effect. The amount of damage
varies from creature to creature. Player Characters
are affected by a "Vanquish Humanoid" effect which
causes them to immediately enter their Dying Count.
Vertigo – This effect prevents the target from running
or even standing up right. The target cannot use any
Skills and, additionally, cannot move without crawling.
Vorpal <#> – This ability grants a number of charges
of the Vorpal effect to the target equal to <#>. This
ability increases the Character's Weapon Damage
call by five(5) for a single landed hit for each charge.
A charge is consumed only when the Character lands
a valid hit. One which strikes a shield or other
weapon does not consume a charge, nor does an
accidental strike to an illegal target. All remaining
charges are lost at the end of Combat.
<Delivery Type/Effect> Ward – This ability may be
activated by the Character to negate a single effect to
which it is applicable. For example a Magic Ward can
negate any effect which is delivered as Magic(-
Strike). A Curse Ward can negate any effect which
causes a Curse effect regardless of delivery type. A
Body Ward can negate any effect which causes Body
damage. A Break Ward can negate any effect which
causes a Break effect. The call for this ability is
"<Effect> Ward".
Weakness – This Curse effect prevents the target
from using Martials Arts with a Range of Strike, or
Skills requiring the use of weapons except Weapon
Proficiencies. The target may still cast Alchemy or
Spells which are delivered as <Delivery Type>-Strike.
Web – This Binding effect forces the target's arms to
their sides and prevents the target from moving or
being moved. The target may still speak and use
Skills which do not require the use of their arms and
hands.
Delivery Types
<Delivery Type>-Strike – An effect which is
delivered as a <Delivery Type>-Strike is one which is
delivered by a weapon. This is a weapon delivered
attack which must strike the target, or their direct
possessions. The attack may not strike areas which
are not legal targets such as the head, hands or
groin. Additionally, any effect delivered as <Delivery
Type>-Strike follows the rules for the specific Delivery
Type. For example, a Magic-Strike follows the rules
under this section as well as those for Magic(-Strike)
<Effect>.
Arcane(-Strike) <Effect> – This effect bypasses any
and all defensive Skills and abilities, including
Immunity.
Area of Effect: <Area> <Delivery Verbal> – This
refers to effects which a defined area with visible
boundaries such as a Circle, Room or the Physical
Representation of a Trap. The affected area is
defined in the "Area" portion of the verbal. Since
these effects affect an area, they cannot be avoided
by Defensive Abilities which are limited to a Weapon
or Packet delivered effect such as Parry but can be
defended against by other effects which are
applicable to the Delivery Type or the Effect itself.
Contingency <Delivery Verbal> – These effects are
ones which are automatically triggered when a
particular trigger has been met. Most commonly
these triggers are entering a particular state of living
such as Dead, Dying, or Unconscious but can be any
valid trigger states defined in the source of this ability.
All effects delivered as Contingency follow all rules
for Touch Casting an effect.
Elemental(-Strike) <Effect> – This effect is magical
in nature but composed of elemental energy. Effects,
with a duration other than instant, are removable by a
Remove Elemental, or Dispel Elemental, or effect
which targets the delivered effect.
Lesser <Delivery Verbal> – Effects which are
delivered as Lesser have a duration of ten(10)
seconds. They can be removed by any effect which
would remove the base effect. In the Deliver Verbal,
Lesser is used immediately before the Delivery Type
or, if present, Piercing. For example, "Lesser Piercing
Magic Sleep" or "Lesser Arcane Stasis".
Magic(-Strike) <Effect> – This effect is magical in
nature and composed of magical energy. Effects, with
a duration other than instant, are removable by a
Remove Magic, or Dispel Magic, or effect which
targets the delivered effect.
Physical(-Strike) <Effect> – This effect is mundane
in nature, though its origin may involve magic in its
creation. Effects, with a duration other than instant,
are removable by a Remove Physical, or Dispel
Physical, or effect which targets the delivered effect.
Piercing <Delivery Verbal> – Effects which are
delivered as Piercing bypass any <Effect> Shield,
Resist <Effect> or <Effect> Resistance the target
may have. In the Deliver Verbal, Piercing is used
immediately before the Delivery Type. For example,
"10 Piercing Magic Fire" or "Piercing Elemental
Death".
Poison(-Strike) <Effect> – This effect is mundane in
nature, being created through Alchemy or the
biological processes of certain creatures. Effects, with
a duration other than instant, are removable by a
Remove Poison, or Dispel Poison, or effect which
targets the delivered effect.
Sound of Voice: <Qualifier> <Delivery Verbal> –
This powerful ability affects all targets which meet the
designated Qualifier, if any, with the effect defined in
the Deliver Verbal. All a Character has to do to be
affected by such an effect is be able to hear the
Deliver Verbal. It is absolutely not acceptable to
simply cover your ears or yell loudly to prevent
yourself or others from hearing the effect and
intentionally doing so constitutes a breach of Fair
Play. Since these effects affect an area, they cannot
be avoided by Defensive Abilities which are limited to
a Weapon or Packet delivered effect such as Dodge
but can be defended against by other effects which
are applicable to the Delivery Type or the Effect itself.
Touch – Though not a delivery type any effect which
can be delivered by one or more packets can also be
delivered as Touch. This has benefits in that there is
no chance of missing your target and it will not trigger
any protective effects the target may have but it also
allows the target the option to refuse the effect. Any
effect, which is delivered by Touch can be "Refused"
by the target. A target who has Refused a Touch
Casting is never affected by the effect. An
Unconscious Character cannot Refuse a Touch Cast
Effect, though they may accept it allowing it to bypass
any protective effects they may have. When cast on
an unwilling Unconscious Character the target may
trigger protectives as though it were a normal packet
delivered attack.
Chapter 7: Alchemy, Magic and Martial Arts
Technique Descriptions
The following are the rules for understanding the
various entries describing Alchemy, Martial Arts and
Spells. Although the nature of these effects vary, all
entries follow the same template.
Technique Name [Effect Keyword]
School: Technique's School Name
Level: Technique Level
Range: Technique Range
Duration: Technique Duration
Effect: What the Technique does and any special
rules for using the Technique.
Incantation: The Technique's Incantation, if any.
Delivery Verbal: The Delivery Verbal which must be
spoken before delivering the Technique.
Technique Keywords
Certain Techniques have associated keywords.
These are shorthand words that make it easy to
recognise an Technique as belonging to a specific
category or if special rules are associated with the
Technique. These keywords are next to the name of
the Alchemy, Martial Art, or Spell being described.
Binding – The effect is considered a Binding Effect.
Binding effects are those which restrict the target's
movements and can be defended against, or
Removed, by abilities which are applicable against
Binding effects or the Delivery Type used to deliver
the Binding effect.
Compulsion – The effect is considered a
Compulsion Effect. Compulsion effects are those
which affect the target's mind and can be defended
against, or Removed, by abilities which are applicable
against Compulsion effects or the Delivery Type used
to deliver the Compulsion effect. The Effects
Dominate and Enthral can override weaker
Compulsion effects including; Calm, Shun, and Fear.
In the event of a Character being affected by Enthral
and Dominate the Enthral effect overrides the
Dominate.
Curse – The effect is considered a Curse Effect.
Curse effects have a variety of different effects such
as preventing the target from using Skills, causing
them to take more damage, or immobilising them
completely. are those which affect the target's mind
and can be defended against, or Removed, by
abilities which are applicable against Curse effects or
the Delivery Type used to deliver the Curse effect.
School
All Techniques belong to a particular School. The
Character must have access to the School to which
the Technique belongs in order to perform a
Technique.
Affliction – The Spell's School of Magic
Alchemy – Alchemy
Conjuration – The Spell's School of Magic
Duelist – The Art's Martial Arts School
Guardian – The Art's Martial Arts School
Protection – The Spell's School of Magic
Restoration – The Spell's School of Magic
Slayer – The Art's Martial Arts School
Transmutation – The Spell's School of Magic
Level
The Technique's level, numbered one(1) through
nine(9). This indicates the Slot which must be
expended in order to cast the Technique.
Range
Packet – The Technique can be delivered as a
packet attack, by Touch, or on the Caster.
Packet, Multiple – The Technique can be delivered
as a packet attack, by Touch, or on the Caster. When
delivered as a packet attack the Character may throw
any number of packets. Any creature struck by one,
or more, packets is affected by the effect only once.
Unless otherwise stated, the Caster may throw the
packets using both hands, and all packets must be
thrown all at once. If not delivered as Packet, the
effect only affects a single target.
Self – The Technique can only be used on the
Caster, or the Technique affects the Caster in some
way granting them special abilities. Techniques with
this Range cannot benefit from the effects of the
Piercing Slime or Piercing Spell Skills. These
Techniques follow the rules for Touch casting Spells
except the Caster may only use these Techniques on
themselves.
Touch – The Technique can be delivered as a Touch
Effect, or on the Caster. Techniques with this Range
cannot benefit from the effects of the Piercing Slime
or Piercing Spell Skills.
Duration
5 Minutes – The Technique lasts until it is Removed,
Dispelled, the Character Dies, or five(5) minutes
pass.
10 Seconds – The Technique lasts until it is
Removed, Dispelled, the Character Dies, or ten(10)
seconds pass.
Charged (#) – This Technique grants the target
multiple Charges of an ability. The Character may
expend charges as described by the Technique. A
Character can expend charges even if they are under
an effect which prevents them from using Skills such
as Drain. The Character must be Conscious to
expend Charges. All Charges are lost if the Character
Dies, the Technique is Removed or Dispelled, or at
the end of the Reset.
Concentration – The Technique lasts until it is
Removed, Dispelled, the Character Dies, the end of
the Day, Reset occurs, or the Character is affected by
any effect which would Disrupt the Skill. If the
Character uses any other Skill while Concentrating,
the Technique ends. Additionally, the Character may
need to follow some additional condition(s) described
by the individual Technique. Finally, the Character
cannot run while Concentrating.
Day – The Technique lasts until it is Removed,
Dispelled, the Character Dies, or the end of the Day.
Indefinite – The Technique lasts until it is Removed,
Dispelled, the Character Dies, or the current Event
ends.
Instant – The Technique is immediate. It cannot be
Removed, or Dispelled, as there is nothing left to
remove. Techniques with this Duration are the only
ones which can benefit from the effects of the
Catalyst or Empower Spell Skills.
Sustained – The Technique lasts until it is Removed,
Dispelled, the Character Dies, Reset occurs, or the
end of the Day. If this Technique is granted by a Spell,
the Technique also ends if the Character casts any
other Spell, or enters any State where they are
unable to cast Spells. If this Technique is granted by
a Martial Art, the Technique also ends if the Character
casts any other Martial Art, or enters any State where
they are unable to cast Martial Art. If this Technique is
granted by an Alchemy, the Technique also ends if
the Character casts any other Alchemy, or enters any
State where they are unable to cast Alchemy.
Timed – These Techniques have a fixed duration.
Either five(5) minutes or ten(10) seconds.
Effect
This section describes what the Technique does as
well as any special rules the Caster needs to follow.
Incantation
This phrase, found only in Spells, is the Spell's
Incantation. Normally Spells must have their
Incantations spoken out loud prior to speaking the
Delivery Verbal. There are a small number of Spells
which do not have Incantations and there are Skills,
such as Quicken Spell and Innate Spell Casting,
which allow a Character to cast Spells without
Incantations.
Delivery Verbal
The Delivery Verbal is a phrase which must be
spoken, loud enough for the target to hear, prior to
delivering the Technique. This section is never
optional as it represents the effect the ability has on
the game on a mechanical level
Stacking Techniques
Unless explicitly stated otherwise, no more than
one effect with the same name can be stacked, which
is to say, a Character can be affected by only one
instance of any effect at a time. This means that a
Character who uses a Martial Art which grants them a
Magic Ward cannot also benefit from a Spell which
grants a Magic Ward. Techniques with the same
name, including effects such as Mirror Image,
Greater Mirror Image and Superior Mirror Image,
cannot be stacked either.
Alchemy
Casting Alchemy
Casting Alchemy requires a Character to have a
free hand, a packet held in that hand, an available
Alchemy Slot of the level of the Alchemy they wish to
cast, and access to the School of Alchemy to which
the Alchemy belongs. Each Alchemy has a Delivery
Verbal associated with it which must be said by the
Caster, in a clear voice loud enough for the target to
hear, prior to delivering the effect. Once the Delivery
Verbal has been stated the Character has three(3)
seconds to throw the packet or touch the target. The
Delivery Verbal must be made in a normal speaking
voice and loud enough to be heard by the target and
an imaginary observer standing next to the target.
Whispering Delivery Verbals is not permitted.
If a Character fumbles the Delivery Verbal the
Alchemy fails but is not expended. The Character can
Meditate in order to regain the use of the fumbled
Alchemy. Likewise, if the Character fails to hit any
target with the Packet or Weapon Strike, is Disrupted,
the Alchemy is Resisted, Warded, or the target is
Immune the Character may regain it by Meditating.
This does not mean that it is acceptable to
intentionally fail the Delivery Verbal or deliberately
miss a target. It is meant to allow a Player who has
tried, in earnest, to perform the Alchemy properly but
failed, to recover the wasted Skill.
Touch Casting Alchemy
Some Alchemy can be delivered by Touch. This
has benefits in that there is no chance of missing
your target and it will not trigger any protective effects
the target may have but it also allows the target the
option to refuse the effect. Any effect, which is
delivered by Touch can be "Refused" by the target. A
target who has Refused a Touch Casting is never
affected by the effect. An Unconscious Character
cannot Refuse a Touch Cast Alchemy, though they
may accept it allowing it to bypass any protective
effects they may have. When cast on an unwilling
Unconscious Character the target may trigger
protectives as though it were a normal packet
delivered attack. All Alchemy effects which allow a
Character to deliver an effect by Packet can also be
delivered by Touch.
Channelling Alchemy
Certain effects, such as the Skills Slimy Coating
and Envenom Weapon, allow a Character to deliver
an Alchemy as an <Effect>-Strike. The Alchemy's
normal Delivery Verbal determines the Delivery
Verbal of the <Effect>-Strike. Effects which are
delivered as Poison <Effect> are delivered as
Poison-Strike <Effect>, while effects delivered as
Lesser Physical <Effect> are delivered as Lesser
Physical-Strike <Effect>, and so on.
In order to cast an Alchemy in this way the
Character must; be wielding a weapon, with which
they are Proficient, state the Spell's Delivery Verbal,
modified as described in the above section, and strike
their target, or their immediate possessions with a
valid weapon strike with the wielded weapon. Shields
and Weapons cannot block an <Effect>-Strike unless
the target has the explicit ability to do so.
It is not possible to Channel an Alchemy when you
are in a state where you are unable to wield
weapons, even by Touch Casting. This means that
you cannot Channel Alchemy while under the effects
of a Web, Fumble, or Cripple <Arm> if you are using
a Two-Handed Weapon.
If an Alchemy grants a Character the ability to
deliver a packet attack, or touch effect, such as the
Alchemy Alchemist's Fire, a Character cannot deliver
these effects by Channelling unless the Alchemy's
description explicitly states otherwise.
Disrupting Alchemy
Alchemy can be Disrupted by causing the Caster to
take Hit Point damage between when they start the
Delivery Verbal and when they deliver the effect.
Additionally, causing the Caster to enter a state
where they are unable to cast Alchemy, such as by
causing them to lose access to Skills, or depriving
them of a free hand such as by some Binding effects
or the Cripple effect.
Stacking Effects
Unless explicitly stated otherwise, no more than
one effect with the same name can be stacked, which
is to say, a Character can be affected by only one
instance of any effect at a time. Spells, and similar
effects, with the same name, including effects such
as Mirror Image, Greater Mirror Image and Superior
Mirror Image, cannot be stacked either.
Alchemical Traditions
Alchemical Traditions represent the manner in
which a Character learned, or practises Alchemy.
Effects which renew or recharge Skills, such as
Inspiration, may never be used to increase the
number of times a Character can benefit from the
effects of their Tradition.
Traditions apply only to Alchemical effects which
the Character delivers from Memory.
Alchemical Conversion [Tradition,
Alchemical]
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: When casting an Alchemy from memory the
Character can choose to expend an Alchemy Slot
which is one(1) or more levels higher than that of the
Alchemy they wish to cast. Casting an Alchemy in this
way consumes only the higher level Alchemy Slot.
Alchemical Leavings [Tradition, Alchemical]
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Whenever the Alchemist casts an Alchemy
from memory, they may also deliver a packet attack
causing an amount of Poison Acid, or Poison Fire
damage equal to the level of the Alchemy cast. The
packet attack must be delivered within ten(10)
seconds of the Alchemy.
Alchemical Reserve [Tradition, Alchemical]
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may Rest in
order to recover three(3) expended Alchemy Slots of
any level(s) they can cast. Additionally, once per
Reset, the Character may grant themselves the effect
of a "Poison Clarity" granting them the benefits of
Meditating instantly.
Careful Preparations [Tradition, Alchemical]
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: After each Reset, the Character may choose
one(1) Alchemy of each level, for which they have
Alchemy Slots, adding them to a list of specially
prepared Alchemy. The Character may cast each
Alchemy on their list of prepared Alchemy once
without expending an Alchemy Slot.
Master Poisoner [Tradition, Alchemical]
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Whenever the Character casts an Alchemy
and mixes it into a food or drink the effect is delivered
as Piercing. This ability can be used any number of
times per Reset, but the Character must Rest in
between uses.
Pocket Flask [Tradition, Alchemical]
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: The Character may cast a single Alchemy
on themselves while under an effect that would
prevent them from using Alchemy such as Drain. This
ability can be used any number of times per Reset,
but the Character must Rest in between uses.
Recycling [Tradition, Alchemical]
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may Rest in
order to recover a single expended Alchemy Slot for
each level they can cast.
Toxicology [Tradition, Alchemical]
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Whenever the Character casts any Alchemy
on themselves they may grant themselves the effects
of a "Poison Vorpal 1". If the Character casts multiple
Alchemies on themselves in sequence, with no more
than ten(10) seconds passing between each casting,
they may combine the benefit of this ability into a
single effect. The call for this ability is "Poison Vorpal
<X>". Where "X" is the number of Alchemies the
Character has cast in sequence.
Volatile Concoction [Tradition, Alchemical]
Requirements: Level 1 Alchemy Slot
Benefit: Whenever the Character uses an Alchemy
with a duration of Sustained, they may choose to use
this ability. If they do, they may continue to use
Alchemy normally but each Alchemy the Character
casts, including those cast as part of the Skill
Contingent Slime, adds one(1) charge to this ability.
Once the Character reaches five(5) charges, or takes
Hit Point Damage, all Charges are expended and the
Character takes ten(10) Poison Body damage for
each Charge they have. The effects of this ability can
be negated if the Character is affected by a Remove
or Dispel Poison effect before either trigger event
occurs.

Table 7.1 – Alchemy List
Alchemy Descriptions
Acid Flask
School: Alchemy
Level: 7
Range: Packet, Multiple
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Alchemy allows the Character to throw
any number of packets. Any creature struck by one or
more packets takes twenty(20) points of Poison Acid
damage.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "20 Poison Acid"
Acid Wave
School: Alchemy
Level: 9
Range: Packet, Multiple
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Alchemy allows the Character to throw
any number of packets. Any creature struck by one or
more packets takes thirty(30) points of Poison Acid
damage.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "30 Poison Acid"
Adhesive Splash [Binding]
School: Alchemy
Level: 8
Range: Packet, Multiple
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Binding Alchemy allows the Character to
throw any number of packets. Any creature struck by
one or more packets is affected by a Physical Pin
effect preventing any target from moving, or lifting,
their right foot though they may still pivot upon it.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Physical Pin"
Alchemist's Fire
School: Alchemy
Level: 9
Range: Self and Packet
Duration: Sustained
Effect: This Alchemy allows the Character to throw a
packet attack causing ten(10) Poison Fire damage at
will. The Character may continue to throw this attack
until they die, the current Reset period ends, or use
another Alchemy. Each individual packet attack is
subject to Disruption but this effect is not.
This Alchemy cannot benefit from the effects of any
Mutation or ability which modifies the casting of a
single Alchemy.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "10 Poison Fire" … "10 Poison
Fire"
Alchemist's Flask
School: Alchemy
Level: 2
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: Upon using this Alchemy the Character
chooses one of the following damage types; Acid, or
Fire. The Character may deliver a packet attack
causing ten(10) points of Poison damage of the
chosen type.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "10 Poison Acid" or "10 Poison Fire"
Alchemist's Flask, Greater
School: Alchemy
Level: 6
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: Upon using this Alchemy the Character
chooses one of the following damage types; Acid, or
Fire. The Character may deliver a packet attack
causing thirty(30) points of Poison damage of the
chosen type.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "30 Poison Acid" or "30 Poison Fire"
Anaesthetize [Compulsion]
School: Alchemy
Level: 6
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Compulsion Alchemy causes the target
to fall into a deep sleep preventing them from moving
or using any Skills. The target will lie down wherever
they are as soon as it is safe to do so. The target is
not aware of their surroundings and is not considered
Conscious. This Alchemy is removed by any effect
which removes Compulsion or Poisons.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Sleep"
Antidote
School: Alchemy
Level: 5
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Alchemy removes all effects which were
delivered as Poison(-Strike) which are currently
affecting the target.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Dispel Poison"
Armour Patch
School: Alchemy
Level: 3
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Alchemy instantly Refits any Physical
Armour worn by the target to its full value.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Refit Physical Armour"
Berserk
School: Alchemy
Level: 9
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Alchemy forces the Character into an
insane killing frenzy forcing them to attack everyone
around them to the best of their Player's ability using
the most effective means at their disposal. This
means that the Character's morals or personality
have no impact on the means they use to attack
those around them.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Berserk"
Binding Agent [Binding]
School: Alchemy
Level: 4
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Binding Alchemy forces the target's arms
to their sides and keeps them there throughout its
duration. The creature cannot use any Skill which
requires the use of their arms such as using weapons
or casting Spells.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Physical Bind"
Cause Critical Damage
School: Alchemy
Level: 6
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Alchemy causes thirty(30) points of
Poison Body damage to the target.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "30 Poison Body"

Choking Vapours [Compulsion]
School: Alchemy
Level: 5
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Compulsion Alchemy prevents the target
from speaking or casting Spells with an Incantation.
Additionally, the target cannot intentionally create any
sound such as clapping or using a noise making tool.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Silence"
Coagulant
School: Alchemy
Level: 1
Range: Self and Touch
Duration: Sustained
Effect: This Alchemy grants the Caster the ability to
deliver a "Poison Stabilise" by Touch at will. The
Caster may continue delivering this effect until they
die or cast another Alchemy. Each individual casting
of the Touch effect is subject to Disruption. Stabilise
instantly puts a target which is Bleeding Out to
zero(0) Hit Points.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Stabilise" … "Poison
Stabilise"

Disinfect
School: Alchemy
Level: 7
Range: Packet
Duration: Charged (1)
Effect: This Alchemy grants the target a Ward
allowing them to negate a single effect delivered as
Poison(-Strike) by which they are struck. The target
chooses when to activate this ability.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Poison Ward"
Disintegoo
School: Alchemy
Level: 6
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Alchemy Destroys the target item. A
Destroyed item cannot be Repaired. Certain
creatures, such as Constructs, may be damaged by
this Alchemy. If the target has multiple items in their
possession which match the description of the item
designated by the Caster it is the target who chooses
which of their items are affected. The target cannot
choose an item which is Indestructible unless it is the
only item they have which matches that description.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Destroy <Item>"
Dissolve
School: Alchemy
Level: 3
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Alchemy Breaks the item designated by
the Caster rendering it unusable until it is Repaired. If
the target has multiple items in their possession
which match the description of the item designated by
the Caster it is the target who chooses which of their
items are affected. The target cannot choose an item
which is Indestructible unless it is the only item they
have which matches that description. Certain
creatures, such as Constructs, may be damaged by
this Alchemy.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Break <Item>"
Distracting Fumes [Compulsion]
School: Alchemy
Level: 2
Range: Packet
Duration: 10 Seconds
Effect: This Compulsion Alchemy prevents the target
from willingly approaching the Caster or attacking the
Caster. This effect ends if the Caster attacks the
target.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Lesser Poison Shun"
Dominate [Compulsion]
School: Alchemy
Level: 8
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 minutes
Effect: This Compulsion Alchemy places the target
under the complete control of the Caster. They will
follow all orders, even self destructive ones. Please
note that this Alchemy does not force a Player to
engage in any activities they are not comfortable with
performing.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Dominate"
Elixir of Death/Life
School: Alchemy
Level: 9
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: As part of casting this Spell the Caster
chooses one of the following options; Death or Life.
This Alchemy allows the Caster to deliver the chosen
effect as a packet attack.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Death" or "Poison Life"
Explosive Concoction
School: Alchemy
Level: 9
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: As part of casting this Spell the Caster
chooses one of the following options; Acid or Fire.
This Alchemy allows the Caster to deliver a packet
attack causing fifty(50) points of Poison damage of
the chosen type.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "50 Poison Acid" or "50 Poison Fire"
Fortifying Elixir
School: Alchemy
Level: 1
Range: Packet
Duration: Indefinite
Effect: This Alchemy grants the target five(5)
Fortification Points. These Fortification Points are
always the first lost due to damage. Multiple effects
which grant Fortification Points do not stack.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Fortify 5"
Glue [Curse]
School: Alchemy
Level: 3
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Curse Alchemy prevents the target from
running or jumping. The target must have at least one
foot on the ground at all times and cannot move
faster than a normal walking pace.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Fetter"
Hallucinogen
School: Alchemy
Level: 2
Range: Packet
Duration: 10 Seconds
Effect: This Alchemy prevents the target from
running or even standing up right. The target cannot
use any Skills and, additionally, cannot move without
crawling. This Alchemy lasts ten(10) seconds.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Lesser Poison Vertigo"
Herbicide
School: Alchemy
Level: 7
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Alchemy instantly Vanquishes the target
Plant Type Creature. A Vanquished creature instantly
begins Bleeding Out. Some powerful Plant creatures
may instead take damage from this Alchemy.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Vanquish Plant"
Insecticide
School: Alchemy
Level: 7
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Alchemy instantly Vanquishes the target
Insectoid Type Creature. A Vanquished creature
instantly begins Bleeding Out. Some powerful
Insectoids may instead take damage from this
Alchemy.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Vanquish Insectoid"
Jellyflesh [Curse]
School: Alchemy
Level: 7
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes or Instant
Effect: As part of casting this Alchemy the Caster
may choose a limb such as left arm, left leg, right
arm, right leg. This Alchemy prevents the target from
using the chosen limb. A Crippled arm cannot be
used to use any item or cast Spells. A Crippled leg
prevents the target from walking, forcing them to
kneel.
Alternatively, this Alchemy may be used to remove all
Cripple effects from the target regardless of Delivery
Type.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Poison Cripple <Limb>" or
"Poison Dispel Cripple"
Liquid Light
School: Alchemy
Level: 1
Range: Touch

Magic
Casting a Spell
Casting a Spell requires a Character to have an
available Spell Slot of the level of the spell they wish
to cast, access to the School of Magic to which the
Spell belongs, a free hand, and a packet held by that
hand. Under normal circumstances, a Character must
complete an Incantation to cast a Spell. This
Incantation is a specific phrase which "forms" the
Spell into existence. Once the Incantation is
completed the Character has three(3) seconds to
state the Delivery Verbal of the Spell. Once the
Delivery Verbal has been stated the Character has
three(3) seconds to throw the packet, touch the
target, or otherwise deliver the effect. Both the
Incantation and Delivery Verbal must be made in a
normal speaking voice and loud enough to be heard
by the target and an imaginary observer standing
next to the target. Whispering Delivery Verbals is not
permitted.
If a Character fumbles an Incantation the Spell fails
but is not expended. The Character can Meditate in
order to recover it. Likewise, if the Character fails to
hit any target with the Packet or Weapon Strike, is
Disrupted, the Alchemy is Resisted, Warded, or the
target is Immune the Character may regain it by
Meditating. This does not mean that it is acceptable
to intentionally fail the Incantation or deliberately miss
a target. It is meant to allow a Player who has tried, in
earnest, to perform the Spell properly but failed, to
recover the wasted Skill.
If a Spell causes an effect with a deferred Delivery
Verbal, such as Healing Orb, is delivered without the
Incantation the Character must state "Magic <Spell
Name>" in order to make it clear they case the Spell.
Touch Casting a Spell
Some Spells, and other similar effects, can be
delivered by Touch. This has benefits in that there is
no chance of missing your target and it will not trigger
any protective effects the target may have but it also
allows the target the option to refuse the effect. Any
effect, which is delivered by Touch can be "Refused"
by the target. A target who has Refused a Touch
Casting is never affected by the effect. An
Unconscious Character cannot Refuse a Touch Cast
Spell, though they may accept it allowing it to bypass
any protective effects they may have. When cast on
an unwilling Unconscious Character the target may
trigger protectives as though it were a normal packet
delivered attack. All Spell effects which allow a
Character to deliver an effect by Packet can also be
delivered by Touch.

Channelling a Spell
Certain effects, such as the Skills Channel Spell
and Spell Channelling, allow a Character to deliver a
Spell as an <Effect>-Strike. The Spell's normal
Delivery Verbal determines the Delivery Verbal of the
<Effect>-Strike. Effects which are delivered as Magic
<Effect> are delivered as Magic-Strike <Effect>, while
effects delivered as Lesser Physical <Effect> are
delivered as Lesser Physical-Strike <Effect>, and so
on.
In order to cast a Spell in this way the Character
must; be wielding a weapon, with which they are
Proficient, complete the Spell's Incantation, unless
another Skill, such as Innate Spellcasting, removes
this requirement, state the Spell's Delivery Verbal,
modified as described in the above section, and strike
their target, or their immediate possessions with a
valid weapon strike with the wielded weapon. Shields
and Weapons cannot block an <Effect>-Strike unless
the target has the explicit ability to do so.
It is not possible to Channel a Spell when you are
in a state where you are unable to wield weapons,
even by Touch Casting. This means that you cannot
Channel Spells while under the effects of a Web,
Fumble, or Cripple <Arm> if you are using a Two-
Handed Weapon.
If an Spell grants a Character the ability to deliver a
packet attack, or touch effect, such as the Spells
Element's Fury and Healing Touch, a Character
cannot deliver these effects by Channelling unless
the Spell's description explicitly states otherwise.
Disrupting a Spell
A Spell can be Disrupted by causing the Caster to
take Hit Point damage between when they start the
Incantation and when they deliver the effect.
Additionally, causing the Caster to enter a state
where they are unable to cast Spells, such as by
causing them to lose access to Skills, or depriving
them of a free hand such as by some Binding effects
or the Cripple effect.
Stacking Effects
Unless explicitly stated otherwise, no more than
one effect with the same name can be stacked, which
is to say, a Character can be affected by only one
instance of any effect at a time. Spells, and similar
effects, with the same name, including effects such
as Mirror Image, Greater Mirror Image and Superior
Mirror Image, cannot be stacked either.
Magical Traditions
Magical Traditions represent the manner in which a
Character learned, or practises magic. Effects which
renew or recharge Skills, such as Inspiration, may
never be used to increase the number of times a
Character can benefit from the effects of their
Tradition.
The effects of Magical Traditions apply only to
Spells the Character casts from memory.
Bardic Magic [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: The Character, the Bard, may create a
specially prepared Stage which allows for the casting
of Spells on multiple targets. In order to use this
ability the Character must first prepare a Stage. A
valid Stage is either an enclosed Room, a structure
with a roof or a valid Circle representation.
The process to prepare the Stage requires the
Character to perform a sixty(60)-second, or longer,
count where they role-play; setting up props, testing
the acoustic or otherwise preparing a stage for a
performance. Once the Stage has been prepared it
remains prepared until the Character leaves its
boundaries. Multiple Characters can share the same
Stage, but each must prepare it for their own use as
above.
Whenever the Character casts a Spell while within
the boundaries of a Stage they prepared they may
perform a sixty(60)-count performance in the form of
music, oration, or other form of public performance.
Ideally this performance should be relevant to the
Spell being cast, this is not necessary. At the end of
this performance the Bard may cast a single Spell,
with a Range of Packet or Touch, and deliver it as
Touch against any number of targets who are within
the boundary of the Stage. No target may benefit
from a single Spell more than once. Once the Bard
goes ten(10)-seconds without affecting a new target,
the Spell ends.
Bound Caster [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: The Character is unable to throw packets to
deliver Spells and is restricted to delivering all Spells
as Touch. This restriction applies to Spells the
Character casts from Scrolls, Wands, and Runes.
The Character can throw Spells Imbued into Ritual
Items normally. Spells which grant the Character the
ability to deliver a packet attack, such as Elemental
Fury and Force Storm, cannot be cast by the
Character. Though this ability does not prevent the
target from casting other harmful spells, the fact that
they must be delivered as Touch means that they are
essentially useless in attacking. Any time the
Character delivers a Spell by Touch they may cast a
second copy of the Spell, which must be delivered
within ten(10) seconds of the first. The call for this
effect is "Echo <Delivery Verbal>". Unlike the Echo
Spellshape Skill, the additional casting granted by
this ability does not include any Spellshapes which
were applied to the original Spell.
Dawn Magic [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may Rest in
order to recover a single expended Spell Slot for
each level they can cast.
Dusk Magic [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may Rest in
order to recover three(3) expended Spell Slots of any
level(s) they can cast. Additionally, once per Reset,
the Character may grant themselves the effect of a
"Magic Clarity" granting them the benefits of
Meditating instantly.
Incanter's Repertoire [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: A number of times per Reset, equal to the
level of the highest level Spell Slot the Character can
cast, the Character may Rest in order to regain a
single expended Spell Slot for a Spell they cast from
Memory and delivered with the Incantation. For
example, a Character with a 4th Level Spell Slot can
use this ability four(4) times per Reset in order to
recover a total of four(4) Spell Slots. While a
Character with 9th Level Spell Slots can use it nine(9)
times per Reset. The Character may expend multiple
uses of this ability during a single Rest.
Additionally, once per Rest, any Spell the
Character has cast using its full Incantation, which
could be regained by Meditation, may be recovered
by the Character on a ten(10) second count. This
secondary ability ability may be recharged by
Resting.
Living Magic [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: Whenever the Character casts a Spell, from
memory, they may also deliver, by Touch, an amount
of Magic Healing equal to the level of the Spell cast.
This healing must be used within ten(10) seconds of
casting the Spell.
Alternatively, multiple Spells can be cast in
sequence. So long as no more than ten(10) seconds
go by between castings the total levels cast can be
combined into a single Magic Healing effect. For
example a Character could cast a number of
protectives on their target whose combined levels
equal seventeen(17), they could then deliver "17
Magic Healing" by Touch to any target within ten(10)
seconds of the last Spell being cast.
Spells which grant the Character the ability to cast
multiple effects similar to spells, such as Healing
Touch, only trigger this ability upon the expenditure of
the originating Spell Slot.
Metaphysical Magic [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: The Character may spend a sixty(60)-
second count to attune to an element. Upon doing so,
the Character may choose one of the following
elemental damage types; Chaos, Destruction,
Dream, Essence, Fire, Ice, Light, Lightning, Order,
Reason, Shadow, or Stone. Whenever the Character
casts a Spell which gives them the option to choose
from the following types; Fire, Ice, Lightning, or
Stone, they may instead choose the elemental
Damage Type to which they have attuned. The
Character can attune to a new element but must Rest
before changing elements. Spells which list only a
single damage type, such as Fireball, cannot be used
in conjunction with this ability.
Prepared Magic [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: After each Reset, the Character may choose
one(1) Spell of each level, for which they have Spell
Slots, adding them to a list of specially prepared
Spells. The Character must have access to the
School of Magic of any Spells which they prepare.
The Character may cast each Spell on their list of
prepared Spells once without expending a Spell Slot.
Sorcery [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: When casting a Spell from memory the
Character can choose to expend a Spell Slot which is
one(1) or more levels higher than that of the Spell
they wish to cast. Casting a Spell in this way
consumes only the higher level Spell Slot.
If the Character has this Tradition and casts the
Cantrip Spell Conversion, they instead regain the
ability to cast one(1) of their expended Cantrips as
though they had Rested, in place of this Cantrip's
normal effects.
Specialised Magic [Tradition, Magical]
Requirements: Level 1 Spell Slot
Benefit: The Character chooses a single School of
Magic. The Character gains one(1) additional Spell
Slot of each level they can cast. These additional
Spell Slots must be used to cast Spells from the
chosen School of Magic. These additional Spell Slots
do not count towards the requirements for any Skill.
Cantrips
Cantrips are special magical effects which can be
cast by any Character who has access to the
appropriate School of Magic. While there is no limit to
how many times per Reset each Cantrip can be used,
they still follow all rules for Casting a Spell including
Disruption. As a Character gains access to additional
Schools of Magic, they gain access to additional
Cantrips each of which can be cast once and are
recovered when the Character Rests. Cantrips
cannot be recovered by Meditating nor are they a
valid target for effects such as Inspiration or Renew.
Energy Darts
School: Conjuration
Level: Cantrip
Range: Self and Packet
Duration: Charged (5)
Effect: This Cantrip grants the Caster five(5) charges
of this effect. The Caster may expend one(1) charge
to deliver a packet attack causing two(2) points of
Elemental Damage of any of the following types; Fire,
Ice, Lightning, or Stone. As a Cantrip, this effect can
be used once, afterwards the Character must Rest in
order to regain its use.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "2 Elemental <Type>"

School: Protection
Level: Cantrip
Range: Self
Duration: Indefinite
Effect: This Cantrip grants the Caster five(5)
Fortification Points. As a Cantrip, this effect can be
used once, afterwards the Character must Rest in
order to regain its use.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Fortify 5"
Healer's Touch

deliver, by Touch, one(1) point of Magic Healing. As a

Character must Rest in order to regain its use.

Spell Conversion
School: Transmutation
Level: Cantrip
Range: Self
Duration: Instant

single Spell Slot in order to regain a single Spell Slot

Cantrip. As a Cantrip, this effect can be used once,
afterwards the Character must Rest in order to regain
its use.
If the Character has the Skill Magical Tradition
<Sorcery>, they instead regain the ability to cast
one(1) of their expended Cantrips as though they had
Rested, in place of this Cantrip's normal effects.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Spell Conversion"
Withering Fire

the Character must Rest in order to regain its use.
