Table 7.6 – Transmutation Spell List
Spell Descriptions
Absorb Energy
School: Conjuration, Protection
Level: 8
Range: Touch
Duration: Charged
Effect: Upon casting this Spell, the Caster chooses
one of the following energy types; Acid, Fire, Ice,
Lightning, or Stone. The target may Absorb a single
effect, which causes the specified damage type,
negating it and healing a number of Hit Points equal
to the damage absorbed. Any healing above the
Character's maximum Hit Point total is lost. The call
for the Absorb ability is "Absorb".
Incantation: "I grant you the Protection of Absorb
Energy"
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Imbue Skill: Absorb
<Type>"
Acid Splash
School: Conjuration
Level: 2
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Spell allows the Caster to deliver a
packet attack causing ten(10) points of Physical Acid
damage.
Incantation: "I Conjure an Acid Splash."
Delivery Verbal: "10 Physical Acid"
Aegis
School: Protection
Level: 6
Range: Self
Duration: Concentration
Effect: Upon casting this Spell, the Caster must
cross both arms across their chest and can move at
no faster than a walking pace. So long as both these
requirements are met, the Caster is Immune to all
Weapon Damage and <Effect>-Strikes.
Incantation: "I grant you the protection of an Aegis."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Aegis"
Alarm
School: Protection
Level: 1
Range: Room
Duration: Day
Effect: This Spell causes the target room to become
magically protected. Any creature within the room at
the time of casting gains an investiture into the Alarm.
Any creature, who is not invested, that attempts to
enter this room must knock loudly for ten(10) seconds
upon entering. A room which is the target of this Spell
must have a large "A" emblazoned on the back of the
Room's Marshal Notes.
Incantation: "I grant you the protection of an Alarm."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Alarm"
Alarm, Greater
School: Protection
Level: 4
Range: Room
Duration: Day
Effect: This Spell causes the target room to become
magically protected. Any creature within the room at
the time of casting gains an investiture into the Alarm.
Any creature, who is not invested, that attempts to
enter this room must knock loudly for ten(10) seconds
upon entering. A room which is the target of this Spell
must have a large "A" emblazoned on the back of the
Room's Marshal Notes. This effect cannot be
Dispelled.
Incantation: "I grant you the protection of a Greater
Alarm."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Greater Alarm"
Alarm, Superior
School: Protection
Level: 7
Range: Room
Duration: Day
Effect: This Spell causes the target Room to become
magically protected. Any creature within the room at
the time of casting gains an investiture into the Alarm.
Any creature, who is not invested, that attempts to
enter this room must knock loudly for ten(10) seconds
upon entering. A room which is the target of this Spell
must have a large "A" emblazoned on the back of the
Room's Marshal Notes. Additionally, the intruding
creature is affected by a single harmful Spell from the
Caster's memory which is cast as part of casting this
Spell and below 7th Level. The chosen spell is
delivered as Magic against the intruder. This effect
cannot be Dispelled.
Incantation: "I grant you the protection of a Superior
Alarm."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Superior Alarm <Spell
Name>"
Ambush
School: Transmutation
Level: 1
Range: Packet
Duration: Charged
Effect: This Spell grants the target the ability to use
any one(1) Skill, which requires the target to be
behind the target, regardless of their position.
Incantation: "I grant you the power of an Ambush."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Ambush 1"
Arcane Shroud
School: Protection
Level: 1
Range: Self
Duration: Concentration and 10 Seconds
Effect: Upon casting this Spell, the Caster must
cross both arms across their chest and can move at
no faster than a walking pace. So long as both these
requirements are met, the Caster is immune to all
Weapon Damage and <Effect>-Strikes. This effect
lasts a maximum of ten(10) seconds.
Incantation: "I grant you the protection of an Arcane
Shroud."
Delivery Verbal: "Lesser Magic Aegis"
Banish
School: Conjuration
Level: 9
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Spell forces the target Extraplanar
creature or Elemental back to its home plane. A
creature which has been affected by this effect
cannot return to the plane from which it was
Banished for twenty-four(24) hours.
This Spell only functions on Hymira and will fail if
cast on any other Plane.
Incantation: "I Conjure a force to Banish."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Banish"
Banshee's Cry
School: Affliction
Level: 9
Range: Circle
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Spell must be cast from within a valid
Circle. Anyone within the Circle is affected by a Magic
Death. The Caster is not Immune to the effects of this
Spell.
Despite this Spell's Range of Circle, it can still
benefit from the Piercing Spellshape.
Incantation: "I inflict the Banshee's Cry upon you."
Delivery Verbal:
"Area of Effect: Circle <Magic Death>"
Barrier
School: Protection
Level: 2
Range: Self
Duration: Indefinite
Effect: This Spell grants its Caster ten(10)
Fortification Points. These points are always the first
lost due to damage. Unless explicitly stated
otherwise, Fortification Points from multiple sources
do not stack.
Incantation: "I grant you the protection of a Barrier."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Fortify 10"
Bind [Binding]
School: Conjuration
Level: 4
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Spell forces the target's arms to their
sides and keeps them there throughout its duration.
The creature cannot use any Skill which requires the
use of their arms such as using weapons or casting
Spells. This Spell is a Binding effect and removed by
Dispel Binding.
Incantation: "I Conjure a Force to Bind you."
Delivery Verbal: "Physical Bind"
Binding Shield
School: Conjuration, Protection
Level: 5
Range: Packet
Duration: Indefinite
Effect: This Spell negates the next Binding effect by
which the target is struck. The following effects are
Binding; Bind, Confine, Pin, and Web.
Incantation: "I grant you the protection of a Binding
Shield."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Binding Shield"
Blood Bolt
School: Affliction
Level: 2
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Spell can be used for one of two(2)
effects. The first causes five(5) points of Piercing
Magic Body damage to the target. The second
causes five(5) points of Piercing Magic Healing to the
target. In either case, the Caster must expend two(2)
Hit Points to cast this Spell.
If this Spell is affected by the Echo Spellshape
effect the caster must expend the Hit Points again for
each time the effect is duplicated.
If this Spell is affected by the Empower Spellshape
effect the caster must instead expend twice the
number of Hit Points the Spell normally requires.
If this Spell is placed in a Potion or Scroll the
Caster must choose which of the two(2) effects are
contained within the crafted item.
Incantation: "I Conjure a Blood Bolt."
Delivery Verbal: "5 Piercing Magic Body" or
"5 Piercing Magic Healing"
Blood Lance
School: Affliction
Level: 7
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Spell can be used for one of two(2)
effects. The first causes thirty(30) points of Piercing
Magic Body damage to the target. The second
causes thirty(30) points of Piercing Magic Healing to
the target. In either case, the Caster must expend
seven(7) Hit Points to cast this Spell.
If this Spell is affected by the Echo Spellshape
effect the caster must expend the Hit Points again for
each time the effect is duplicated.
If this Spell is affected by the Empower Spellshape
effect the caster must instead expend twice the
number of Hit Points the Spell normally requires.
If this Spell is placed in a Potion or Scroll, the
Caster must choose which of the two(2) effects are
contained within the crafted item.
Incantation: "I Conjure a Blood Lance."
Delivery Verbal: "30 Piercing Magic Body" or
"30 Piercing Magic Healing"
Blood Wall
School: Affliction
Level: 8
Range: Packet, Multiple
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Spell can be used for one of two(2)
effects. The first causes twenty(20) points of Piercing
Magic Body damage to the target. The second
causes twenty(20) points of Piercing Magic Healing to
the target. In either case, the Caster must expend
eight(8) Hit Points to cast this Spell. The Caster may
throw any number of packets when using this Spell
and any creature struck by one or more packets is
affected.
If this Spell is affected by the Echo Spellshape
effect the caster must expend the Hit Points again for
each time the effect is duplicated.
If this Spell is affected by the Empower Spellshape
effect the caster must instead expend twice the
number of Hit Points the Spell normally requires.
If this Spell is placed in a Potion or Scroll, the
Caster must choose which of the two(2) effects are
contained within the crafted item.
Incantation: "I Conjure a Blood Wall."
Delivery Verbal: "20 Piercing Magic Body" or
"20 Piercing Magic Healing"
Bone Armour
School: Affliction
Level: 3
Range: Self
Duration: Day
Effect: In order to cast this Spell the Caster must
have costuming depicting bones on their torso. This
Spell grants the Caster five(5) points of Natural
Armour. This effect does not stack with any other
source of Natural Armour.
Incantation: "I grant you the power of Bone Armour."
Delivery Verbal: "Physical Imbue Skill: Natural
Armour 5"

Delivery Verbal: "Physical Imbue Skill: Natural

Bone Armour, Superior
School: Affliction

Range: Self
Duration: Day
Effect: In order to cast this Spell the Caster have

Incantation: "I grant you the power of Superior Bone

Delivery Verbal: "Physical Imbue Skill: Natural
Armour 20"
Break
School: Transmutation
Level: 3
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Spell Breaks the target item. A Broken
item cannot be used until it is Repaired. Certain
creatures, such as Constructs, may be damaged by
this Spell. If the target has multiple items in their
possession which match the description of the item
designated by the Caster it is the target who chooses
which of their items are affected. The target cannot
choose an item which is Indestructible unless it is the
only item they have which matches that description.
Incantation: "I alter this form to be Broken."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Break <Item>"
Calm [Compulsion]
School: Affliction
Level: 4
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Compulsion Spell causes the target to
enter an extremely calm and tranquil state. The target
cannot attack themselves or any other creature
though they will not ignore danger. This effect ends if
the target is attacked.
Incantation: "I Compel you to be Calm."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Calm"
Charm [Compulsion]
School: Affliction
Level: 3
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Compulsion Spell causes the target to
view the caster as a trusted friend listening to their
suggestions and not attacking them. This does not
grant the Caster any control over the target. This
effect ends if the Caster attacks the target.
Incantation: "I Compel you to be Charmed."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Charm"
Clarity
School: Restoration
Level: 7
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Spell instantly grants the target the
benefits of having Meditated. This applies only to
expended Skills which are recovered by Meditating.
Incantation: "I call upon the Dawn to grant you
Clarity."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Clarity"
Cleanse
School: Restoration
Level: 9
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant

School: Transmutation
Level: 6
Range: Touch
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Spell can create any mundane item such
as a weapon or suit of armour. It cannot be used to
create an item with any special properties such as a
silvered weapon or enhanced armour. The player
must provide the physical representation of any item
created by this Spell.
This Spell cannot be used to create any item of
value such as gold, silver, gems or other precious
materials. Nor can it be used to create items with
supernatural qualities such as Ritual Components.
Any attempt to create such items results in worthless
duplicates such as brazen coins, coloured glass or
materials of poor quality.
Incantation: "I alter this form to be Fabricated."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Fabricate <Item>"

School: Affliction
Level: 8
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Spell appears to return a Dead creature
to life, however it only lasts five(5) minutes, after
which the target will again die, restarting their Death
count.
This effect returns the target to life at one(1) Hit
Point. While under the effects of this Spell the target
is Conscious and able to act and use Skills normally.
If this effect is Removed or Dispelled the target
immediately restarts their Death Count. A Life effect
received by the target before this effect ends
removes it without causing the target to die.

Delivery Verbal: "Magic False Vitality"

remains active unless it ends or is Removed. Until it
does, if the target returns to the Caster's line of sight

an attempt to once again break line of sight.

without affecting a new target. No target may benefit
from this effect more than once from a single casting.
This Spell also ends if the Caster casts another Spell
or suffers any effect which would Disrupt Spell
casting including taking damage or entering a state
which would prevent them from casting Spells. The
Caster cannot run while Concentrating.
Incantation: "I grant the Masses the protection of
Fortification."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Fortify 5" … "Magic Fortify
5"

Imprison
School: Protection
Level: 9
Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Spell encases the target in a magical
field preventing them from speaking, moving, being
moved, or using any Skills. While inside the Imprison,
the target is immune to all effects. This effect can be
removed by a Dispel Magic effect. The Caster may
also end the Imprison effect by simply touching the
target and stating "Imprison down". If the Caster is
also the target they may expend one use of the Skill
Harden Magic to negate a single Dispel Magic effect
from removing the Imprison.
The Caster can also use the Imprison to deliver a
Killing Blow, which counts as Magic, upon the target.
To do so the Caster must touch the target and state
"Killing Blow 1, Killing Blow 2, Killing Blow 3, Magic"
Incantation: "I Conjure a Force to Imprison you."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Imprison"

effect, if the target is affected by any damaging effect,
uses any Skill, or moves faster than a normal walking

target is healed of all harmful effects except those
that persist through the target's Death. It is up to the
target's Player to determine which effects are
harmful. This effect ends, without effect, if the target

Effect: This Spell grants the Caster the ability to
deliver, by Touch, a "Magic Remove Drain" effect at
will. This effect removes, from the target, a single
Drain effect. The Caster may continue to deliver this
effect until they go ten(10) seconds without affecting
a new target or the Spell is Disrupted. No target can
benefit from this effect more than once.
Incantation: "I call upon the Dusk to Remove the
Drains of the Masses."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Remove Drain … Magic
Remove Drain"

Safeguard
School: Protection
Level: 7
Range: Container
Duration: Event
Effect: This Spell magically seals the target
Container preventing any creature from accessing the
affected Container. Creatures which were touching
the Container at the time of casting may add or
remove items from inside the Safeguard. This Spell
cannot be Dispelled. Characters can never be stored
inside of a Safeguard. The Container targeted by this
ability must be marked with an "S".
If this Spell is cast upon a container within a
Sanctuary, the Caster may choose to cause the
target container to become unmovable. This Spell
must be included in the Marshal Notes for the Room.
Examples of valid containers for this effect are;
pouches, bags, chests, bottles, jars and even pockets
which have covers. Invalid targets are bowls, cups or
pockets which do not have a top.
Incantation: "I grant you the Protection of a
Safeguard."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Safeguard"
Sanctuary
School: Protection
Level: 9
Range: Room
Duration: Event
Effect: This Spell requires the Caster to touch a
portal into the targeted room and perform a special
five(5) minute ceremony in order for the Spell to have
any effect. Once this Spell takes effect it magically
seals the target Room preventing any creature from
entering, or exiting, the affected area. Creatures
which were inside at the time of casting may enter or
exit the Sanctuary room at will, and can even
"Recognize" others into the area. The Caster may
spend five(5) minutes to grant one or more new
Creatures the ability to pass in and out of the effect
as if they were present at the time of casting. Neither
weapon, nor packet attacks, can pass through the
Sanctuary and those inside are not affected by any
effect originating from outside the Sanctuary. This
Spell cannot be Dispelled. The Room targeted by this
ability must be marked with an "S" at all doorways
into the room. This Spell must be included in the
Marshal Notes for the Room.
Incantation: "I grant you the Protection of a
Sanctuary."
Delivery Verbal: "Magic Sanctuary"

Martial Arts
Performing a Martial Art
Casting a Martial Art requires the Character to
have a packet, or in the case of effects delivered as
Physical-Strike a weapon with which they are
Proficient, in hand as well as have an available
Martial Arts Slot of the level of the Martial Art they
wish to cast, and access to the School of Martial Arts
to which the Martial Art belongs. There are no
Incantations for Martial Arts, only the Delivery Verbal
needs to be spoken. Once the Character states the
Delivery Verbal they have three(3) seconds to deliver
the strike or throw the packet. All Delivery Verbals
must be made in a normal speaking voice and loud
enough to be heard by the target and an imaginary
observer standing next to the target. Whispering
Delivery Verbals is not permitted.
If a Character fumbles the Deliver Verbal, the
Character fails to hit any target with the Packet or
Weapon, the Martial Art is Disrupted, the effect is
Resisted, Warded, or the target is Immune, the
Character may regain it by Meditating. This does not
mean that it is acceptable to intentionally fail the
Delivery Verbal, or deliberately miss a target. It is
meant to allow a Player who has tried, in earnest, to
perform the Martial Art properly but failed, to recover
the wasted Skill.
Touch Casting Martial Arts
Unless they have a Range of "Touch", Martial Arts
cannot be Touch Cast. Martial Arts, which can be
delivered by Touch, can be "Refused" by the target. A
target who has Refused a Touch Casting is never
affected by the effect. An Unconscious Character
cannot Refuse a Touch Cast Martial Art, though they
may accept it allowing it to bypass any protective
effects they may have. When cast on an unwilling
Unconscious Character the target may trigger
protectives as though it were a normal packet
delivered attack. Martial Arts with a Range of Self still
require the Character to have a free hand and be
holding a packet.
Disrupting Martial Arts
A Martial Art can be Disrupted by causing the
Caster to take Hit Point damage between when they
start the Delivery Verbal and when they deliver the
effect. Additionally, causing the Caster to enter a state
where they are unable to use Martial Arts, such as by
causing them to lose access to Skills, or depriving
them of the use of their weapons such as Binding
effects or the Weakness effect which prevents the
use of Martial Arts with a Range of Strike.
Stacking Effects
Unless explicitly stated otherwise, no more than
one effect with the same name can be stacked, which
is to say, a Character can be affected by only one
instance of any effect at a time. Spells, and similar
effects, with the same name, including effects such
as Mirror Image, Greater Mirror Image and Superior
Mirror Image, cannot be stacked either.
Martial Traditions
Martial Traditions represent the manner in which a
Character learned, or practices hurting other
Characters. Effects which renew or recharge Skills,
such as Inspiration, may never be used to increase
the number of times a Character can benefit from the
effects of their Tradition.
The effects of Martial Traditions apply only to
Martial Arts the Character delivers from memory.
Assassin [Tradition, Martial]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: In order to use this benefit of this ability the
Character must be behind their target and able to see
both of their target's shoulder blades. The Character
may double the damage of any single Martial Art
which is delivered as Physical-Strike. This ability is
usable a total of three(3) times, but the Character
must Meditate in between each use. The Character
may, once per Reset, spend a Rest to recover all
uses of this ability for a maximum of six(6) uses per
day.
Berserker [Tradition, Martial]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may Rest in
order to recover three(3) expended Martial Art Slots
of any level(s) they can cast. Additionally, once per
Reset, the Character may grant themselves the effect
of a "Physical Clarity" granting them the benefits of
Meditating instantly.
Combat Archer [Tradition, Martial]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: Whenever the Character delivers a Martial
Art as Physical-Strike using an Archery packet they
may, if the initial packet fails to strike any target, or
the attack is negated by the defensive ability Dodge,
deliver a copy of the effect, including any Skills which
modify the attack, within ten(10) seconds without
using another Martial Arts Slot. If this second packet
fails to hit any target it may be recovered by
Meditation. If the second packet attack does strike a
valid target the Martial Arts Slot may not be recovered
by Meditation. The call for this ability is "Echo
<Delivery Verbal>".
Focused Warrior [Tradition, Martial]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: The Character chooses a single weapon
type; Archery, Axe, Mace, Sword, or Thrown. The
Character loses the ability to use Martial Arts while
not wielding their chosen weapon. The Character
gains one(1) additional Martial Art Slot of each level
they can cast. These additional Martial Arts Slots do
not count towards any Requirements for Skills.
Prepared Warrior [Tradition, Martial]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: After each Reset, the Character may choose
one(1) Martial Art of each level, for which they have
Martial Art Slots, adding them to a list of specially
prepared Arts. The Character may cast each Art on
their list of prepared Arts once without expending a
Martial Art Slot.
Readied Warrior [Tradition, Martial]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: Prior to casting any Martial Art the Character
may spend ten(10)-seconds, during which time they
must plant their feet and role-play "readying" their
weapon. Once this count has been finished their next
Martial Arts used, before moving, is delivered as
Piercing.
Tireless Warrior [Tradition, Martial]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: Once per Reset, the Character may Rest in
order to recover a single expended Martial Arts Slot
for each level they can cast.
Twin Weapons [Tradition, Martial]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: Whenever the Character uses a Martial Art
which is delivered by weapon they may deliver a copy
of the Martial Art within ten(10) seconds of the first.
This second Martial Art must be delivered using a
different weapon than the first. This ability is usable a
total of three(3) times, but the Character must
Meditate in between each use. The Character may,
once per Reset, spend a Rest to recover all uses of
this ability. The call for this ability is "Echo <Delivery
Verbal>".
Unfaltering Champion [Tradition, Martial]
Requirements: Level 1 Martial Arts Slot
Benefit: Whenever the Character performs a Martial
Art they may grant themselves a number of
Fortification Points equal to the level of the expended
Martial Art. This effect must be delivered within
ten(10) seconds of the Martial Art being cast. Unlike
the Magic Tradition Living Magic, multiple Martial Arts
cast in sequence do not increase the number of
Fortification Points granted by this ability, only the
most recent Martial Art performed matters. The call
for this ability is "Physical Fortify <X>".
If the Character has the Skill Bodyguard, they may
expend a use the Parry Skill to negate a single effect,
to which the Skill Parry is applicable, which strikes a
target within the Character's weapon reach.
Additionally, they may, once per Reset, Rest in order
to regain the use of Parry granted by the Bodyguard
Skill for use on their charge.

School: Duelist, Guardian
Level: 2
Range: Strike
Duration: 10 Seconds
Effect: This Artistry forces the target to take ten(10)
steps away from the Caster and prevents them from
approaching for its duration.

worn by the Caster by five(5) points. This ability does
not stack with any other effects which Enhance
Physical Armour.

5"

does not stack with any other effects which Enhance

20"
Awakening Shout
School: Guardian
Level: 4
Range: Packet
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Artistry instantly removes all
Compulsions effects from the target. The following
effects are Compulsions; Calm, Charm, Dominate,
Fear, Shun, Silence, Sleep. This Artistry does not
affect Enthral.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Physical Dispel Compulsions"
Banishing Strike
School: Slayer
Level: 9
Range: Strike
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Artistry forces the target Extraplanar
creature or Elemental back to its home plane. A
creature which has been affected by this effect
cannot return to the plane from which it was
Banished for twenty-four(24) hours.
This Artistry only functions on Hymira and will fail if
cast on any other Plane.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Physical-Strike Banish"
Battle Meditation
School: Duelist, Guardian, Slayer
Level: 7
Range: Self
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Artistry instantly grants the target the
benefits of having Meditated. This applies only to
expended Skills which are recovered by Meditating.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Physical Clarity"
Binding Shot [Binding]
School: Duelist
Level: 4
Range: Strike
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Binding Artistry can be used only with a
Thrown Weapon or the Archery Skill. This effect
forces the target's arms to their sides and keeps them
there throughout its duration. The creature cannot
use any Skill which requires the use of their arms
such as using weapons or casting Spells.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Physical-Strike Bind"

Duration: Instant

against any <Effect>-Strike effect.

This Spell causes ten(10) points of Physical Healing.

Disabling Strike [Curse]
School: Duelist, Slayer
Level: 7
Range: Strike
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Curse Artistry allows the Caster to
deliver a Physical-Strike Cripple effect affecting the
limb of the Caster's choice. A Crippled arm cannot be
used for any Skills and cannot hold any items. A
Crippled leg cannot be used to walk and prevents the
target from moving faster than a slow, limping walking
pace. The target cannot hop. If both legs are Crippled
the target can only kneel in place.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Physical-Strike Cripple <Limb>"

Range: Strike
Duration: 10 Seconds

using a single weapon chosen by the Caster, or using
any Skill requiring the use of the weapon for its
duration. If the target is wielding more than one
weapon which matches the description named by the
Caster it us up to the target which weapon is affected.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Physical-Strike Fumble <Item>"

item designated by the Caster it is the target who

description.

Heroic Stand
School: Guardian
Level: 9
Range: Self
Duration: Sustained
Effect: This Artistry grants the Caster Physical
Resistance. This effect reduces all damaging effects
delivered as Physical(-Strike) by half as well as
reducing the duration of all effects delivered as

if the Character dies, enters a state where they are
unable to use Skills, uses any other Artistry or
Combat ends. Combat is considered to be over when
the Character goes one(1) minute without attacking,
or being attacked by, an enemy.

Resistance"
Intimidating Display [Compulsion]
School: Duelist
Level: 7
Range: Packet, Multiple
Duration: 10 Seconds
Effect: This Compulsion Artistry allows the Caster to
throw any number of packets. Any creature struck by
one or more packets must immediately flee the
Caster's presence attempting to break line of sight
with them. They cannot use any Skills while under
this effect. Once the target has broken line of sight
with the Caster for ten(10) seconds they may again
use their Skills but this effect remains active unless it
ends or is removed. Until it does, if the target returns
to the Caster's line of sight they again lose access to
all Skills and must flee in an attempt to once again
break line of sight.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Lesser Physical Fear"

Range: Packet
Duration: 5 Minutes
Effect: This Compulsion Artistry causes the target to
immediately flee the Caster's presence attempting to
break line of sight with them. They cannot use any
Skills while under this effect. Once the target has
broken line of sight with the Caster for ten(10)
seconds they may again use their Skills but this effect
remains active unless it ends or is removed. Until it
does, if the target returns to the Caster's line of sight
they again lose access to all Skills and must flee in
an attempt to once again break line of sight.
Incantation: None
Delivery Verbal: "Physical Fear"

Chapter 8: Rituals Both Mundane and Magical
Rituals
Rituals are used to create powerful items, or imbue
individuals, with special abilities. These abilities may
be a weapon with increased damage, armour that
can negate Spells and items which anyone can use
to cast stored Alchemy. Additionally, these effects can
be used to grant Characters special powers, respend
Skill Points or even travel to other Planes of
Existence.
Performing Ritual Effects
Any Character with the Ritualist Skill is able to cast
any Ritual Magic effect for which they have the Scroll.
Any Character with the Grand Master Smith Skill is
able to cast any Ritual Smithing effect for which they
have the Schematic.
Any Character with the Alkahestry Skill is able to
cast any Ritual Alchemy effect for which they have
the Formula.
Eschew Components and Tribute Invocation
Other than casting in a Circle of Creation, there are
two abilities which can decrease the cost to perform a
Ritual. The first Eschew Components, a branch of the
Skills; Master Alchemist, Master Ritualist, and Secret
Technique, allow a Character to reduce the
Component cost of a Ritual by one(1) for a particular
Component Type. These abilities apply only once to
each Component Type meaning multiple Casters with
these Skills do not ever deduct more than one(1)
from the Component cost of the Ritual.
The Tribute Invocation can be cast once on a
Ritual for each Component Type. Doing so can
reduce the cost by an additional Component type for
each casting of Tribute but never more than once for
each type.
Finally, casting in a Circle of Creation reduces the
Power cost for all Rituals performed within by one(1).
If multiple Circles are nested this effect does not
stack.
The minimum cost for each Component Type
required by a Ritual is one(1) regardless of Tribute
and Eschew Components.
Definitions
Backlash – This refers to the complete failure of a
Ritual. Not only does the Ritual Fail, but it also
causes harm according to those in the Circle and the
Ritual's Casters. Some Backlashes can even be
Catastrophic. See Page 179
Body – This refers to a normal Character who is not
in an alternate state of existence such as out of
game, or on another plane. These effects end if the
Character dissipates.
Caster – All Characters participating in the Ritual as
either the Primary Caster or a Secondary Caster.
Primary Caster – The Character who is in control of
the Ritual Effect.
Catastrophe – This is an especially harmful
Backlash caused by performing Unstable Rituals.
These are far more harmful to the Casters and those
within the Circle.
Circle – A Circle is a closed loop with a total diameter
of no more than 5 metres and a total circumference of
no more than 16 metres. Circles must be lit, either
because they are in an area which is constantly
illuminated or because the Circle itself is lit.
Component – A Component is an item which is
necessary for casting Rituals. Components have
seven types; Power, Creation, Destruction,
Elemental, Spirit, Time and Void. Components are
expended to pay the Component cost listed on each
Ritual.
Void Components are used by some Rituals but
can also be used in place of any other Component
type in a Ritual. Should the number of Void
Components used meet or exceed one-half the total
number of components needed in the base Ritual
cost, the Ritual becomes Unstable.
In order to Identify components the Character must
possess one(1), or more, of the following Skills;
Alkahestry, Grand Master Smithing, Ritual Magic. A
Character without at least one of these Skills is able
to recognize that it is a Component, but not the type
or Strength. Some Ritual Components require the
Identify effect or knowledge of the specific component
to reveal all of their properties.
Component Voucher – A Component Voucher is a
special item which can be used towards the
Component cost for a single Ritual, or Ritual Concert.
A Component Voucher is treated as if it were a
collection of Strength one(1) Components whose total
value equals that listed on the Component Voucher.
At the end of the Ritual, or Ritual Concert, the
Voucher is consumed regardless of any Component
value remaining on the Voucher. All Component
Vouchers have an expiration date determined by
Logistics.
Concert – Also called a "Batch Casting", this refers to
a series of Rituals which are cast back to back on a
single target. The Primary Caster can change for the
casting of each individual Ritual but all participants in
any one Casting must be present as a Caster for all
casting within the Concert. Should any Ritual in the
Concert Fail or Backlash the Concert immediately
ends. Alternatively the Concert automatically ends if
one of the following events occurs; the Primary
Caster chooses to end the Concert, more than one(1)
minute, per Caster, goes by between Castings, or the
Concert completes the casting of an Extend Ritual
effect.
Fail – A Ritual can fail if one of several events occur,
all of which are described below. A Ritual which Fails
ends without producing its effect but all components
that would have been used for its casting are
consumed.
Flaw – A Ritual can succeed but with an unusual
effect either positive or negative. Flaws only occur in
Rituals which are naturally unstable, include Special
Components which cause it to become unstable, or
the Concert is the target of the Instil Chaos Ritual
effect.
Item – This refers to the Mastercrafted, Legendary or
Mythical Item, which is the target of one or more
Ritual effects. It can be a weapon, trinket, or armour.
All items which contain one or more Ritual effects are
Indestructible and Immune to the effects of Break and
Destroy <Item>.
Legendary Item – These rare Items can hold up to
eight(8) Ritual Effects. These Items can be found in
limited quantities and some Skills can create these
Items but this can only be once per year.
Limited – Certain scrolls are marked as Limited. This
means that their use by players is limited. They
cannot be memorised by Player Characters and
cannot be Copied.
Mastercrafted Item – These Items can hold up to
five(5) Ritual Effects. They are the most common
form of Item and can be found or created by Skills
such as Smithing.
Mythical Item – These incredibly rare Items can hold
up to ten(10) Ritual Effects. These Items can only be
found in the rarest of circumstances and never
created by Skills.
Protective Circle – All two(2) Year Ritual Circles and
Circles created with the Protective Circle Ritual Effect
are magically protected from intrusion. No creature
which is not Invested in the Circle may enter, or exit,
the Circle unless they are first "Recognize" by a
Character is is Invested in the Circle. All those inside
of a Protective Circle are Immune to all effects which
originate from outside the Circle.
Ritual Effect Slots – A Mastercrafted Item has five(5)
Slots for Ritual Effects. A Legendary Item has eight(8)
Slots for Ritual Effects. A Mythical Item has ten(10)
Slots for Ritual Effects. Characters have five(5) Slots
for Ritual Effects. A target may have a number of
Ritual Effects, of any type; Alchemy, Smithing, or
Magic, whose total does not exceed the target's
number of Ritual Effect Slots and attempts to add a
additional Ritual Effect(s) automatically Fail.
Ritual Voucher – This is a special Plot issued
Component set. A Ritual Voucher lists the name of
the Ritual acting as both the Scroll and all
Components needed for a single casting.
Secondary Caster – One or more additional
Characters who are aiding in the casting of a Ritual
effect.
Scroll – This is the general term for any Scroll,
Formula, or Schematic which details and allows for
the casting of the described Ritual effect contained
therein.
Special Component – This refers to a Special
Component which needed to perform certain Ritual
effects. Special Components can be Identified to
discover any Ritual to which they are applicable. For
example, a Component may be an E4, but can also
be used as the Special Component needed to create
a Planar Gate to the plane of Fire. Some Special
Components have special effects which modify the
performance of a Ritual. For example, there is a
component which provides all Power needed for a
Ritual, but causes the Ritual to become Unstable.
Spirit – This refers to the target creature with the
ability to resurrect; such as a Player Character, or an
NPC designated as such by Plot. Simple animals,
mindless creatures, and even some townspeople do
not have a strong enough Spirit to be targeted by
Ritual effects.
Unstable Ritual – Some Rituals are inherently
unstable, or can become unstable with the addition of
Special Components or if the Concert is the target of
the Instil Chaos Ritual effect. Depending on the
cause, unstable Rituals may have an increased
chance to Fail, Flaw or Backlash.
Prior to Casting a Ritual
● Determine the Primary Caster. The Primary
Caster must have access to one or more of
the Schools listed in the description of the
Ritual effect. Please note that Ritual Smithing
and Ritual Alchemy do not have Schools and
any Ritual which lists them as a type can be
cast as such by the Character. Additionally,
the Primary Caster must either know, through
a Skill like Memorise Ritual, or have access
to a Scroll which describes the Ritual to be
cast.
● Determine the effective number of Casters.
The effective number of Casters is
determined by both the number of Characters
with the appropriate Ritual Skill as well as
any Skills which increase the number of
Casters a single Character counts as. For
example, the Primary Caster, a Character
with the Ritual Magic Skill and three(3) Ranks
in the Master Ritualist <Practised Ritualist>
Skill counts as four(4) Casters. One of their
Secondary Caster has Ritual Magic and
one(1) Rank in the Master Ritualist
<Practised Ritualist> Skill counts as two
bringing the total up to six(6). Finally there
are two other Characters, with the Natural
Ritualist Skill, acting as Secondary Casters
bringing the total number of effective Casters
up to eight(8).
● Determine the Components needed for the
Ritual. All Rituals have a base number of
Components, of specific types, which must
be used to cast a Ritual Effect. Additionally,
some Rituals have Special Components
required for their casting. When casting a
Ritual as part of a Ritual Concert, the total
number of Components, including any
Special Components, needed for the Concert
needs to be calculated and included.
While Void can be used in place of any
components, other than Special
Components, if the number of Void
Components exceeds one half the
Components cost of the base Ritual the
Ritual becomes Unstable.
● Find a Marshal, or Ritual Logistics Log
according to local Policy. A Marshal's
approval and/or presence, or pre-established
local policy, is required before casting any
Ritual Effect.
Casting a Ritual
● The Characters determine if the Casting is a
Concert or a single casting. If the Concert is
going to include the Instil Chaos Ritual, it
must be the first Ritual in the Concert. If the
Concert is going to include any form of the
Extend Ritual effect, it must be the last Ritual
in the Concert.
● The Primary Caster must know, via the
Memorise Ritual or similar Skill, or have
access to the Scroll they wish to cast.
● All casting must be performed within a valid
Circle. The Circle must contain props, or
decorations appropriate for the type of Ritual
being performed. For example, Ritual Magic,
typically, uses glyphs, runes, candles and
various other props. Ritual Smithing, typically,
involves a forge, runed anvil, various
blacksmithing tools and flux. Ritual Alchemy,
typically, involves chemistry sets, mortar and
pestles, various flasks and vials and assorted
plants, powders and ingredients.
● All Casters, the Scroll, target(s), and any
Components required for the casting of the
Ritual, or Concert, must be within the Circle
for the entirety of the casting. If any of these
are missing at the start the Ritual/Batch will
not begin.
● Once a Ritual has begun, the target, the
Casters, the Scroll, and all Components
needed for the Casting are locked into the
Circle and are unable to leave it. If any
Components are Destroyed, the Ritual
automatically Fails unless the Circle already
contains sufficient Components to perform
the Ritual.
● Unless otherwise specified on the Scroll, the
casting of a Ritual effect takes five(5)
minutes. This time is reduced by one(1)
minute for each additional Caster
participating in the Ritual to a minimum of
one(1) minute. A single Character who counts
as multiple Casters also reduces the total
time needed to perform the Ritual by one(1)
minute for every Caster they count as beyond
one(1).
● The Ritual will automatically Backlash if any
of the following events happen; any Caster
takes Hit Point damage or enters a state
where they are unable to use Skills, the
Scroll or Circle is Destroyed, or any Caster
chooses to cause the Ritual to Backlash.
● If the Ritual does not Fail, or Backlash, as
described above, the Ritual completes. If the
Ritual is being cast as part of a Concert, the
Casters have one(1) minute per effective
Caster in the Concert to begin the next Ritual
in the Concert. Upon beginning the next
Ritual, return to Step 2.
● At the conclusion of the Ritual, or Concert, all
expended Components and Scrolls are to be
turned over to the Marshal. Any updates to
Character Sheets, Item Tags will be carried
out as soon as possible.
● In the event that a Ritual is targeting a Player
Character owned item or protective effect,
such as a Sanctuary, the Marshal may, at
their discretion, increase the base time
required to perform the Ritual up to one(1)
hour. Any Skills the Caster may possess
which decreases the cast time are still
applicable to the new base time required to
complete the casting.

P4 C1 S1 T1 + P1 Component per target
Table 8.1 – Ritual List
Ritual Description
Ritual Name [Ritual]* *Rituals marked with a "*"
can be cast on a target more than once.
Cost: Component Cost to Perform the Ritual
Type: The Type of Ritual. Alchemy, Magic, or
Smithing.
School: The School of Magic the Caster must know if
this is a Ritual Magic Effect. If multiple schools are
listed the Caster only needs access to one.
Target: List of Valid Targets which can be chosen for
the Ritual Effect.
Duration: The base duration of the Ritual Effect.
Effect: What effect the Ritual has when cast.
Annihilation [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: P4 D3 S3 + Special
Type: Magic
School: Affliction
Target: Any Spirit
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Ritual instantly slays the target and
causes them to receive two(2) additional deaths. The
effects of this Ritual are negated if the target receives
a Life Spell before Dissipating.
This Ritual is always Unstable, takes one
hour to cast and has a 10% Chance to Succeed, a
60% Chance to Flaw, a 20% Chance to Fail, and a
10% chance to Backlash. If this Ritual Backlashes,
roll on the Catastrophe table.
Blood Drinker [Ritual]*
Cost: P2 D2 S2
Type: Magic, Smithing
School: Affliction, Transmutation
Target: Any Sword
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target weapon with the
following ability. Once per Reset, the wielder of this
Weapon may use it to deliver an attack causing "20
Physical-Strike Body".
Channel Poison [Ritual]*
Cost: P4 C2 E2 S2 T1
Type: Alchemy
School: None
Target: Any Weapon
Duration: Event
Effect: At the time of this Ritual's casting the Caster
chooses a single Alchemy, they can cast, with a
range of Packet. Whenever the wielder casts the
chosen Alchemy they may use this Weapon to deliver
the effect as <Effect>-Strike. The Delivery Type of the
attack changes to match that of the Channelled
Alchemy.
Channel Spell [Ritual]*
Cost: P4 C2 E2 S2 T1
Type: Magic
School: Any
Target: Any Weapon
Duration: Event
Effect: At the time of this Ritual's casting the Caster
chooses a single Spell , they can cast, with a range of
Packet. This Ritual imbues the target weapon with
the following ability. Whenever the wielder casts the
chosen Spell they may alter the Delivery Type to
<Effect>-Strike. The Delivery Type of the attack
changes to match that of the Channelled Spell.
Cleaving Weapon [Ritual]*
Cost: P3 D2 T2
Type: Smithing
School: None
Target: Any Axe
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target weapon with the
following ability. Once per Reset, the wielder of this
Weapon may use it to Echo a single Martial Art which
they have just Cast from memory. The Echoed Martial
Art includes all the same options, modifications,
including effects from Katas and Martial Traditions,
which were applied to the original Martial Art. The
decision to use this ability must be made within
ten(10) seconds of the original being delivered. The
call for this ability is "Echo <Delivery Verbal>".
Corruption Bane [Ritual]*
Cost: P4 C3 S3
Type: Smithing
School: None
Target: Silver Mace
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target weapon with the
following ability. Once per Reset, the wielder of this
Weapon may use it to deliver a "Physical-Strike
Vanquish Abomination".
Create Alchemical Fountain [Ritual]*
Cost: P5 C2 E2 S2 T2
Type: Alchemy
School: None
Target: Any Circle
Duration: Two(2) Years
Effect: This Ritual transforms the target Circle into a
Protective Circle of one of the following types;
Creation: The Power Component cost of any Ritual
Alchemy cast in this Circle is reduced by one(1) to a
minimum of one(1).
Knowledge: Anyone invested may Identify any
Alchemy effect within the Circle.
Vorpal Font: Anyone invested may spend a three(3)-
count to grant themselves the effects of a "Poison
Vorpal 5".
Anyone in the Circle for the casting gains an
Investiture into the Circle allowing them to enter or
leave at will as well as allowing others to do so. Any
creature inside of the Circle is Immune to all effects
which originate from outside it.
Multiple castings of this Ritual on the same Circle
can be done. Each additional casting grants the
target Circle one additional functions outlined above
and grants the Circle a Ward against the next Break
or Destroy Magic effect by which it is targeted. These
Wards are replenished at the end of the Day.
This Ritual cannot target a Circle under the effects
of the Ritual Create Forge of Kings, or Create Magic
Circle.
Create Bane [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: Varies
Type: Magic
School: Affliction
Target: Any Spirit
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual creates a Bane of the type
specified by the Scroll. When used to deliver a Killing
Blow, the Bane causes the creature to immediately
die and seek Resurrection. Banes have additional
effects based on the Scroll.
Eg. Bane of Vampires: 1/Ever Arcane Solidify
Create Forge of Kings [Ritual]*
Cost: P5 C2 E2 S2 T2
Type: Smithing
School: None
Target: Any Circle
Duration: Two(2) Years
Effect: This Ritual transforms the target Circle into a
Protective Circle of one of the following types;
Creation: The Power Component cost of any Ritual
Smithing cast in this Circle is reduced by one(1) to a
minimum of one(1).
Honing: Any Character who is invested in the Circle
may spend one(1) minute to sharpen a single
weapon granting them the effects of the Invocation
Hone Weapon.
Knowledge: Anyone invested may Identify any
Smithing effect within the Circle.
Anyone in the Circle for the casting gains an
Investiture into the Circle allowing them to enter or
leave at will as well as allowing others to do so. Any
creature inside of the Circle is Immune to all effects
which originate from outside it.
Multiple castings of this Ritual on the same Circle
can be done. Each additional casting grants the
target Circle one additional functions outlined above
and grants the Circle a Ward against the next Break
or Destroy Magic effect by which it is targeted. These
Wards are replenished at the end of the Day.
This Ritual cannot target a Circle under the effects
of the Ritual Create Alchemical Fountain, or Create
Magic Circle.
Create Golem [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: Varies
Type: Magic, Smithing
Target: Any Spirit
School: Protection, Transmutation
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual creates a Golem of the type
specified by the Scroll.
The Golem is bound to perform a single Service
and a number of additional Services based on the
number of Casters participating in the casting. For
every five(5) Casters participating, rounded down, the
number of services may be increased by one(1) to a
maximum of five(5) total Services.
As Golems are mindless, they are only able to
perform simple Services such as "Defend me,",
"Follow me and search every creature I kill." or
"Guard this area.". Invalid Services are ones which
require Sentience such as "Travel to the town of
Glenn and count how many foreign soldiers are
there.". The maximum time a Service can take is
one(1) hour.
Create Homunculus [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: Varies
Type: Alchemy
School: None
Target: None
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual creates a creature of the type
specified by the Scroll to perform one(1) or more
services for the Primary Caster.
The power and number of services performed by
the creature is determined by the number of Casters
participating in the casting. For every three(3)
Casters participating, rounded down, the power or
number of services may be increased by one(1) to a
maximum of five(5) in either category. For example
six Casters could increase the power to two(2) and
the number of services to two(2). Alternatively they
could increase the power to three(3) and have only
the one(1) service provided by the base effect.
A Service is a single finite task. The maximum time
a Service can take is one(1) hour. Examples of valid
Services are; "Follow and protect me on this
adventure.", "Go to the town of Exampleton and tell
me how many soldiers are there.", "Aid me with the
casting of this Ritual.". Examples of invalid Services
are; "Guard this area forever.", "Stay in this Circle and
help us cast all our Rituals.", "Explore all the camps
of the foreign military and bring me a list of all
commanders.".
Create Magic Circle [Ritual]*
Cost: P5 C2 E2 S2 T2
Type: Magic
School: Any School of Magic
Target: Any Circle
Duration: Two(2) Years
Effect: This Ritual transforms the target Circle into a
Protective Circle of one of the following types;
Binding: The number of Services provided by any
creature Summoned into this Circle is increased by
one(1).
Creation: The Power Component cost of any Ritual
Magic cast in this Circle is reduced by one(1) to a
minimum of one(1).
Knowledge: Anyone invested may Identify any
magical effect within the Circle.
Resurrection: Anyone invested may Resurrect a
dead character whose spirit has travelled to the
Circle.
Anyone in the Circle for the casting gains an
Investiture into the Circle allowing them to enter or
leave at will as well as allowing others to do so. Any
creature inside of the Circle is Immune to all effects
which originate from outside it.
Multiple castings of this Ritual on the same Circle
can be done. Each additional casting grants the
target Circle one additional functions outlined above
and grants the Circle a Ward against the next Break
or Destroy Magic effect by which it is targeted. These
Wards are replenished at the end of the Day.
This Ritual cannot target a Circle under the effects
of the Ritual Create Alchemical Fountain, or Create
Forge of Kings.
Create Undead [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: Varies
Type: Magic
School: Affliction, Transmutation
Target: Any Spirit
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual creates an Undead of the type
specified by the Scroll.
Mindless Undead are bound to perform a single
Service and a number of additional Services based
on the number of Casters participating in the casting.
For every three(3) Casters participating, rounded
down, the number of services may be increased by
one(1) to a maximum of five(5) total Services.
Mindless Undead are only able to perform simple
Services such as "Defend me,", "Follow me and
search every creature I kill." or "Guard this area.".
Invalid Services are ones which require Sentience
such as "Travel to the town of Glenn and count how
many foreign soldiers are there.". The maximum time
a Service can take is one(1) hour.
Intelligent Undead are bound to perform a single
Service and a number of additional Services based
on the number of Casters participating in the casting.
For every five(5) Casters participating, rounded
down, the number of services may be increased by
one(1) to a maximum of five(5) total Services.
A Service is a single finite task. The maximum time
a Service can take is one(1) hour. Examples of valid
Services are; "Follow and protect me on this
adventure.", "Go to the town of Exampleton and tell
me how many soldiers are there.", "Aid me with the
casting of this Ritual.". Examples of invalid Services
are; "Guard this area forever.", "Stay in this Circle and
help us cast all our Rituals.", "Explore all the camps
of the foreign military and bring me a list of all
commanders.".
Crushing Blow [Ritual]*
Cost: P2 C1 D1
Type: Magic, Smithing
School: Affliction, Conjuration, Transmutation
Target: Any Mace
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target weapon with the
following ability. Once per Reset, the wielder of this
Weapon may use it to deliver an attack causing "20
Physical-Strike Force".
Delve History [Ritual]
Cost: P3 S2 T2
Type: Alchemy, Magic, Smithing
School: Conjuration, Transmutation
Target: Item
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Ritual reveals, to the casters, details
about the past of the target item. The nature and
scope of the information is determined by Plot.
Destroy Magic [Ritual]
Cost: P4 D2 T2
Type: Magic
School: Affliction, Transmutation
Target: Magical Effect, Item or Creature
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Ritual Destroys all magic, including
Ritual Magic, in effect upon the target.
Duplicate Scroll [Ritual]
Cost: P2 C1 E1 T1 + See Text
Type: Alchemy, Magic, Smithing
School: All (See Text)
Target: Any Ritual Scroll
Duration: One(1) Year
Effect: This Ritual effect allows the Caster to create a
duplicate of the target Ritual Scroll. As an additional
cost to cast this Ritual the Caster must include
additional components equal to the full Component
cost of the Ritual effect being copied. Skills and
effects which reduce the number of components
needed to cast a Ritual cannot reduce the number of
Components needed for this additional cost.
When performed as a Ritual Magic effect this
Ritual must target a Ritual Magic Scroll and creates a
Ritual Magic Scroll. When cast as a Ritual Alchemy
effect it targets and creates a Ritual Alchemy
Formula. Ritual Smithing targets and creates a Ritual
Smithing Schematic.
Echoing Quiver [Ritual]*
Cost: P2 C2 T1
Type: Magic, Smithing
School: Transmutation
Target: Any Quiver
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target Quiver with the
following ability. Once per Reset, the wielder may
Echo a single Martial Art delivered by one, or more,
Archery Packet(s). The Call for this ability is "Echo
<Delivery Verbal>"
Elemental Weapon [Ritual]*
Cost: P2 C1 E2
Type: Alchemy, Magic, Smithing
School: Conjuration
Target: Any Weapon
Duration: Event
Effect: As part of Casting this Ritual the caster
chooses one of the following Damage Types; Chaos,
Destruction, Dream, Essence, Fire, Ice, Light,
Lightning, Order, Reason, Shadow, or Stone. The
wielder of the target Weapon may use the chosen
Damage Type in addition to the Weapon's normal
Weapon Type. Additionally, when using an <Effect>-
Strike which uses the Weapon's Weapon Type, they
may instead replace the Weapon Type with the
Chosen Damage Type.
Elixir of Lies [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: P2 C1 D1 S2
Type: Alchemy
School: None
Target: None
Duration: Event
Effect: This effect creates a special elixir which
renders its imbiber into a brief, but highly suggestible
state. Once consumed, the effects of this Ritual take
effect on the imbiber as follows; The target
immediately forgets all events which have occurred in
the last hour. The Caster may describe, in ten(10)
words or fewer, an alternate memory. If the Caster
describes an alternate memory the target will believe
it completely. If this effect is not Destroyed by the end
of the Event, the memories become permanent.
The Elixir created by this Ritual expires at the end
of the Event in which it was cast.
Enhance Armour [Ritual]*
Cost: P2 C1 S1
Type: Magic, Smithing
School: Protection, Transmutation
Target: Any Physical Armour
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual increases the Armour Points
granted by the target suit of Physical Armour by
two(2) for each casting. For every ten(10) additional
points of armour, or portion thereof, this effect counts
as one(1) effect towards the item's Ritual Effect Limit.
Enhance Weapon [Ritual]*
Cost: P3 C1 D2 E1
Type: Magic, Smithing
School: Affliction, Conjuration, Transmutation
Target: Any Weapon
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual increases the base damage of the
target weapon by one(1). Additional castings of this
Ritual Effect in the same Concert increase the
damage according to the following table.
Castings Damage Bonus Ritual Slots
1 +1 1
3 +2 2
6 +3 3
10 +4 4
15 +5 5
Exorcism [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: P2 S2 T1 + Special
Type: Magic
School: Affliction, Protection, Restoration
Target: Any Room or Structure
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual instantly removes any Spirits from
the area and prevents any from entering the affected
area for its duration.
Extend Ritual [Ritual]
Cost: P2 T2
Type: Alchemy, Magic, Smithing
School: All
Target: Ritual Concert
Duration: One(1) Year
Effect: When cast as the last Ritual in a Ritual
Concert, this Ritual extends the duration of all valid
Ritual effects in the current target Ritual Concert to
one(1) year. This effect does not count towards the
number of Ritual Effects on an Item.
Extend Ritual, Greater [Ritual]
Cost: P4 C2 T2 (Requires one P2 or P4)
Type: Alchemy, Magic, Smithing
School: All
Target: Any Ritual Effect or Concert.
Duration: Two(2) Years
Effect: When cast as the last Ritual in a Ritual
Concert, this Ritual extends the duration of all valid
Ritual effects in the current target Ritual Concert to
two(2) years. This effect consumes one(1) of the
item's Ritual effect slots.
Extend Ritual, Superior [Ritual]
Cost: P6 C4 T6 + Special
Type: Alchemy, Magic, Smithing
School: All
Target: Any Ritual Effect or Concert.
Duration: Five(5) Years
Effect: When cast as the last Ritual in a Ritual
Concert, this Ritual extends the duration of all valid
Ritual effects in the current target Ritual Concert to
five(5) years. This effect consumes two(2) of the
Item's Ritual effect slots. This effect also requires a
Special Component to be included.
Funerary Rites [Ritual]
Cost: P1 S1
Type: Magic
School: Affliction, Restoration
Target: Any Permanently Dead Creature
Duration: Up to 5 Minutes
Effect: This Ritual allows the Caster to summon the
spirit of a permanently dead creature to the Circle.
The maximum duration of this Ritual is five(5)
minutes. The maximum number of years which can
have elapsed since the target's death is ten(10)
years. Each additional caster doubles the maximum
number of years in this range.
Grounded Armour [Ritual]*
Cost: P2 C1 E2
Type: Smithing
School: Smithing
Target: Iron Armour or Iron Shield
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target armour with the
following ability. Once per Reset, the Item can negate
a single effect delivered as Magic(-Strike). The Call
for this ability is "Ward".
Imbue Alchemy [Ritual]*
Cost: P5 C2 E2 + See Text
Type: Alchemy
School: None
Target: Any Item or Spirit
Duration: Event
Effect: As part of casting this Ritual the Caster
chooses one(1) Alchemy they can cast from memory.
This Ritual requires an amount of additional Power
equal to one-third (1/3) the level of the Alchemy to be
stored, rounded up. This Ritual imbues the ability to
cast the chosen Alchemy into the target. The Caster
cannot use their own Skills to modify the Alchemy in
any way. The Character who is attuned to the item, or
is the target of this effect, may cast the imbued
Alchemy once per Reset. The call to use an Alchemy
which is imbued is "Activate <Delivery Verbal>". A
Character cannot use their own Skills to modify an
Alchemy which is cast using this effect.
Imbue Skill [Ritual]*
Cost: P3 C2 S2 + See Text
Type: Magic
School: Transmutation
Target: Any Item or Spirit
Duration: Event
Effect: As part of casting this Ritual the Caster
chooses one Skill they possess. Only Skills which are
usable "Once per Reset" can be Imbued. Alchemy
Slots, Martial Arts Slots, Spell Slots, and Heritage
Skills cannot be Imbued by this Ritual Effect. The
same Skill can never be Imbued into a target more
than once.
The following Skills may be Imbued into an Item
but require the Character to have at least one(1)
Rank in the Skill in order to use the Imbued Skill.
These Skills include; Create Cauldron, Create Flask,
Create Potion, Create Scroll, Create Wand,
Defensive Roll, Rune Carving, and Smithing.
This Ritual requires an additional number of Power
Components equal to one half the Skill Point cost, at
Priority A, of the Skill to be stored rounded up. This
Ritual imbues the ability to use the chosen Skill into
the target. The Character who is attuned to the item,
or is the target of this effect, may use the imbued skill
once per Reset. The call to use a Skill which is
imbued by this effect is "Activate <Skill Verbal>".
Imbue Spell [Ritual]*
Cost: P2 C2 E2 + See Text
Type: Magic
School: Any (See Text)
Target: Any Item or Spirit
Duration: Event
Effect: As part of casting this Ritual the Caster
chooses one Spell they can cast, with a Range other
than Self, from memory. This Ritual requires an
amount of additional Power equal to one third the
level of the Spell to be stored rounded up. This Ritual
imbues the ability to cast the chosen Spell into the
target. The Caster cannot use their own Skills to
modify the Spell in any way. The Character who is
attuned to the item, or is the target of this effect, may
cast the Imbued Spell once per Reset. The call to use
a Spell which is Imbued by this effect "Activate
<Incantation> <Delivery Verbal>". A Character cannot
use their own Skills to modify a Spell which is cast
using an item.
Instil Chaos [Ritual]
Cost: P1 C1 E1 S1 T1
Type: Alchemy, Magic, Smithing
School: Any
Target: Any Ritual Concert
Duration: Instant (See Text)
Effect: This Ritual can only target a Ritual Concert of
the appropriate type and must be the first Ritual
performed in the Concert. This Ritual allows the
Primary Caster to roll on the Random Flaw Table. For
each additional Caster after the first, the Primary
Caster gains one(1) charge of Chaos.
After each Ritual in the Concert is cast, the Primary
Caster rolls on the Flaw Table. They then have the
option to expend one(1) or more Chaos to modify the
result, increasing it, or decreasing it, by an equal
amount. The Primary Caster is able to view the Flaw
Table and their expected result.
This Ritual cannot be cast more than once on a
single Concert. Additionally, the Chaos available does
not reset after each Ritual Casting. For example, The
Ritual Concert begins with ten(10) Chaos. The
Primary Caster rolls on the Flaw Table and chooses
to change their result by three(3). Their amount of
Chaos available for the rest of the Ritual Concert is
now seven(7).
Each Void Component used in the casting of a
Ritual Concert targeted by this Ritual increases the
amount of Chaos in the Ritual by one(1).
Magic Weapon [Ritual]
Cost: P2 D2
Type: Magic, Smithing
School: Any
Target: Any Weapon
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target weapon with the
following ability. Whenever a Weapon with this Ritual
effect is used to deliver a Killing Blow, the wielder
may deliver the Killing Blow as Magic allowing them
to kill certain powerful creatures requiring magic
weapons to kill.
Modify Memory [Ritual, Limited]*
Cost: P2 C2 D1 S1
Type: Alchemy, Magic
School: Affliction
Target: Spirit
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual allows the Caster to modify a
specific memory of the target. While this Ritual can,
with permission of the target's Player, affect a more
general memory, it normally is limited to a single hour
which has occurred on the same Day in which this
Ritual is cast. The Caster can choose one of the
following three effects;
Amnesia: The target forgets the target memory.
Modified: The target remembers the memory slightly
differently with persons or events being as described
by the Caster. In this case the Caster must describe
the altered memory in thirty(30) words or fewer.
Fabricated: The memory is completely different. In
this case the Caster must describe the new memory
in twenty(20) words or fewer.
This effect becomes permanent if it is not
Destroyed before the end of the Event during which it
was cast.
Mortal Strike [Ritual]*
Cost: P4 D2 S2
Type: Smithing
School: None
Target: Adamantine Sword
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target weapon with the
following ability. Once per Reset, the wielder of this
Weapon may use it to deliver an attack causing
"Physical-Strike Frailty".
Neutralize [Ritual]
Cost: P4 D2 T2
Type: Alchemy
School: None
Duration: Instant
Target: Ritual Alchemical Effect, Item or Creature
Effect: This Ritual Destroys all Ritual Alchemy in
effect on the target as well as removing all effects
delivered as Poison(-Strike) from the target.
Penetrating Quiver [Ritual]*
Cost: P2 D2 T1
Type: Magic, Smithing
School: Transmutation
Target: Adamantine Quiver
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target weapon with the
following ability. Once per Reset, the wielder of this
weapon may use it to deliver a single Martial Art
delivered by one, or more, Archery Packet(s), as
Piercing.
Phalanx Guard [Ritual]*
Cost: P3 C2 T2 + See Text
Type: Magic, Smithing
School: Protection, Transmutation
Target: Any Shield
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual imbues the target Shield with the
ability to, once per Reset, negate a single effect
Delivered as <Effect>-Strike which strikes the
Wielder. The call for this effect is "Activate Parry".
This Ritual requires a number of additional Power
Components based on the size of the Shield. A
Gauntlet or Buckler requires one(1) additional Power,
a Small Shield requires two(2) additional Power while
a large Shield requires three(3) additional Power. If
this Ritual is performed as a Smithing Ritual the
Smith may substitute a Special Material Ingot;
Adamantine, Iron or Silver, in place of the additional
Power. For example if constructing a Small Shield the
Smith could expend an Adamantine and Silver ingot
in order to pay the addition two(2) Power needed to
perform this Ritual on the target.

Planar Attunement [Ritual]*
Cost: P1 C1 + P1 per additional target
Type: Alchemy, Magic
School: Conjuration, Protection, Transmutation
Target: The Caster and One additional target per
additional Power Component expended.
Duration: Event [Cannot be Extended]
Effect: This Ritual grants all targets Immunity to the
harmful passive planar effects of the chosen plane.
This effect cannot be extended and expires at the
end of the Event. The maximum number of targets of
this Ritual is equal to the twice number of Casters
participating in the Ritual's casting.
Planar Gate [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: P4 C3 E3 S3 T3 + Special
Type: Magic
School: Conjuration, Protection, Transmutation
Target: Circle
Duration: See Text
Effect: This Ritual causes the target Circle to
become a gate to the target plane. This gate remains
open for one(1) hour per Caster but can be closed
early by any Caster touching it. Any creature
travelling through the gate gains Planar Attunement
to the plane they travel to.
Planar Rift [Ritual]
Cost: P4 C2 E2 S2 + P1 per additional target
Type: Magic
School: Conjuration, Protection, Transmutation
Target: The Caster and One additional target per
additional power Component expended.
Duration: See Text
Effect: This Ritual causes the targets to be
transported to the target Extra-Plane for up to one(1)
hour per Caster. The exact duration is chosen at the
time of Casting the Ritual. Additionally, all targets gain
the effects of Planar Attunement to the target plane.
The maximum number of targets of this Ritual is
equal to twice the number of Casters participating in
the Ritual's casting.
Casting this Ritual on a target under its effects
causes the Ritual to immediately end returning the
target to its home plane.
Poison Reserve [Ritual]*

Recover Materials [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: P1 C1 D1 E1 S1 T1
Type: Alchemy, Magic, Smithing
School: Transmutation
Target: Magical Effect or Item
Duration: Instant
Effect: This Ritual Destroys the magic upon the
target effect refunding a prorated portion, rounded
down, of the components used to create it. The
amount refunded is based on the total number of
components, divided by the duration, multiplied by
the number of months left. For example, an item
which required P12, C8, E10, S8, and T6 to create,
with one year left of its two year duration would
refund P6, C4, E5, S4, and T6. If the same item had
only four months left it would refund P2, C1, E1, S1
and T1. The refunded components are issues in the
form of a Component Voucher with the same expiry
date as the original item.

Type: Magic
School: Affliction, Conjuration, Restoration
Target: Spirit
Duration: Two(2) Years
Effect: This Ritual empowers the target with a

Arcane Body damage for each additional Caster. This
ability can be triggered once per Reset for every
five(5) Casters, rounded up, who participated in its

unless it is more powerful than the previous one.

Cost: P1 S1 T1
Type: Alchemy, Magic, Smithing
School: Restoration, Transmutation
Target: Any Spirit
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual causes the target's Natural

target, any Natural Weapon wielded by the target in
their off-hand instead counts as either an Axe, Sword,
or Blunt Weapon. Once this Ritual has been
performed on the target they may benefit from Ritual
effects which target that particular type of weapon.
Without this Ritual effect a target is unable to benefit
from the effects of any Rituals which target a specific
type of weapon, such as Blood Drinker, but may still
benefit from Rituals which target any type of weapon
such as Enhance Weapon.

School: Transmutation
Target: Item and Spirit
Duration: Event

five(75)% red. Spiritbound items can be Conjured to

access to a valid Physical Representation. A hold

though it is acceptable for a Player to return the item
directly to its owner.

them, but an Item cannot be Spiritbound to multiple
Spirits.

Summon Creature [Ritual, Limited]
Cost: Varies
Type: Magic
School: Conjuration
Target: Any Spirit
Duration: Event
Effect: This Ritual summons a creature of the type
specified by the Scroll to perform one(1) or more
services for the Primary Caster.
The power and number of services performed by
the creature is determined by the number of Casters
participating in the casting. For every three(3)
Casters participating, rounded down, the power or
number of services may be increased by one(1) to a
maximum of five(5) in either category. For example
six Casters could increase the power to two(2) and
the number of services to two(2). Alternatively they
could increase the power to three(3) and have only
the one(1) service provided by the base effect.
A Service is a single finite task. The maximum time
a Service can take is one(1) hour. Examples of valid
Services are; "Follow and protect me on this
adventure.", "Go to the town of Exampleton and tell
me how many soldiers are there.", "Aid me with the
casting of this Ritual.". Examples of invalid Services
are; "Guard this area forever.", "Stay in this Circle and
help us cast all our Rituals.", "Explore all the camps
of the foreign military and bring me a list of all
commanders.".

Cost: P4 C1 S1 T1 + P1 per additional target
Type: Magic

has been triggered.
Flaws and Backlashes
Certain Rituals do not always function
perfectly. Either because they are inherently unstable
or because they have been made unstable due to the
casting of the Instil Chaos Ritual or the inclusion of
Special Components which cause this effect.
Whatever the cause they all have the same
behaviours.
Backlash
This occurs when the Ritual not only fails, but does
so violently. This can be caused by any of the Ritual's
Casters taking Hit Point damage, entering a state
where they are unable to use Skills, such as
becoming Drained or Unconscious, or chooses to
cause the Ritual to Backlash.
There are two standard effects which occur
whenever a Ritual Backlashes. The first is that
everyone in the Circle takes an amount of Arcane
Body damage equal to the total number of
Components in the Ritual times ten(10). The second
is that all Casters are affected by an Arcane Drain.
Flaws
Flaws occur when something unexpected happens
in the casting of a Ritual. Not all are negative, some
can even result in beneficial effects which are
normally impossible to obtain. There are roughly an
equal number of good flaws to negative flaws making
it tempting for some Casters to take the risk.
Catastrophes
In rare cases, the Backlash of a Ritual may result
in a Catastrophe. These are effects which are far
more harmful than the standard Backlash. In the
event of a Catastrophe a Hold must be called and the
Primary Caster rolls on the Catastrophe Table.
The effects of all Catastrophes are considered to
be delivered as Arcane and as such they cannot be
avoided in anyway even by Immunity.
Chaos

For each Caster who participates in the Instil
Chaos Ritual effect, Characters who count as more
than one(1) Caster due to Skills such as Master
Ritualist <Practised Ritualist> still count as additional
Casters. Additionally, some Special Components also
increase the amount of Chaos available.
Once the initial value of Chaos has been
determined the Primary Caster of the Instil Chaos
Ritual has sole decision making authority as to how it
is spent. They are also the one who rolls on the Flaw
Table to determine the result.
There is one rare exception which can increase the
amount of Chaos available in the Ritual. When a
Special Component which increases Chaos is used in
the Casting of a Ritual which is part of the Concert,
the additional Chaos is available for that roll, and only
that roll. Any remaining Chaos originating from the
Special Component is lost.
General Flaw Table
Die
Result
(d%) Flaw

3 No Creation Components are Consumed in Current Ritual

6 No Spirit Components are Consumed in Current Ritual
7 No Time Components are Consumed in Current Ritual
8 No Void Components are Consumed in Current Ritual

Total Components Needed is Decreased by one(1) of each type.
Applies to Current Ritual only. Min Zero(0)

Table 8.4 – Ritual Catastrophe List
Invocations
Invocations are powerful abilities that those who
have learned to perform Rituals are able to perform.
In order to perform an Invocation the Character must
have a Focus. A Focus is a book, scroll, or similar
object measuring at least 10cm by 15cm which the
Character references as part of performing the
Invocation. A Character must attune to their focus by
performing a special five(5) minute ceremony to do
so. Once attuned the Character remains attuned to
the Focus until the current Event ends, or they attune
to another Focus.
A Character is able to perform an Invocation of any
Type for which they have the appropriate Ritual skill.
A Character with Ritual Magic can perform any Magic
Invocation. All Invocations require a special sixty(60)-
second count to activate during which time the
Character references their Focus. An interrupted
count does not expend a use. A Character is only
able to perform a limited number of Invocations each
Reset. Generally speaking, any Skill which causes a
Character to count as an additional Caster for a
Ritual effect also increases the number of Invocations
a Character can cast each Reset.
No Character may have more than one copy of any
Invocation at a time, even if they cast it using a
different method such as Smithing and Alchemy. Any
casting of an Invocation, the Character is already
under the effect of, removes and replaces the old one
with the new one.
The effects of any active Invocation ends if the
Character enters their Death Count, they lose
possession of their Focus or they are subjected to an
appropriate Destroy <Alchemy/Magic/Smithing>
effect.
A Character may have no more than five(5)
Invocations active at any time. Invocations whose
effects are instant, such as Identify, do not count
against this maximum. Should the Character perform
a sixth Invocation they choose which of their active
Invocations is replaced.
Name [Invocation]
Type: The Type of Ritual Skill needed
Duration: The effect's duration
Effects: The effects of the Invocation.
Augmentation Tomes
Augmentation Tomes are special items which can
be used by any Character who has the ability to
perform the Invocation of their type. A Character with
the ability to perform Magic Invocations can use any
Augmentation Tome whose Type is specified as
Magic, Greater Magic, or Superior Magic.
These Tomes may be found as treasure or created
with Limited Ritual Effects. All Augmentation Tomes
are Indestructible. A Character can attune to, and
benefit from, no more than one(1) Augmentation
Tome per Reset even if they can perform Invocations
of multiple types.
