Table 8.5 – Invocation List
Invocation Descriptions
Adamantine Gilding [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge to
negate a single Break or Destroy effect by which they
are struck. The call for this effect is "Ward".
Alchemical Agent [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy
Duration: Charged (5)
Effects: Upon casting this Invocation the Caster
chooses one of the following elements; Acid, Fire,
Ice, Lightning, or Stone. This Invocation grants the
Character five(5) Charges. The Character may spend
one(1) Charge to deliver a packet attack causing
twenty(20) points of Physical damage of the chosen
element.
Alchemical Burst [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy
Duration: Charged
Effects: This Invocation grants the Character
three(3) Charges. The Character may expend one(1)
Charge to throw any number of packets, with one
hand, to deliver any Alchemy with a Range of Packet.
Alchemical Counteragent [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy
Duration: Charged (3)
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge to
negate a single effect delivered as Poison(-Strike) by
which they are struck. The call for this effect is "Ward"
Arcane Mantle [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation increases the Armour Points,
granted by any Arcane Armour, worn by the
Character by ten(10). This effect stacks with all other
effects which Reinforce Arcane Armour.
Channel Technique [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy, Magic
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge
when casting a Technique with a Range of Packet to
instead deliver the Technique as an <Effect>-Strike
with any weapon with which the Character is
Proficient.
Circle of Life [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Sustained
Effects: This Invocation requires the Character to be
inside of a valid Circle. Once cast, this Invocation
allows the Caster to Resurrect Characters as though
they were a Magic Circle of Resurrection.
Displacement [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Charged
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge to
negate a single effect delivered by Weapon or
Packet. The Call for this ability is "Dodge".
Elemental Portal [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Concentration
Effects: Upon activating this Invocation the
Character chooses one of the following elements;
Chaos, Destruction, Dream, Essence, Fire, Ice, Light,
Lightning, Order, Reason, Shadow, or Stone. The
Character may deliver, by packet, an attack causing
ten(10) points of Elemental Damage of the chosen
type at will.
The effects of this Invocation last until the
Character uses any other Skill, takes Hit Point
damage, is subjected to any effect which would
Disrupt the casting of a Spell, or they move any faster
than a normal walking pace.
Elementium Gilding [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge to
negate a single effect, by which they are struck,
which causes any of the following Damage Types;
Chaos, Destruction, Dream, Essence, Fire, Ice, Light,
Lightning, Order, Reason, Shadow, or Stone. The call
for this effect is "Ward".
Elixir of Healing [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy
Duration: Charged (5)
Effects: This Invocation grants the Character five(5)
Charges. The Character may spend one(1) Charge to
deliver a packet attack causing twenty(20) points of
Poison Healing.
Familiar Bond [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Reset
Effects: At the time of this Invocation's casting, the
Character must touch a single target. The Caster
gains the ability to cast Spells with a range of "Self"
on the target as Touch spells. A Caster cannot have
more than one target of Familiar Bond in existence at
a time. This effect ends if the Caster enters their
Death Count or they Cast this Invocation on a new
target.
If the Caster casts a Spell with a duration of
Sustained on the target, any Spells cast by the target,
or having another Spell with a duration of Sustained
cast on them removes the previous Spell's effects.
Forge Salts [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Concentration
Effects: This Invocation allows the Character to
perform Smithing abilities without the need for a
Portable Forge. This Invocation ends when the
Character after five(5) minutes or until the Character
uses any Skill other than Smithing.
Hone Weapon [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation increases the damage the
Character causes with a single weapon by one(1).
This effect does not stack with the effects of the
Enhance Weapon Ritual Effect.
Identify [Invocation]
Type: All
Duration: Instant
Effects: This Invocation reveals to the Caster any
and all special properties of the target item. Special
properties are all Ritual Effects present on the target,
as well as all other information on the item's tag. At
the Marshal's discretion, this effect may also reveal
additional information on the nature of the item.
This takes an amount of time equal to the amount
of time needed for the Marshal to convey the
information.
Investiture [Invocation]
Type: All
Duration: Instant
Effects: This Invocation requires both the Caster and
Target Character to to be standing fully within a target
Protective Circle in which the Caster is Invested.
When performed as an Alchemy Invocation the
Circle must be an Alchemical Fountain. When
performed as a Magic Invocation the Circle must be a
Magic Circle. When performed as a Smithing
Invocation the Circle must be a Forge of Kings.
This Invocation grants the Target Character an
Investiture into the target Circle.
Iron Gilding [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge to
negate a single Compulsion effect by which they are
struck. The call for this effect is "Ward".
Living Spell [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge
when casting a Spell to Quicken the Spell, as per the
Skill Quicken Skill. Alternatively, they may expend
three(3) Charges in order to cast a Spell when they
are in a state where they would be unable to Cast
Spells such as under the effects of a Confine or Drain
effect. This does not allow the Character to cast
spells while Dead or Unconcious.
Magic Aura [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation allows the Caster to deliver
their Killing Blows as Magic.
Master's Touch [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation causes the target
Masterwork item to become Indestructible and
immune to Break and Destroy. This effect ends if the
item leaves the Caster's possession.
Master's Watch [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation must be cast on, and within,
a valid Circle. All items within the Circle gain
Immunity to Break and Destroy so long as they
remain in the Circle. This effect ends if the Caster
leaves the Circle or enters their Death Count.
Metal Dust [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster five(5)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge in
order to deliver, by Touch, an <Iron, Normal, or
Silver> Aura effect allowing the target to use the
chosen Weapon Type against a single opponent until
end of Combat.
Polycast [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Charged (1)
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster one(1)
Charge. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge
when casting a Spell, with a range of "Packet", to
throw any number of packets, all at once. Any
creature struck by one or more packets takes the
effects of the Spell. Alternatively they may expend
one(1) Charge, when casting a Spell with a range of
"Touch" or greater, to be able to deliver an additional
copy of Spell, by Touch, on any number of targets
until one of the following occurs; the Spell is
Disrupted, the Caster goes ten(10) seconds without
affecting a new target, or the Caster uses any other
Skill. No target may benefit from the effects of a
single Spell cast using this ability more than once.
Purify Land [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Instant
Effects: This Invocation removes all forms of
corruption, rot, and disease from the target land. The
Marshal of the encounter determines the number of
casting needed to completely remove any deleterious
effects on the land.
Purify Water [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy
Duration: Instant
Effects: This Invocation removes all forms of
corruption, rot, and disease from the target water.
The Marshal of the encounter determines the number
of castings needed to completely remove any
deleterious effects on the water.
Purity of Flesh [Invocation]
Type: All
Duration: Charged (3)
Effect: This Invocation grants the Character three(3)
Charges of this ability. The Character can expend
one(1) Charge to ignore the effects of all Carrier
Attacks delivered by the target against whom this
ability is Invoked.
Ready Armour [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation increases the Armour Points,
granted by any Physical Armour, worn by the
Character by ten(10). This effect stacks with all other
effects which Reinforce Physical Armour.
Shadow Slime [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation increases the damage the
Character causes, when Behind their target, with a
single weapon by two(2). This effect does not stack
with the effects of the Stalker's Weapon Ritual Effect.
Silver Gilding [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge to
negate a single Curse effect by which they are struck.
The call for this effect is "Ward".
Spirit of the Forge [Invocation]
Type: Smithing
Duration: Concentration
Effects: This Invocation targets a Portable Forge.
The target Portable Forge counts as a Town Forge
until one of the following events occur; the Character
uses any Skill other than Smithing, the targeted
Portable Forge is moved, the Caster moves more
than three(3) metres from the Forge or the Caster is
subjected to any effect which would Disrupt Spell
casting.
Steel Slime [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation increases the Armour Points,
granted by any Natural Armour, worn by the
Character by ten(10). This effect stacks with all other
effects which Reinforce Natural Armour.
Transmute Weapon [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation changes the Weapon Type
of the target Weapon to one of the following; Iron,
Normal, or Silver. This effect ends if the item leaves
the Caster's possession. As long as this Invocation is
in effect, the Alchemist can spend one(1) minute to
change the Weapon Type to any other valid option.
Tribute [Invocation]
Type: All
Duration: Charged (1)
Effects: This Invocation grants the Character one(1)
Charge. The Character may spend one(1) Charge to
reduce the number of Components of a particular
type needed to cast a Ritual Effect of the same type
as this Invocation by one(1), to a minimum of one(1).
For example, when used as a Smithing Invocation
this ability can reduce the number of Power
Components needed by any Ritual Smithing effect by
one(1). No Ritual can be targeted by this effect, for a
particular Component Type, more than once.
Unstable Aura [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Reset
Effects: This Invocation increases the damage the
Character causes with a single weapon by one(1).
This effect does not stack with the effects of the
Enhance Weapon Ritual Effect. This effect ends if the
item leaves the Caster's possession.
Vital Elixir [Invocation]
Type: Alchemy
Duration: Charged (3)
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge to
negate a single effect, by which they are struck,
which causes Body damage. The call for this effect is
"Ward".
Wall of Force [Invocation]
Type: Magic
Duration: Charged (3)
Effects: This Invocation grants the Caster three(3)
Charges. The Caster may expend one(1) Charge of
this ability in order to deliver a packet delivered
"Arcane Repel" effect. Alternatively, the Character
may expend all three(3) Charges, the Caster may
throw any number of packets when delivering this
effect. Any target struck by one(1) or more Packets is
affected by this effect.
Martial Kata
A Martial Kata represents a Character's near
supernatural mastery of martial arts allowing them to
perform otherwise impossible feats of skill. For every
Rank the Character has in the Skill Martial Kata, the
Character can, once per Reset, perform a single Kata
as described below.
Performing a Martial Kata
The process to perform a Martial Kata requires the
Character to have the Treatise which describes the
effect or must have the Kata memorised using the
Martial Secret Skill. Performing a Kata requires
one(1) minute if the Character is in an area in which
they can Rest, or five(5) minutes anywhere else. A
Kata is interrupted if the Character performing the
Kata, or being targeted by the Kata, is struck by any
effect, uses any Skill or chooses to stop performing
the Kata. If the performance of the Kata was
interrupted the Character must Meditate before
beginning any new Katas. A Kata is considered used
only when it is successfully completed. The effects of
a Kata are lost at Reset and persist through the
Death, but not the Resurrection, of the Character.
A Character is able to benefit from no more than
five(5) Kata at any time. Should a sixth Kata, with a
duration other than Instant, be performed the Player
must choose one(1) active Kata to be lost. Kata
whose effects are instant, such as the Aethercleave
Kata, do not count against this limit. Kata which do
not affect the Caster, such as Combat Forms, do not
apply to the caster's limit of active Katas, but does
apply to the limits of all Characters who are the
targets of such Katas.
A Character may Remove the effects of a Kata
they have performed on themselves by spending
one(1) minute to do so.
Mentoring Martial Kata
A Character who has the Skill Martial Secret may
mentor another Character, allowing the mentored
Character to perform any Kata the mentoring
Character has memorised. This process requires
five(5) minutes and can only be performed in an area
of Rest. To benefit from this effect, the mentored
Character must still possess the Skill Martial Kata,
have one(1) or more uses available to benefit from
mentoring and access to the appropriate School of
Martial Arts.
Stacking Martial Kata Effects
A Character cannot benefit from more than a single
copy of any Kata at a time. This includes Kata which
the Character may not be the source of the effect
such as the targets of the Defensive Forms Kata.
Kata Name [Kata]
School: Martial Arts School needed to perform.
Effect: The effect of the Kata.

Table 8.6 – Martial Kata List
Kata Descriptions
Aethercleave [Kata]
School: Slayer
Effect: This Kata allows the Caster to perform a
"Physical-Strike Sunder Magic" upon its completion. The
Physical-Strike must be performed within ten(10)
seconds of the end of this Kata. This Kata does not
count against the five(5) Kata limit.
This effect allows the Character to Destroy a magical
effect, but it cannot be used against an Item or a
Creature. When this Kata is completed, or Interrupted,
the Caster is affected by an Arcane Weakness effect.
In the event that this ability is used against a Player
Character created protective effect, such as a
Sanctuary, the Marshal may increase the time needed to
perform this Kata to up to one(1) hour.
The caster must be able to touch the effect during the
casting. In the case of targeting a Sanctuary, the caster
must be able to touch one of the doorways, or portal,
into the targeted Sanctuary.
Archer's Quiet [Kata]
School: Duelist
Effect: This Kata grants the Character one(1) Charge
of this ability. The Character can expend one(1)
Charge to activate this ability. Upon activating this
ability the Character may use the Weapon Type:
Massive for all Archery attacks. This effect ends if the
Character moves from where they were standing
when they activated this ability.
Armour Bond [Kata]
School: Guardian
Effect: This Kata increases the Character's Wear
Point total by ten(10) to a maximum of fifty(50).
Additionally, the value of any Physical Armour the
Character has is increased by ten(10). This effect
stacks with all other effects which Reinforce Physical
Armour.
Bladed Defence [Kata]
School: Duelist
Effect: This Kata grants the Character three(3)
Charges of this ability. When using the Parry Skill, the
Character may expend one(1) Charge to instead
Reflect the effect.
Blood Frenzy [Kata]
School: Slayer
Effect: This Kata grants the Character one(1) Charge
of this ability. The Character can expend one(1)
Charge to instantly heal to full Hit Points. This can be
triggered if the Character is Dead or below zero(0)
Hit Points but the decision to do so must be made
immediately. Once this ability has been triggered the
Character gains Resistance to all Damage until they
receive any other healing or Combat ends.
Callisthenics [Kata]
School: Duelist
Effect: For every Rank the character has in the Skill
Martial Kata, they may designate one(1) Character as
a target of this Kata. Every target, other than the
Caster, gains a single use of the Dodge Skill allowing
them to, Once per Reset, avoid a single effect
delivered by weapon or packet. No target can benefit
from this Kata more than Once per Reset.
Cleaving Strikes [Kata]
School: Slayer
Effect: This Kata grants the Character three(3)
Charges of this ability. The Character can expend
one(1) Charge to Echo a single Martial Art.
Combat Forms [Kata]
School: Duelist
Effect: For every Rank the character has in the Skill
Martial Kata, they may designate one(1) Character as
a target of this Kata. Every target, other than the
Caster, increases their Weapon Damage by one(1).
Dance of the Dragon [Kata]
School: Slayer
Effect: This Kata grants the Character five(5)
Charges of this ability. The Character can expend
one(1) Charge to deliver an attack causing "50
Physical-Strike <Weapon Type>". Once the first
charge has been used the Character has ten(10)
seconds to use another charge or all remaining
Charges are lost and this effect ends.
Dead Magic Form [Kata]
School: Guardian
Effect: This Kata grants the Character five(5) Charge
of this ability. The Character can expend one(1)
Charge to reduce the damage of an effect delivered
as Magic(-Strike) by half. Alternatively, the Character
may expend one(1) Charge to reduce the duration of
any effect delivered as Magic(-Strike) to ten(10)
seconds. The call for this ability is "Reduced".
Defensive Forms [Kata]
School: Guardian
Effect: For every Rank the character has in the Skill
Martial Kata, they may designate a one(1) Character
as a target of this Kata. Every target, other than the
Caster, increases their maximum Hit Points by five(5).
This effect does not stack with any other effect which
increases Hit Points.
Elemental Bladeshroud [Kata]
School: Duelist, Slayer
Effect: This Kata grants the Character one(1) Charge
of this ability. The Character can expend one(1)
Charge to deliver an Elemental-Strike causing one-
hundred(100) points of damage of any of the
following elements; Chaos, Destruction, Dream,
Essence, Fire, Ice, Light, Lightning, Order, Reason,
Shadow, or Stone. Once this attack has been
delivered against their target, the Character may
replace their Weapon Type with the chosen element
against the same target. Once activated, this ability
lasts until the end of Combat.
Gauntlet Forms [Kata]
School: Duelist, Guardian
Effect: Upon casting this Kata the Caster designates
one arm as their off-hand arm. So long as the
Character is not wielding a weapon with their off-hand
arm they are Immune to Weapon Damage which
strikes the chosen arm, except for Weapon Type
Massive. This effect is suppressed if the Character
wields any two-handed weapon, a second weapon or
a shield. It is also suppressed if the Character holds
any in-game item in that arm. Finally, the off-hand
does not count as a free hand for Spell casting. The
Character does not need to wear a Gauntlet on their
off-hand but must wear a red bracelet around the
wrist.
Invincible Defence [Kata]
School: Guardian
Effect: This Kata grants the Character three(3)
Charges of this ability. The Character can expend
one(1) Charge to gain the ability to block Weapon
Type: Massive from a single opponent against whom
this ability is Invoked.
Iron Skin [Kata]
School: Guardian
Effect: This Kata grants the Character five(5) Charge
of this ability. The Character can expend one(1)
Charge to reduce the damage of an effect delivered
as Physical(-Strike) by half. Alternatively, the
Character may expend one(1) Charge to reduce the
duration of any effect delivered as Physical(-Strike) to
ten(10) seconds. The call for this ability is "Reduced".
Jester's Dance [Kata]
School: Duelist
Effect: This Kata grants the Character five(5)
Charges of this ability. The Character can expend
one(1) Charge to Dodge a single attack delivered by
Weapon or Packet. Once the first charge has been
used the Character has ten(10) seconds to use
another charge or all remaining Charges are lost and
this effect ends.
Last Stand [Kata]
School: Guardian
Effect: This Kata grants the Character one(1) Charge
of this ability. The Character can expend one(1)
Charge to activate this ability. Upon activating this
ability the Character immediately enters a special
state of Immunity. Upon entering this state the
Character begins a five(5) minute countdown. During
this count, the Character is Immune to all effects. This
effect ends as soon as one of the following occurs;
the five(5) minute countdown ends, Character takes
ten(10) steps, or the Character voluntarily ends this
effect. Once this effect ends the Character is Dead
and the amount of time left in their Death Count is
equal to the amount of time left on the five(5) minute
countdown which began when this ability was first
activated. If there is any time left in their Death Count,
because they ended this ability before the countdown
reaches zero(0), they may receive a Life Spell and
any effects which trigger upon Death are triggered. If
the countdown reaches zero(0) while the Character
was still under this effect they immediately Dissipate
as though their Death Count reached zero(0).
This effect can be ended early due to the
Character entering a state where they are unable to
use Skills or Abilities, or forced to move, as a result of
an effect delivered as Arcane(-Strike). In these states
the effect ends as if the Character ended it voluntarily.
Perfection of Self [Kata]
School: All
Effect: This Kata grants the Character three(3)
Charges of this ability. The Character can expend
one(1) Charge to ignore the effects of all Carrier
Attacks delivered by the target against whom this
ability is Invoked.
Purity of Body [Kata]
School: Guardian
Effect: This Kata grants the Character five(5) Charge
of this ability. The Character can expend one(1)
Charge to reduce the damage of an effect delivered
as Poison(-Strike) by half. Alternatively, the Character
may expend one(1) Charge to reduce the duration of
any effect delivered as Poison(-Strike) to ten(10)
seconds. The call for this ability is "Reduced".
Rending Strikes [Kata]
School: Slayer
Effect: This Kata grants the Character three(3)
Charges of this ability. The Character can expend
one(1) Charge to deliver a single Martial Art as
Piercing.
Shadow Bond [Kata]
School: Duelist, Slayer
Effect: This Kata increases the Character's Weapon
Damage, when behind their target and able to see
both their target's shoulder blades, with the chosen
weapon by two(2). This Kata may be performed more
than once but each additional effect must target
another weapon. This effect does not stack with any
other Kata which increases Weapon Damage.
Shadow Knife [Kata]
School: Duelist, Slayer
Effect: This Kata grants the Character five(5) Charge
of this ability. The Character can expend one(1)
Charge to throw a packet in place of a Thrown
Weapon. This ability can be used to deliver any
Martial Art with a range of Strike including those
which can only be delivered by a Thrown Weapon.
The Shadow Knife can also be used to deliver a
Weapon Attack benefiting from any Weapon Training
and Stealth Training the Character possesses.
Additionally, the Character is considered to be
behind their target for any other ability which requires
them to be behind their target. The Weapon Type of
the attack, if applicable, is changed to Shadow.
Finally, as the packet attack is considered to be a
Thrown Weapon, abilities such as Shadow Shot or
Split Shot, which allow the Character to throw
additional packets are applicable to this ability as
well.
Shadow Specialisation [Kata]
School: Duelist, Slayer
Effect: This Kata increases the Character's Weapon
Damage, when behind their target and able to see
both their target's shoulder blades, with the chosen
weapon by four(4). Once the Character has
performed this Kata they are unable to use Martial
Arts with any other weapon until this effect ends, or
the Character casts this effect again, choosing a new
weapon. This effect does not stack with any other
Kata which increases Weapon Damage.
Spiritual Weapon [Kata]
School: Duelist, Slayer
Effect: This Kata grants the Character three(3)
Charges of this ability. The Character may expend
one(1) Charge to deliver a Packet attack causing an
amount of Magic Force damage equal to the
combined number of Ranks the Character has in
Weapon Training and Stealth Training. The Character
may throw any number of Packets when using this
ability. Any Character struck by one(1), or more,
packets is affected by this effect.
Spiteful Charge [Kata]
School: Slayer
Effect: This Kata grants the Character three(3)
Charges of this ability. The Character may expend
one(1) Charge from this ability in order to double the
damage caused by any Martial Art delivered as an
<Effect>-Strike.
Alternatively, the Character may expend one(1)
Charge from this ability to double the damage caused
by any of the following Skills; Critical Strike, Deadly
Strike, Penetrating Strike, Precise Strike, Vital Strike.
Regardless of which effect of this ability is used,
once the effect is delivered the Character is affected
by an Arcane Fumble preventing them from using the
weapon for ten(10) seconds after delivering the
effect, even if they fail to strike any target.
Third Eye [Kata]
School: Guardian
Effect: This Kata grants the Character three(3)
Charges of this ability. The Character may expend
one(1) Charge to avoid a Packet or Weapon
delivered effect by which they are struck to which the
Dodge Skill would be an applicable defence. The call
for this effect is "Dodge".
Alternatively, the Character may expend one(1)
Charge and a use of the Dodge Skill, or two(2)
Charges of this ability, in order to avoid any effect to
which the Skill Evasion is applicable. The call for this
use of this ability is "Evasion".
Void Dance [Kata]
School: Duelist
Effect: This Kata increases the Character's Wear
Point total by ten(10) to a maximum of fifty(50).
Additionally, the value of any Avoidance Armour the
Character has is increased by ten(10). This effect
stacks with all other effects which Reinforce
Avoidance Armour.
Weapon Focus [Kata]
School: All
Effect: This Kata increases the Character's Weapon
Damage with all weapons, with which they are
proficient, by one(1). This effect does not stack with
any other Kata which increases Weapon Damage.
Weapon Specialisation [Kata]
School: All
Effect: This Kata increases the Character's Weapon
Damage with a single chosen weapon, such as Long
Sword, Short Axe, Two-Handed Mace, by two(2).
Once the Character has performed this Kata they are
unable to use Martial Arts unless they are wielding
their chosen weapon. This effect does not stack with
any other Kata which increases Weapon Damage.
Interventions
Interventions are extremely powerful abilities that
the most experienced Characters have the ability to
perform. Upon gaining any Intervention Skill the
Character can perform a single Intervention each
Event. The Intervention is chosen, and recorded, at
the start of each Event. If they have multiple Skills
which grant the ability to perform an Intervention, or
the Skill Favoured by Destiny, it allows them to
prepare an additional Intervention they can add to the
list of those they can perform and increases the total
number of Interventions they can perform each Event
by one(1) but never more than once per Day. For
example, a Character with three(3) Intervention Skills
is able to choose three(3) different Interventions to
add to their list for the Event. They may then, no
more than once per Day, perform one(1) of their
chosen Interventions. The Character may perform
any combination of their chosen Interventions
including performing the same one multiple times
over the Event.
If the Character has the Skill Favoured by Destiny,
the Character can perform one(1) additional
Intervention per Event, but never more than one(1)
per Day.
The decision to trigger an Intervention, or its
effects, is always made by the Player. The call to
activate an Intervention is: "I Invoke <Intervention
Name>.". This call can be made even while under an
effect which prevents the use of Skills and can even
be performed during the Character's Death Count.
Intervention Name [Intervention]
Type: The Intervention's Type (Crafting, Magical,
Martial, or Shadow) Interventions of more than one
type may be performed by anyone with at least
matching type of Intervention.
Benefit: The effects of the Intervention. Some
Interventions have special requirements before they
can be used. These requirements may require the
Character to have access to a particular School of
Magic or some other requirement.

Table 8.7 – Intervention List
Interventions Descriptions
Beacon of Hope [Intervention]
Type: Crafting, Magical
Benefit: When performed as a Magical Intervention
this Intervention can only be performed by a
Character able to cast Spells from the Restoration
School of Magic. The Character delivers, by voice, a
"Arcane Revive" to all Adventurer's on the field. The
call for this ability is "Sound of Voice: All Adventurers,
Arcane Revive!".
Collapsing Cosmos [Intervention]
Type: Magical, Martial
Benefit: This Intervention grants the Character the
ability to deliver one attack for each of the following
elements; Fire, Ice, Lightning, Stone, and Force.
Each of the five(5) attacks causes one-hundred(100)
points of Piercing Elemental damage of the
appropriate type.
When performed as a Magical Intervention, the
Character may choose to have all of these attacks
delivered as packet attacks. Alternatively, and when
performed as a Martial Intervention, the effect is
delivered as Weapon attacks. The Character may use
these attacks in any order but at the end of combat,
all remaining attacks are lost. When delivered as a
packet attack the call is "100 Piercing Elemental
<Type>". When delivered as a weapon attack the call
is "100 Piercing Elemental-Strike <Type>"
Comforts of the Hearth [Intervention]
Type: Crafting
Benefit: This Intervention targets a Circle. The Circle
becomes an Area of Rest allowing Characters to
Rest within its borders. Additionally, any Character
who enters the Circle may Meditate and Refit Armour
on a ten(10)-Count instead of the normal sixty(60)-
Count. This effect continues until the Character
activating this Intervention leaves the Circle, or enters
a state where they are unable to use Skills such as
Unconscious or Dead.
Devouring Shadows [Intervention]
Type: Shadow
Benefit: This Intervention allows the Character to
deliver a packet attack causing two-hundred(200)
points of Arcane Shadow damage. The Call for this
ability is: "200 Arcane Shadow". If the Character
misses with this packet attack they may continue to
throw packets until they successfully hit any target.
Once the Character strikes a target successfully, the
Character benefits from the effects of an Arcane
Revive.
Elemental Annihilation [Intervention]
Type: Crafting, Magical
Benefit: When performed as a Magical Intervention
this Intervention can only be performed by a
Character able to cast Spells from the Conjuration
School of Magic. The Character delivers, by voice,
one-hundred(100) Piercing Elemental damage of any
of the following types; Fire, Ice, Lightning, or Stone,
to all enemies on the field. Upon delivering this effect,
the Character gains a single Charge of this ability.
This Charge may be expended in order to deliver, by
voice, a second one-hundred(100) Piercing
Elemental damage of the same type. The call for this
ability is "Sound of Voice: All Enemies, 100 Piercing
Elemental <Type>!"
Eldritch Apotheosis [Intervention]
Type: Magical
Benefit: This Intervention requires the Character to
have access to the following Schools of Magic;
Affliction, Conjuration, Protection, Restoration, and
Transmutation. Upon performing this Intervention the
character gains five(5) Charges of this ability. The
Character may, when casting a Spell from memory,
expend one(1) Charge of this ability to gain one of the
following effects;
Triplet Spell: The Character may deliver two(2)
copies of the Spell. These copies include all the same
Spellshapes or options made when casting the Spell
as per the Skill Echo Spell except the Character may
Echo the Spell Twice instead of only once.
Intensify Spell: This ability can only be used on
Spells with a Range of Packet or Touch. The damage,
or healing, caused by the Spell are quadrupled.
Arcane Spell: This ability allows the Character to
deliver a single Spell, below 9th Level, as Arcane(-
Strike).
Parallelisation: This ability can only be used on
Spells with a Range of Packet or Touch. The
Character may throw any number of packets to
deliver the Spell. Any Character struck by one or
more packets is affected by the effect. Alternatively,
the Caster may deliver the Spell, by Touch, to any
number of targets so long as no target receives the
same effect more than once and no more than
ten(10) seconds go by without the Character affecting
a new target.
Cyclical Magic: The Character recovers all Spells
Slots which were expended to Cast Spells which
benefited from the effects of this Intervention.
Each ability can be used only once. No Spell may
benefit from more than a single effect of this
Intervention at a time. Any unused Charges are lost
at the end of the current Reset period.
Feast of Souls [Intervention]
Type: Crafting, Magical
Benefit: When performed as a Magical Intervention
this Intervention can only be performed by a
Character able to cast Spells from the Affliction
School of Magic. The Character delivers, by voice,
fifty(50) Arcane Body damage, to all enemies on the
field. Additionally, the Character delivers to all allies,
fifty(50) Arcane Healing. The call for this ability is
"Sound of Voice: All Enemies, 50 Arcane Body!
Sound of Voice: All Adventurers, 50 Arcane Healing!"
Hungering Shadows [Intervention]
Type: Shadow
Benefit: This Intervention grants all Adventurers the
ability to use the damage bonus from any Skill or
ability requiring them to be behind their target, such
as Stealth Training, to receive its benefits. It also
grants the Characters the ability to use any Skill
which requires them to be behind their target. While
under this effect the Character's base Weapon Call
does not consume charges of the Ambush ability.
This effect ends at the end of Combat. The call for
this ability is "Sound of Voice: All Adventurers, Arcane
Imbue Skill: Ambush <Infinite>"
Indomitable Fortress [Intervention]
Type: Martial
Benefit: This Intervention grants the Character
Resistance to all effects until the end of Combat. This
causes the Character to take half-damage from all
damaging effects and reduce the duration of all
harmful effects with a duration other than Instant to
ten(10) seconds. The Player determines which effects
are harmful.
Martial Perfection [Intervention]
Type: Martial, Shadow
Benefit: This Intervention doubles the bonus damage
from the Character's Weapon Training and Stealth
Training. Additionally, all Techniques and Skills
delivered by weapon attack the Character uses are
delivered as Piercing. These effects last until the end
of Combat
Phoenix Flame [Intervention]
Type: Crafting, Magical, Martial, Shadow
Benefit: This Intervention grants the Character the
effects of a Magic Cleanse and a Magic Revive.
Additionally, the Character may deliver, by Touch, a
Magic Life at will. No target may be affected by this
Touch ability more than once. Finally, this ability
grants the Character one(1) Charge. The Character
may expend this Charge at any time, even while
Dead, to grant themselves another Magic Cleanse
and Magic Revive. This effect, and any remaining
Charges ends at the end of Combat.
Shadow Duplication [Intervention]
Type: Shadow
Benefit: Upon activating this Intervention, the
Character gains the following ability; Whenever the
Character uses the Dodge Skill, the Character goes
Out of Game for ten(10) Steps. The Character must
make all ten(10) of these steps immediately and
cannot stop until they have taken all ten(10).
Whenever the Character uses the Evasion Skill, they
gain the same benefits they would if they used a
Dodge but they, additionally, also benefit from an
Arcane Refit Avoidance Armour. This ability cannot be
used to reach areas the Character could not reach
normally. This effect ends at the End of Combat.
Shadow Walk [Intervention]
Type: Crafting, Magical, Shadow
Benefit: This Intervention allows the Character to
deliver against all willing Adventurers an Arcane
Teleport. This ability follows all other rules for the
Spell Teleport. The call for this ability is "Sound of
Voice: All Willing Adventurers, Arcane Teleport!"
Sunder Reality [Intervention]
Type: Martial
Benefit: This Intervention allows the Character to
deliver an attack causing five-hundred(500) points of
Arcane-Strike Body damage. Upon doing so, the
Character takes one-hundred(100) points of Arcane
Body damage. The Call for this ability is "500 Arcane-
Strike Body". If the Character misses with this attack
they suffer one-hundred(100) points of Arcane Body
damage but may attempt to use this Intervention
again, after Meditating, until they successfully struck
a target.
Time Stop [Intervention]
Type: Crafting, Magical
Benefit: When performed as a Magical Intervention
this Intervention can only be performed by a
Character able to cast Spells from the Transmutation
School of Magic. The Character may perform an
Intervention causing one of the following four(4)
effects; "Sound of Voice: All Adventurers, Lesser
Arcane Stasis", "Sound of Voice: All Enemies, Lesser
Arcane Stasis", "Sound of Voice: Lesser Arcane
Stasis", or they may deliver a packet attack causing
an "Arcane Stasis" effect. The Character performing
this Intervention is never affected by this effect unless
they target themselves with the "Arcane Stasis"
effect.
Warding Shell [Intervention]
Type: Crafting, Magical
Benefit: When performed as a Magical Intervention
this Intervention can only be performed by a
Character able to cast Spells from the Protection
School of Magic.
This Intervention creates a magical defensive
barrier preventing anyone from entering, but not
leaving, the area and rendering all fully within its
bounds Immune to all effects originating from outside
of its boundary. In order to use this effect the
Character must be inside of a valid Circle. The Caster
may "Recognize" an individual into the Circle. This
effect continues until the Character activating this
Intervention leaves the Circle, or enters a state where
they are unable to use Skills such as Unconscious or
Dead. This effect does not end if the Caster is under
the effects of Stasis, though it does prevent the
Caster from recognizing anyone in.
Alternatively, the Character may instead grant
themselves five(5) charges of this ability. The
Character may then expend one(1) Charge in order
to negate any effect, other than those delivered as
Arcane, by which they are struck as per the Ward
ability. The Character may, at will, Meditate to regain
one(1) Charge of this ability.
The effects of this Intervention last until the end of
Combat.
Warlord's Onslaught [Intervention]
Type: Martial
Benefit: This Intervention requires the Character to
have access to the following Martial Arts Schools;
Duelist, Guardian, Slayer. Upon performing this
Intervention the character gains five(5) Charges of
this ability. The Character may expend one(1) Charge
of this ability to gain one of the following effects;
Triplet Strike: When performing a Martial Art, the
Character may deliver two(2) copies of the Martial
Art. These copies include all the same options made
when casting the Martial Art as per the Echo Ability
except the Character may Echo the Martial Art Twice
instead of only once.
Worldbreaker's Strike: This ability can only be
used on Martial Arts with a Range of Strike. When
performing a Martial Art, the Character may
quadruple the damage caused by the Martial Art.
Strike at the Soul: This ability allows the
Character to Invoke, against a single target, the
ability to ignore all positional requirements for their
abilities. This last until the end of Combat.
Unstoppable Will: This ability instantly restores
the Character to full Hit Points, as per the Revive
effect, and removes all harmful effects, as per the
Cleanse effect. This ability can be used regardless of
any effects on the Character. The call for this ability is
"Physical Revive, Physical Cleanse".
Cycle of Violence: The Character Reflects a
single effect by which they are struck.
No single Martial Art may be affected by more than
once(1) ability at a time. Any Charges remaining at
the end of Combat are converted into Healing. At the
end of Combat the Character will receive an amount
of healing equal to ten(10) per Charge remaining. If
the Character is Dead, one(1) Charge is expended to
instead return them life at zero(0) Hit Points. Any
remaining Charges heal as normal.
Chapter 9: Background Skills
Background Skills
Background Skills represent Character's
professions and Skills which they have gained which
are not directly related to Adventuring. Background
Skills are not affected by a Character's Skill Priority
and have the same cost for every Character.
Background Sub-Skills
Certain Background Skills, such as Academic and
Merchant, have a number of Sub-Skills which a
Character may choose from whenever they gain a
Rank in the appropriate Background Skill. These
Skills, and their Sub-Skills are described below.
Background Skill Descriptions
Academic [Background]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains an additional Rank in
the Academic Skill See Page 199 for information on
this Skill.
Bodyguard [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
guard another. The Character may, once per Reset,
designate a Character as their charge. A Character
may have only one(1) charge at a time but may
change it as often as they Rest.
Once per Reset, the Character may negate any
Weapon Attack, or attack delivered, by Weapon or
Packet, as <Effect>-Strike or Physical by which their
charge is struck. The charge must be within
Weapon's reach of the Character to be targeted by
this effect. The call for this ability is "Parry".
If the Character has any uses of the Parry Skill, the
Character can use this ability to negate an applicable
attack which strikes their Charge so long as they are
within their Weapon's reach.
Diagnose [Background]
Requirements: First Aid
Benefit: This Skill allows the Character to determine
the state of the target. In order to use this Skill, the
Character must touch a willing, or helpless creature
and state "Diagnose" followed by one of the following
questions; "Are you Sleeping?", "Are you
Unconscious?", "Are you Dying?", "Are you Dead?",
"Are you Paralyzed?", "Are you Poisoned?", "Are you
Regenerating?", "How long until you dissipate?",
"How long until you wake-up?", "How long until you
die?", "How long until you become Unparalyzed?",
"How long until you finish Regenerating?". "How
many Hit Points are you missing?". It is not possible
to use this Skill on a target without their knowledge
and an unwilling target can simply say "I Block you." A
Helpless target is unable to stop this Skill from being
used on them.
The Character cannot ask another question until
the first is answered and this Skill takes as long as it
takes for the Character to ask the question and the
target to answer. It cannot, normally, be used against
non-humanoid creatures, or those that have no, or
alien metabolisms.
First Aid [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: This Skill allows the Character to Stabilise a
Dying target. In order to use this Skill, the Character
must touch the target and state "First Aid". If the
target is not Dying, or immune to First Aid, the target
must immediately state "No Effect". Applying First Aid
is a sixty(60) second Counted Action.
Once First Aid has begun, the target immediately
pauses their Dying Count. it remains paused until
either the First Aid count is finished, or interrupted. In
the latter case the dying creature immediately
resumes their count where it left off.
At the end of the First Aid count, the target is
Stabilised and at zero(0) Hit Points. After one(1)
minute the target regains Consciousness at one(1)
Hit Point.
This Skill is modified by the Physician background
skill. For more information on how these Skills
interact, See Page 203.
Invoker [Background]
Requirements: Level 5 Alchemy Slot, Level 5 Spell
Slot, or Smithing 5.
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may perform a
single Invocation of any type for which the meet this
Skill's Requirements. Level 5 Alchemy Slots allow a
single Alchemy Invocation. Level 5 Spell Slots allow a
Magic Invocation. Five(5) Ranks in the Skill Smithing
allows a Smithing Invocation.
A Character who meets multiple requirements does
not gain the ability to perform multiple Invocations
with this Skill but expands their options for the one(1)
they may perform.
This Skill does not count towards the
Requirements of any other Skills or abilities.
Merchant [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains one Rank in the
Merchant Skill as well as a single Merchant Sub-Skill
for which they meet the Requirements. See Page 210
for more information.
Physician [Background]*
Requirements: Diagnose and First Aid
Benefit: The Character gains an additional rank in
the Physician Skill See Page 203 for information on
this Skill.
Read and Write [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains the ability to read and
write in all languages they know. Without this Skill the
Character is unable to read or recognize even the
simplest of symbols.
Please note that this does not mean a Character
without this Skill is stupid or unable to perform maths,
merely that they are unable to understand any form of
written media.
Read and Write is not Considered a Skill for any
effect or action which limits the use of Skills. Meaning
it is possible to read while Meditating, Resting or
even while casting Ritual Magic.
Sailor [Background]
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains immunity to many
environmental effects while fighting on, or beneath
the sea. The specific effects are up to the Marshal
who must be informed of this Skill at the start of the
Module.
Additionally, once per Reset the Character may,
when drinking any beverage, grant themselves either
of the following effects; 20 Poison Healing, or Poison
Remove <Delivery Type> of any of the following
Delivery Types; Elemental, Magic, Physical, or
Poison. Once per Reset the Character may Rest to
recover the use of this ability.
Seer [Background]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains an additional rank in
the Seer Skill See Page 206 for information on this
Skill.
Spy [Background]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is in the know
about many secrets. At the start of each Event, the
Player receives a rumour sheet.
Trophy Hunter [Background]*
Requirements: None
Benefit: The Character gains an additional Rank in
the Trophy Hunter Skill See Page 209 for information
on this Skill.
