Table 9.1 – Background Skills
Academic
The Academic is a Character who has devoted a
large portion of their time to learning a variety of
subjects gaining access to borderline, and
occasionally legitimately, supernatural abilities due to
their studies. Each time this Skill is bought, the
Character may purchase a single Sub-Skill for which
they meet all prerequisites. These Sub-Skills may be
purchased in any order provided the Character has
any requisite Sub-Skills.
Additionally, the Character gains the following
abilities; as they gain Ranks in this Skill. The
Character automatically gains these abilities for free
as soon as they have the prerequisite Ranks in the
appropriate Skill.
Researcher [Academic]
Prerequisites: One Rank in Academic
Benefit: Whenever the Character is in a library
setting related to any of the Character's Academic
Sub-Skills they may inform the encounter's Marshal
of this fact. The Marshal may, at their discretion,
provide the Character with additional information
related to the subject matter.
The encounter's Marshal determines how, if any,
knowledge is distributed but the most common
methods are to inform all those with an applicable
Sub-Skill at once with all information, or to distribute a
single piece of information to each applicable
Character.
Studious Focus [Academic]
Prerequisites: Five Ranks in Academic
Benefit: Once per Day, the character may grant
themselves the effects of a "Physical Compulsion
Ward".
Eclectic Knowledge [Academic]
Prerequisites: Ten Ranks in Academic
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may perform
the Identify Invocation. The Character must follow all
rules for the casting of the Invocation. This Skill does
not grant the Character the ability to perform any
other Invocation, nor does it count towards the
number of Invocations the Character may cast each
Day.
Academic Sub-Skill Descriptions
Archaeology [Academic]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: The Character may also use the Diagnose
Skill on Constructs. Additionally, they may, once per
Day, deliver by packet, the effects of a "Magic Break
<Item>". Finally, the Character may use all
applications of the Physician Skill on Constructs.
Archaeology, Advanced [Academic]
Prerequisites: Archaeology
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may deliver a
packet attack causing a "Physical Vanquish
Construct" effect. Additionally, a Character with the
Skill Smithing may use the Field Mend ability to
restore five(5) Hit Points to the target Construct.
Astrology [Academic]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: The Character gains knowledge of the stars
and their movements allowing them a number of
benefits. Firstly, the Character may act as a
Secondary Caster for any Ritual Magic effect cast
under the night sky. They may not be the Primary
Caster unless they have Ritual Magic Skill. If the
Character has the Ritual Magic Skill they count as an
additional Caster when performing any Ritual Magic
effect cast under the night sky. Finally, the Character
may also discern more accurate information when
using the Prophecy Ritual Magic effect.
Astrology, Advanced [Academic]
Prerequisites: Astrology
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may perform a
special divination ceremony taking at least five(5)
minutes. Once they have completed this ceremony
they may grant themselves the effects of a "Magic
Mirage 5".
Botany [Academic]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: The Character may also use the Diagnose
Skill on Plants. Additionally, they may, once per Day,
grant themselves the effects of a "Physical Poison
Shield". Finally, the Character may use all
applications of the Physician Skill on Plants.
Botany, Advanced [Academic]
Prerequisites: Botany
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may deliver a
packet attack causing a "Physical Vanquish Plant"
effect.
Cosmology [Academic]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: The Character may also use the Diagnose
Skill on Elementals. Additionally, they may, once per
Day, grant themselves the effects of a "Magic
Elemental Shield". Finally, the Character may use all
applications of the Physician Skill on Elementals.
Cosmology, Advanced [Academic]
Prerequisites: Cosmology
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may deliver a
packet attack causing a "Physical Vanquish
Elemental" effect.
Entomology [Academic]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: The Character may also use the Diagnose
Skill on Insectoids. Additionally, they may, once per
Day, grant themselves the effects of a "Poison Vorpal
5". Finally, the Character may use all applications of
the Physician Skill on Insectoids.
Entomology, Advanced [Academic]
Prerequisites: Entomology
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may deliver a
packet attack causing a "Physical Vanquish
Insectoid" effect.
History [Academic]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may spend
one(1) minute explaining to another, or muttering to
themselves, some bit of historical trivia to grant the
target, or themselves, the effects of a "Physical Curse
Shield".
History, Advanced [Academic]
Prerequisites: History
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may deliver, by
packet, the effects of a "Magic Frailty".
Necrology [Academic]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: The Character may also use the Diagnose
Skill on Undead. Additionally, they may, once per Day,
grant themselves the effects of a "Physical Magic
Shield". Finally, the Character may use all
applications of the Physician Skill on Undead.
Necrology, Advanced [Academic]
Prerequisites: Necrology
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may deliver a
packet attack causing a "Physical Vanquish Undead"
effect.
Zoology [Academic]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: The Character may also use the Diagnose
Skill on Animals and Hybrids. Additionally, they may,
once per Day, grant themselves the effects of a
"Physical Physical Shield". Finally, the Character may
use all applications of the Physician Skill on Animals
and Hybrids.
Zoology, Advanced [Academic]
Prerequisites: Zoology
Benefit: Once per Day, the Character may deliver a
packet attack causing a "20 Physical Body" damage
or "20 Physical Healing".
Merchant
The Merchant has learned to trade in a variety of
crafts and materials. Their efforts have been
rewarded with a number of connections allowing
them to acquire or offload valuables at market rates.
Merchant encompasses a number of different Sub-
Skills which may be purchased in any order so long
as the Character meets all prerequisites for the
chosen Sub-Skill.
Each Rank in this Skill a Character has grants
them a single Merchant Ticket. These tickets may be
used to enter special Merchant only draws for item
lots. A Merchant may place any number of their
tickets into any draw. These tickets are usable only
for the event they are issued and cannot be given
away or used by any other Character. The contents
of, and number of draws available at an Event is at
the discretion of the Marshal.
Merchant Sub-Skill Descriptions
Apothecary [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: This Skill grants the Character the ability to
Identify Potions and Poisons which are not in a living
target. This ability takes sixty(60)-seconds to use on
any number of targets the Character is holding.
Additionally, if the Character has the Sense Poison
Skill, they can also use this Skill to Identify Poisons
present in living creatures.
Appraiser [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: This Skill allows the Character to accurately
determine the coin value of Trinkets. A Trinket is any
Game Item which has a measurable value, usually of
at least one(1) gold piece.
Banker [Merchant]
Prerequisites: Read & Write
Benefit: This skill grants the character the ability to
invest their money to gain interest. Any money the
character has in the Bank gains one(1%) percent
interest each month. Additionally, the Character may
issue withdrawals or deposits to the Royal Bank
during an Event.
Chandler [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: This skill grants the character knowledge of
candle making. Once per Day, they may perform a
five(5) minute ceremony involving the creation of a
number of candles equal to the number of candles for
which they have Physical Representations. Once
created, any Character may expend the created
candle to deliver a single "Activate: Physical Lantern"
effect.
Additionally, once per year, the Character may
create an exceptionally well made candle with a value
of at least one-hundred(100) gold pieces. This item is
Legendary and can be used in the creation of an
eight(8) Ritual effect item. The Character must
expend an amount of gold equal to the value of the
item.
Chef [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: Once per Event, a Character with this skill
may prepare an exceptionally fortifying meal which
grants all who consume it five(5) additional Hit Points
until the end of the Day. Additionally, they may, once
per Day, create a meal which grants all who consume
it five(5) Fortification Points. Each additional
Character with the Chef Sub-Skill increases the
number of Hit Points, or Fortification Points by one(1).
A Character can never gain more than ten(10) Hit
Points from this ability.
Fence [Merchant]
Prerequisites: Appraiser
Benefit: This skill grants the Character the ability to
increase the amount of coin gained from selling items
to the Merchant's Guild by ten(10%) percent. This
Skill cannot be used on any Player created Trinkets.
Jeweller [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: This skill allows the Character to create
Trinkets with a value of ten(10), or more, gold pieces.
These items are Mastercrafted and are valid targets
for Ritual effects. Additionally, once per year, the
Character may create an exceptionally well made
piece of jewellery with a value of at least one-
hundred(100) gold pieces. This item is Legendary
and can be used in the creation of an eight(8) Ritual
effect item. The Character must expend an amount of
gold equal to the value of the item.
Leatherworker [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: This skill allows the Character to create
leather Trinkets with a value of ten(10), or more, gold
pieces. These items are Mastercrafted and are valid
targets for Ritual effects. Additionally, once per year,
the Character may create an exceptionally well made
piece of leatherwork with a value of at least one-
hundred(100) gold pieces. This item is Legendary
and can be used in the creation of an eight(8) Ritual
effect item. The Character must expend an amount of
gold equal to the value of the item.
Locksmith [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: The Character may, if they have a set of
lockpicks, attempt to open any in-game lock. If no
lock is present, the Character needs to role-play the
process as a sixty(60)-count.
If the Character has the Open Lock Skill, they
reduce the time needed to open a lock to ten(10)
seconds. If the lock has a limited number of tries,
they gain one(1) additional attempt.
Additionally, this skill allows the Character to create
locking Trinkets, such as locks, sealed containers and
chests, with a value of ten(10), or more, gold pieces.
These items are Mastercrafted and are valid targets
for Ritual effects. Additionally, once per year, the
Character may create an exceptionally well made
lock work with a value of at least one-hundred(100)
gold pieces. This item is Legendary and can be used
in the creation of an eight(8) Ritual effect item. The
Character must expend an amount of gold equal to
the value of the item.
Tailor [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: This skill allows the Character to create cloth
Trinkets, such as clothing, with a value of ten(10), or
more, gold pieces. These items are Mastercrafted
and are valid targets for Ritual effects. Additionally,
once per year, the Character may create an
exceptionally well made piece of clothing with a value
of at least one-hundred(100) gold pieces. This item is
Legendary and can be used in the creation of an
eight(8) Ritual effect item. The Character must
expend an amount of gold equal to the value of the
item.
Textiles Artist [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: This skill allows the Character to create
woven Trinkets, such as rugs, flags and tapestries,
with a value of ten(10), or more, gold pieces. These
items are Mastercrafted and are valid targets for
Ritual effects. Additionally, once per year, the
Character may create an exceptionally well made
piece of textile artistry with a value of at least one-
hundred(100) gold pieces. This item is Legendary
and can be used in the creation of an eight(8) Ritual
effect item. The Character must expend an amount of
gold equal to the value of the item.
Tinkerer [Merchant]
Prerequisites: None
Benefit: This skill grants the character knowledge of
proper journeyman repair techniques. Characters
with this skill may Repair Broken objects made of
Steel at a forge, as per the Smithing skill. If a
character possesses this skill and Field Mend in the
Smithing skill, then they can repair two(2) Mastercraft
items instead of one(1).
Additionally, if the Character possesses the Skill
Field Repair, they may use its ability twice before
needing to Rest.
Physician
After having mastered the art of Diagnose and
First Aid, a character is ready to uncover the secrets
of the living body. This skill gives a character a much
broader depth understanding of the workings of First
Aid and emergency surgery. The Physician skill
grants its users a number of abilities based on the
level of Physician they possess. In order to perform
surgery,the Physician must carry with them a doctor's
bag, minimum volume of four(4) litres, and medical
tools of the trade. These tools do not require a tag.
Characters can normally only use First Aid, and the
effects of this Skill on Humanoids. But if they have as
Skill which allows them to use First Aid on non-
Humanoid creatures they are able to use the abilities
of this Skill on those creatures as well. For example,
if they have Academic (Necrology), they can use their
skills on Undead.
Physician Sub-Skill Descriptions
In the following descriptions of the Physician Sub-
Skills, the following terms are in use; "Patient" refers
to the Character which is the target or beneficiary of
the Physician's skills. "Physician" refers to the
Character using the Skill.
Field Medic [Physician]
Prerequisites: 1 Rank in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill may perform First
Aid with greater versatility and efficiency than those
without it. Firstly, the Character is able to perform
First Aid on a target that is being carried. Secondly,
their First Aid count is not interrupted by Hit Point
Damage. Thirdly, they may perform First Aid on
two(2) targets simultaneously. Finally, when the
Character completes their First Aid count they may
also deliver two(2) points of Physical Healing to all
Characters they are performing First Aid upon. The
call for this effect is "First Aid Complete. 2 Physical
Healing".
Plague Doctor [Physician]
Prerequisites: 2 Ranks in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to identify
most non-magical diseases and cure the target of
them. Using this ability requires a one(1) minute
ceremony and requires the Patient to lie down and
remain still for its duration. During this ceremony the
Physician is required to role-play an examination and
the administration of medicine to the target. This
process is interrupted if either the Physician is
interrupted or either Character takes Hit Point
damage. Additionally, this ability allows the Character
to cure the target of many common afflictions. In
addition to the previously mentioned abilities, once
per Day the Character may deliver a "Physical Dispel
Curse" effect.
Physicker [Physician]
Prerequisites: 3 Ranks in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill may identify any
Poisons or mundane toxins present in the target and
flush them from the target's body. Using this ability
requires a one(1) minute examination and requires
the Patient to lie down and remain still for its duration.
During this ceremony the Physician is required to
role-play an examination and the administration of
medicine to the target. This process is interrupted if
either the Physician is interrupted or either Character
takes Hit Point damage. This Skill can be used on a
Patient no more than once per Day. The call for this
ability is "Physical Dispel Poison".
Additionally, the Character is able to perform a
special form of First Aid, called Field Dressing, on
targets that are not Dying. The Physician may spend
one(1) minute to heal the target a number of missing
Hit Points equal to their Ranks in Physician. Once a
target has benefited from this effect they must wear
an armband, supplied by the Physician, made of
gauze. The Patient cannot benefit from this ability
until they spend five(5) minutes Resting at which time
they may remove the armband and return it to the
Physician.
Once a Character has three(3) Ranks in this Skill,
they gain the ability to create a special Curative
Tincture. Once per Reset, the Character may deliver
a Touch effect causing five(5) points of Physical
Healing to the target. This Character is able to use
this ability once for every three(3) Ranks the
Character has in the Physician Skill. The Character
may Rest in order to regain all expended uses of this
ability. The maximum number of times per Reset the
Character can use this ability is equal to the number
of Ranks they have in the Physician Skill. For
example, a Character with six(6) Ranks in Physician
may deliver the effects of the Curative Tincture twice
before needing to Rest to recover the expended
uses, allowing them to use this ability a maximum of
six(6) times per Reset.
Surgeon, Novice [Physician]
Prerequisites: 4 Ranks in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill may perform
Surgery on Patients. This Surgery has two primary
uses, the first is to repair injury. This allows the
Character to heal a number of Hit Points equal to
twice the Character's Ranks in the Skill Physician.
The call for this ability is "# Physical Healing". The
second use of this ability is to heal mundane injuries
such as the removal of foreign objects and the setting
of broken bones. Using either of these abilities
requires a five(5) minute ceremony and requires the
Patient to lie down and either be Unconscious,
Sleeping, or Intoxicated for its duration. During this
ceremony the Physician is required to role-play an
examination and the administration of medicine to the
target. This process is interrupted if either the
Physician is interrupted or either Character takes Hit
Point damage. Once the Surgery is complete, the
target is affected by a "Piercing Physical Drain" effect.
A Character may benefit from Surgery no more than
once per Day with any attempts after the first ending
instead as a "Physical Death" in place of its normal
effect(s).
Additionally, the Character may, as a sixty(60)-
count, grant a Dead Character the effects of a
"Physical Tarry". Neither Character can be moved
during this count.
Finally, the number of targets the Physician may
affect with First Aid, or Field Dressing, simultaneously
is increased to three(3).
Doctor [Physician]
Prerequisites: 5 Ranks in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to perform
Surgery which is able to remove virtually all afflictions
on the target. Using this ability requires a five(5)
minute ceremony and requires the Patient to lie down
and either be Unconscious, Sleeping, or Intoxicated
for its duration. During this ceremony the Physician is
required to role-play an examination and the
administration of medicine to the target. This process
is interrupted if either the Physician is interrupted or
either Character takes Hit Point damage. Once the
Surgery is complete, the target is affected by the
effects "Physical Cleanse" and "Piercing Physical
Drain" in that order. A Character may benefit from
Surgery no more than once per Day with any
attempts after the first acting instead as a "Physical
Death".
Upon reaching five(5) Ranks in this Skill the
Character gains the ability to create yet another
Curative Tincture. Once per Reset, for every five(5)
Ranks the Character has in this Skill the Character
may deliver a "Physical Remove <Delivery Type>" of
any of the following Delivery Types; Elemental,
Magic, Physical, or Poison. This use of Curative
Tincture counts against the Character's maximum
uses Curative Tinctures before Resting and per
Reset.
Resuscitator [Physician]
Prerequisites: 6 Ranks in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to, as a
sixty(60)-second count, deliver a "Physical Life" effect
on a Dead target. Upon doing so, the Physician is
affected by a "Physical Drain". Alternatively, the
Character may "hand-off" the count to another,
willing, Physician, who has at least four(4) Ranks in
this Skill. If the Character chooses to have another
Physician take over for them it is the latter who is
affected by the "Physical Drain".
Additionally, the Character may, as a ten(10)-count,
grant a Dead Character the effects of a "Physical
Tarry". Neither Character can be moved during this
count.
Surgeon, Expert [Physician]
Prerequisites: 7 Ranks in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to perform
Surgery which is able to restore all the target's lost
Hit Points. Using this ability requires a five(5) minute
ceremony and requires the Patient to lie down and
either be Unconscious, Sleeping, or Intoxicated for its
duration. During this ceremony the Physician is
required to role-play an examination and the
administration of medicine to the target. This process
is interrupted if either the Physician is interrupted or
either Character takes Hit Point damage. Once the
Surgery is complete, or interrupted, the target is
affected by the effects "Physical Revive" and
"Piercing Physical Drain" in that order.
Additionally, the number of targets the
Physician may affect with First Aid, or Field Dressing,
simultaneously is increased to four(4).
Expert Medic [Physician]
Prerequisites: 8 Ranks in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to perform
Field Dressing on conscious Characters with greatly
increased speed and effectiveness. The Physician
may spend ten(10) seconds to heal the target a
number of missing Hit Points equal to their Ranks in
Physician. Once a target has benefited from this
effect they must wear an armband, supplied by the
Physician, made of gauze. The Patient cannot benefit
from this ability until they spend five(5) minutes
Resting at which time they may remove the armband
and return it to the Physician.
Master Medic [Physician]
Prerequisites: 9 Ranks in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to perform
First Aid even while under an effect which would
normally keep them from using Skills such as Drain,
though they must still be Conscious. Additionally, they
may perform First Aid while using another Skill such
as Diagnose. Finally, when the Character completes
their First Aid count they may also deliver five(5)
points of Physical Healing to all Characters they are
performing First Aid upon. The call for this effect is
"First Aid Complete. 5 Physical Healing".
Master Physician [Physician]
Prerequisites: 10 Ranks in Physician
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to perform
Surgery on one(1) additional target for each
Character with 5 Ranks in Physician that is assisting
them. Additionally, they may perform Surgery on a
Patient twice per Day and the Patient no longer
needs to be Unconscious, Sleeping or Intoxicated.
Finally, the number of targets the Physician may
affect with First Aid, or Field Dressing, simultaneously
is increased to five(5).
Upon reaching ten(10) Ranks in this Skill the
Character gains the ability to create yet another
Curative Tincture. Once per Reset, for every ten(10)
Ranks the Character has in this Skill the Character
may deliver a "Physical Cleanse" or "Physical Life"
effect by Touch. This use of Curative Tincture counts
against the Character's maximum uses Curative
Tinctures before Resting and per Reset.

Practised Medic [Physician]
Prerequisites: 15 Ranks in Physician plus 5 Ranks
for each additional time this Skill is bought.
Benefit: A Character with this skill increases the
number of targets the Physician may affect with First
Aid, or Field Dressing, simultaneously is increased by
one(1).
Practised Surgeon [Physician]
Prerequisites: 20 Ranks in Physician plus 10 Ranks
for each additional time this Skill is bought.
Benefit: A Character with this skill increases the
number of targets the Physician may affect with
Surgery simultaneously is increased by one(1).
Seer
A Seer is a Character who is able to perceive and
interact with the Spirit World. Though this Skill does
not grant the Character control of Spirits, it does allow
the Character to interact with them in significant
ways. Additionally, some Ranks of this Skill grant the
Character special abilities when Dead, or in the Spirit
World.
While most ceremonies described in this Skill have
a minimum duration of five(5) minutes, it is possible
that some Spirits may be exceptionally hostile or
resistant to being invoked. As a result, the duration of
the base ceremony may, at the Marshal's discretion,
be increased to up to one(1) hour.
Each Rank a Character has in this Skill grants
them access to one of the below abilities.
Seer Sub-Skill Descriptions
Spectral Sight [Seer]
Prerequisites: 1 Rank in Seer
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to perceive
the presence of Spirits that are normally invisible. A
Spirit is a creature which is in game but wearing a red
headband to indicate that it is invisible to most. A
Character with this Skill can notice the presence of
Spirits but cannot recognize, hear or converse with
the Spirit.
Speaker for the Dead [Seer]
Prerequisites: 2 Ranks in Seer
Benefit: Once per Day, a Character with this Skill is
able to temporarily call the Spirit of a deceased
creature back into its body in order to answer one(1)
question for each Rank in the Seer Skill the
Character possesses. The revived Spirit is able to do
nothing but answer the questions of the Seer with the
answers; "Yes.", "No.", or "I don't know.". The Spirit is
not required to tell the truth. This ability automatically
ends after five(5) minutes regardless of the number of
questions left unanswered.
Portent [Seer]
Prerequisites: 3 Ranks in Seer
Benefit: Once per Day, a Character with this Skill is
able to glimpse into the future. The Character may
spend a minimum of five(5) minutes, and a maximum
number of minutes equal to their Ranks in Seer,
performing a ceremony involving instruments of
portents such as astrology, rune stones, entrails,
bones, tarot, tea leaves, etc. Upon the completion of
this ceremony the Character may grant themselves,
or another Character who was present for, and the
target of, the ceremony, a single use of the Dodge
Skill. This skill is lost at the end of the Day and does
not count as a prerequisite for any Skill or effect.
Spectral Manifestation [Seer]
Prerequisites: 4 Ranks in Seer
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to
recognize Spirits that are normally invisible. A Spirit is
a creature which is in game but wearing a red
headband to indicate that it is invisible to most. A
Character with this Skill can also speak with Spirits
but at no more than a whisper. Additionally, once per
Day, the Character may, upon entering their Death
Count, manifest as a Spirit. While a Spirit, the Player
must wear a red headband and cannot speak except
to answer the question: "Who are you?". The Player
must remain within ten feet of where they died.
Should the Character be affected by a Life effect the
Spirit is immediately pulled back into their body. The
process of manifesting as a Spirit is traumatic to the
mind and as such a Character has no memories of
their time as a Spirit.
Speaker for the Departed [Seer]
Prerequisites: 5 Ranks in Seer
Benefit: Once per Day, a Character with this Skill is
able to invoke the spirits to provide them with
information about the past, present or future. Prior to
using this Skill the Character must inform a Plot
Marshal. The Character may spend a minimum of
five(5) minutes, and a maximum number of minutes
equal to their Ranks in Seer, performing a ceremony
involving instruments of portents such as astrology,
rune stones, entrails, bones, tarot, tea leaves, etc.
Upon the completion of this ceremony the Character
may ask the presiding Marshal a number of questions
equal to the number of minutes they spent performing
the ceremony. The answers may not be
straightforward, or even truthful, and will never
provide information on PCs.
Should the base duration of this ceremony be
greater than five(5) minutes, the Character may
reduce the amount of time required to complete it by
one(1) minute per Rank of Seer to a minimum of
five(5) minutes.

Otherworldly Voice [Seer]
Prerequisites: 6 Ranks in Seer
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to speak
normally with Spirits. Additionally, once per Day, the
Character may perform a five(5) minute ceremony to
invoke a particular spirit for information. In order to
use this ability the Character must know the name of,
or be in possession of an important item connected
to, the Spirit to be invoked. After they have completed
this Ceremony they may ask the Spirit a number of
questions equal to their Ranks in the Seer Skill. The
Spirit may not provide a clear answer and is not
required to tell the truth. Prior to using this ability the
Character must inform a Plot Marshal.
Seance [Seer]
Prerequisites: 7 Ranks in Seer
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to perform
a special ceremony meant to call and bind a
particular Spirit. To do so, the Seer must make use of
a large item to act as a focal point for the ceremony
such as a crystal ball, mirror or similar reflective
surface, as well as a number of additional implements
related to the specific Spirit, portents or other mystic
implements. The ceremony may include additional
Characters beyond the Seer. The base ceremony has
a ten(10%) percent chance of success and each
additional participant, with at least two(2) Ranks in
Seer, increases the chance of success by an
additional ten(10%) percent. Additionally, the Marshal
presiding over the ceremony may, at their discretion,
modify the chance of success, in response to; good
role-playing by the participants, ambience and
performance of the Ceremony. Upon completing this
five(5) minute ceremony the Spirit will be called into
the Circle and, if the ceremony was successful, be
bound. Once the Spirit is bound all participants must
link hands until the Spirit departs. If the participants
unlink hands prior to the departure of the Spirit, or the
Spirit is not successfully bound, all Participants are
reduced to one(1) Hit Point from the backlash of
spiritual energy. A bound spirit may be asked three(3)
questions by the Seer and is required to answer as
truthfully as they are capable. Once all three(3)
questions have been answered, or ten(10) minutes
have elapsed since the Spirit was bound, this effect
ends and the Spirit departs.
Should the base duration of this ceremony be
greater than five(5) minutes, the Character may
reduce the amount of time required to complete it by
one(1) minute per Rank of Seer to a minimum of
five(5) minutes.
Traverse the Veil [Seer]
Prerequisites: 8 Ranks in Seer
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to perform
a special ceremony meant to sever their Spirit's
connection to their physical body. Upon completing
this special five(5) minute ceremony the Character
immediately dies entering their Death Count
bypassing any Defensive Ability or immunity. The
Player must leave a large white cloth, all items which
are not Spirit Linked and a sign which states "You
see <Character's> body here." and don a red
headband. This white cloth should be approximately
the size of the Player who placed it. The Character is
a Spirit and may speak with, and use Skills upon
other Spirits. As a Spirit the Character is invisible to
most Characters and Immune to almost all attacks
and effects, While this ability is in effect the
Character's body is immune to Life and Tarry effects
unless their Spirit form is touching their physical body
represented by the white cloth. If the Seer does not
return to their body before their Death Count ends
they immediately seek Resurrection.
If the target is affected by an Arcane Life, or
Arcane Revive, effect they must immediately return to
their "body" as quickly as they are safely able to do
so.
Should the base duration of this ceremony be
greater than five(5) minutes, the Character may
reduce the amount of time required to complete it by
one(1) minute per Rank of Seer to a minimum of
five(5) minutes.
Beckon Haunting [Seer]
Prerequisites: 9 Ranks in Seer
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is able to perform
a special ceremony to summon a Spirit into a
structure or room. This ceremony has a number of
requirements including a doll, placed over the
entrance to the target room or structure, as well as an
offering bowl containing items meant to entice and
offer payment for the Spirit. The contents of the
offering bowl determine the type of Spirit summoned.
An offering of sweets, valuables and toys summon a
benevolent Spirit while offerings of junk, rotten food,
and worthless items bereft of meaning will summon a
malevolent Spirit. It is up to the presiding Marshal to
determine the type of Spirit summoned by the
offering. The Character must spend five(5) minutes
chanting, or preparing the area to host the Spirit.
Once the ceremony is complete, the Spirit will
haunt the area in which it was summoned. This has a
number of effects depending on the nature of the
Spirit summoned. A benevolent Spirit grants a
number of benefits including increasing the chance of
success of any Seance by ten(10%) percent.
Additionally, the Spirit will warn those within of any
intruders. This warning follows the rules for the Alarm
effect and cannot be removed unless the Spirit is
driven out. Finally, the benevolent Spirit may
occasionally tidy up the area. If a malevolent Spirit is
summoned, it causes a number of detriments, the
first is that no Seer Skill can be used within the
affected area. Additionally the malevolent Spirit may
move or hide items in the affected area. This effect
lasts one(1) year or until the effect is ended by the
Exorcism Ritual. A Seer may have only one(1)
Haunting in effect at a time.
A Seer, with access to the Haunted Room, may
negate this effect by performing this ceremony again.
Should the base duration of this ceremony be
greater than five(5) minutes, the Character may
reduce the amount of time required to complete it by
one(1) minute per Rank of Seer to a minimum of
five(5) minutes.
Transcend Mortality [Seer]
Prerequisites: 10 Ranks in Seer
Benefit: A Character with this Skill is perceived by
Spirits as a Spirit themselves and are much more
inclined to speak with them, though they are not
compelled to do so. Additionally, the Seer is able to
use the Spectral Manifestation Skill at will as well as
gaining the ability to speak normally while under this
effect. Finally, when the Character uses the Traverse
the Veil Skill, they benefit from a Tarry effect upon
activation of the Skill. In order to benefit from any part
of this Skill the Character must carry a lit Jack o'
Lantern.
Trophy Hunter
A Trophy Hunter is a Character who hunts
monsters, and occasionally people, for a price. The
Character has devoted much of their life to the study
of dangerous creatures whose components are
valuable to the right buyer, as well as rare resources
in areas rife with such creatures. Their training in
these areas mean that they are occasionally sought
out to capture particularly dangerous individuals who
have escaped the guards.
Trophy Hunters have a Badge which indicates their
Rank allowing for quick proof of their skill.
Bounty Hunter [Trophy Hunter]
Prerequisites: One(1) Rank in Trophy Hunter
Benefit: The Character gains access to Bounty
Missions, the details of this mission are provided to
all Trophy Hunters and vary from monster killing to
rescue missions.
Additionally, the Character is able to harvest
Trophies from creatures and certain areas. These
items can be traded but have no value to anyone
other than the Trophy Hunter's Guild.
Finally, once per Reset, for each Rank the
Character has in this Skill, the Character may deliver
a Physical-Strike Fetter effect using any Weapon with
which they are Proficient.
Lead Hunter [Trophy Hunter]
Prerequisites: Two(2) Ranks in Trophy Hunter
Benefit: The Character begins each Event with a
number of Trophies equal to the number of Days of
the Event.
Additionally, once per Reset, for every two(2)
Ranks the Character has in this Skill, the Character
may deliver a Physical-Strike Pin effect using any
Weapon with which they are Proficient.
Expert Hunter [Trophy Hunter]
Prerequisites: Three(3) Ranks in Trophy Hunter
Benefit: The Character may deputise one(1)
Character as a Trophy Hunters granting them the
ability to harvest Trophies as if they had one(1) Rank
in this Skill.
Additionally, they will receive a letter containing a
mission which they may complete for additional
Trophies.
Finally, once per Reset, the Character may deliver
a Physical-Strike Confine effect using any Weapon
with which they are Proficient.
Master Hunter [Trophy Hunter]
Prerequisites: Five(5) Ranks in Trophy Hunter
Benefit: The Character may deputise up to two(2)
other Characters as Trophy Hunters granting them
the ability to harvest Trophies as if they had one(1)
Rank in this Skill.
Additionally, the Character begins each Event with
a number of additional Trophies equal to the number
of Days of the Event. This stacks with the Trophies
received from Rank two(2) of this Skill for a total of
two(2) Trophies per Day of the Event.
Finally, the Character may use the abilities granted
by having one(1), two(2), and three(3) Ranks in this
Skill an additional time per Reset, but the Character
must Rest in between uses of these.
Trophy Exchange
Characters with this Skill are able to obtain
Trophies. These are a form of currency represented
by a variety of items described in Table 9.2. Trophies
may be traded or given to another Character once
they are received by the Trophy Hunter.
Trophy Trophy Value
Scale 1
Fang 5
Claw 25
Feather 50
Horn 100

As a Trophy Hunter accumulates Trophies they
may trade them to the "Trophy Hunting Guild", or
similar organization, in exchange for rewards
described in Table 9.3. Below is a list of the rewards,
typically, available to Trophy Hunters. Only a
Character with the Trophy Hunter Skill, or granted a
Rank temporarily by the Skills Expert Hunter or
Master Hunter, may turn in Trophies.

Chapter 10: Glossary and Miscellaneous Rules
Miscellaneous Rules
The following are rules which are not appropriate in
the other sections.
The Bank
Crucible uses a banking system in order for
players to store non-expiring game treasure in the
form of coins, ingots and similar items. This is done in
order to help supplement the limited availability of
these items and to reduce treasure accidentally being
lost. As a result the game exists in both an in and out
of game capacity. It exists as an in-game institution
for the storage of in-game resources but is otherwise
completely out of game and cannot be affected by
any in-game actions. Resources stored in the bank
can be retrieved only by the Character who deposited
the items.
Below is a list of items which can be stored in the
bank.
The Merchant Draw
Characters with the appropriate Skills will, at the
time of Event Registration, be given a number of
tickets for the Merchant Draw. These tickets cannot
be transferred to another Character or stolen. They
represent abstract connections the Character has
made with a large network of merchants throughout
the region. At the end of the Event any unused
Merchant Tickets must be destroyed.
At each Event there will be a number of Merchant
Lots which all Characters with Merchant Tickets may
enter. The lots will have a general description of the
contents but may be general. Characters may divide
their tickets in any number of these lots. At the end of
the Event a single ticket will be drawn from each
Merchant lot containing one(1) or more tickets. The
Character who holds the winning ticket stub will be
awarded the contents of the lot.
Under no circumstances may a Non-Player
Character enter or win a Merchant Draw. In the event
this should occur the ticket is discarded and another
is drawn in its place.
Item Can be Stored?
Currency Yes
Special Material Ingot Yes
Non-Expiring Ritual Components Yes
Potions/Scrolls Never
Mastercraft Item Never
Ritual Items Never
Plot Items Never
Table 10.1 Bankable Items
The Mailbox
The Mail system which Characters to communicate
with others through letters. If this rule is in use, a
Mailbox will be available for use by all Characters
allowing those with the ability to read and write to
send letters. Letters may be sent to any character so
long as the sender knows the recipient's name. Mail
is not instantaneous, nor does it even communicate
at a predictable rate. Only letters may be deposited
into the mailbox. Only Staff may open the mailbox to
retrieve letters.
Pre-Event Logistics
In order to accommodate the large number of
logistical activities that are carried out immediately at
the start of an Event certain activities can be
performed in advance. Effects which are placed upon
your sleeping area and personal belongings, such as
Sanctuary and Safeguard may be cast before the
beginning of gameplay. Spells cast upon a shared
sleeping area must include all those assigned to, or
set up to, sleep in that area. Additionally, certain
Ritual effects may be cast in advance by informing
Logistics in advance of the Event.
Costumes and Disguises
While Players are encouraged to be creative when
designing their Character's costume there are some
artificial limitations in what Characters can wear do to
the limits of of our own world. Firstly, Player
Characters cannot wear monster masks which may
cause others to mistake them for a monster. It would
be obvious to even a casual observer that a human
wearing cloth skeleton bandanna is not really a
skeleton but there is little difference between a PC
wearing a skull mask and an NPC wearing a similar
one. As a result, the only masks that Player
Characters are permitted to use are coloured
bandanna, domino masks and veils provided that
these do not depict any creature.
All Player Characters are recognisable as
themselves at all times. This is unfortunately
necessary as some Players may split their time
playing multiple Characters during an Event or take
spend some time helping to NPC adventures.
Additional Rooms
Event Staff may, at their discretion, designate
additional areas as valid targets for effects which
target a Room, such as the Sanctuary effect, when
they believe the type of available accommodations, or
other circumstances of the Event warrant them.
Location of Town
Event Staff may, at their discretion, designate a
specific location as being "Town" for the purpose of
effects, such as Teleport, which return a Character to
Town. Their discretion even allows them to designate
a specific area as "Town" for the purposes of effects
requiring you to be in Town such as Rest.
Character Ritual Item Slots
Each Character is able to use no more than five(5)
Ritual Items at once. An item is considered to be a
Ritual Item, and subject to this limit, if it has at least
one(1) Ritual Effect. Should a Character have more
than five(5) such items in their possession they are
unable to use any of the effects contained in the
additional items unless they reduce the number of
items they have in their possession to five(5) or
fewer.

Town Forge, Potion and Scroll Laboratories
Each town includes a Town Forge, Scroll and
Potion Laboratory which is freely usable by all Player
Characters. These laboratory include a number of
props and paraphernalia related to the crafted items
such as hammers, anvils for Smiths. Inks, quills and
papers for Scrolls. Herbs, vials of liquid and mixing
devices for Potions. Players are not permitted to
remove items from these laboratories. The supplies
for these laboratories are unlimited as each Player
must provide their own props for any Scrolls or
Potions they create with the aid of the laboratory.
Additionally, Players may, with the approval of
Staff, create their own private laboratories but these
areas face a number of restrictions. Firstly, they can
only be placed in private areas where they will not
interfere with gameplay. Placing them in high traffic or
public areas is not allowed. Secondly, they must be
inspected and confirmed to meet a minimum
standard of immersion as determined by Staff. Finally,
they must be included in the Marshal notes for any in
game area in which they are placed.
Glossary
The following is a glossary of all game specific
terms as well as general shorthand and commonly
used slang used as shorthand.
Blanket – This refers to an amount of Experience
Points equal to their Character's current Skill Point
Total.
Character – This refers to the creature, Player
Character or other in game character.
Circle – A Circle is a closed loop with a total diameter
of no more than 5 metres and a total circumference of
no more than 16 metres. All Circles must be lit, either
because they are in an area which is constantly
illuminated or because the Circle itself lights up.
Circles can never be hidden and must be visible to an
observer standing within five(5) metres of the Circle.
# Count (aka Counted Action) – This is an action
which takes some amount of time for the Character to
perform. Unless explicitly stated otherwise, in the
ability's description or the Character's Skill Card, all
counts are interrupted if; the Character is struck by
any effect other than Touch Casting, the Character
uses any Skills, or enters a state where they are
unable to use Skills. A Character can perform only
one count at a time. This section applies only to
actions taken by the Character. It does not apply to a
Character's Death Count and similar timed effects.
Day – This refers to a Game Day. Events have a
number of game Days, usually two(2), that they run.
Unless the Event is only one(1) Day long, each Day
ends at 18:00 and the next one begins. Effects which
end at the end of the Day automatically expire at
18:00 or the end of the Event, whichever occurs first.
Encounter (aka Module or Adventure) – This refers
to a planned encounter run, or instigated, by an
Encounter Marshal as part of the Event's Plot.
Event – This refers to the event itself. Effects which
last until the end of the Event, as well as all Scrolls
and Potions automatically expire at the conclusion of
the Event.
Experience Points ((E)XP) – This refers to the
Character's accumulated experience total. As the
Character gains Experience Points these points are
automatically converted into Skill Points at a ratio
determined by the Character's Level.
Humanoid – This refers to all Player Characters as
well as similar creatures.
Invested – Certain effects, such as Sanctuary and
Protective Circles, have Characters Invested in them.
An Invested Character is able to enter or exit the
effect. Any other Character is prevented from passing
through the effect, entering or exiting, unless they are
"Recognized" through the effect by a Character who
is Invested. Characters can become Invested
according to the rules defined by the individual
effects.
Jack o' Lantern – This refers to the prop needed for
the Rank 10 Seer ability Transcend Mortality. The
Jack o' Lantern must appear to be a carved lantern
depicting a face which is lit from within. The lantern
should be between 1 and 10 litres in volume.
Kata – This refers to a special five(5) minute Martial
Arts ability which grants the Character one of several
special abilities dependent on the one performed.
Marshal – This is a local member of staff who
oversees one or more aspects of an event. There are
many types of Marshals ranging from Weapon
Marshals, to Encounter Marshals.
Meditate (aka Meditation) – This is a special
sixty(60)-second Count where the Character recovers
all Skills which failed to strike any target or were
negated by the Defensive Abilities: Immunity, Resist
and Ward. This also includes any abilities which
explicitly state they can be recovered by Meditating.
Regardless of how many Skills the Character is
recovering, the Character needs to Meditate only
once to recover all of them. Meditating is interrupted if
the Character is struck by any effect, other than
Touch Casting, the Character uses any Skills, or
enters a state where they are unable to use Skills.
The Clarity effect grants the Character the benefit of
Meditating instantly.
Overhealing – This refers to any healing a Character
is affected by which would put them above their
maximum Hit Point total. For example, if a Character
is affected by an effect causing "30 Magic Healing"
but the Character is missing only fourteen(14) Hit
Points they would be healed to full and would have
sixteen(16) points of Overhealing. Normally
Overhealing is lost but some abilities, such as the
Narahk's Runic Flesh ability, allow the Character to
take advantage of it in some way. Additionally, some
effects, such as the Spell Feast of Flesh, have
specific effects when Overhealing.
Player – This refers to the Player themselves and not
their Character.
Player Character (aka PC) – This refers specifically
to Characters created by and under the control of
their Player.
Plot – This refers to the group of Marshals who run
the Event.
Non-Player Character (aka NPC) – This refers to
Characters who are under the control of Plot. They
may be indistinguishable from a PC or be something
more unusual.
Rest – This refers to a special five(5) minute action
where a Character recuperates from combat. It can
only be performed in Town or at a designated Rest
area. Certain Skills and abilities can be recovered if
the Character Rests. If the Character has multiple
abilities which require a Rest they may recover all of
them with a single Rest. A Rest is interrupted if the
Character is struck by any effect, other than Touch
Casting, the Character uses any Skills, or enters a
state where they are unable to use Skills.
Ritual – This refers to any of the various Ritual
effects which can be performed by a Character with
the appropriate skills. These can be used to create
items imbued with special abilities, summon servants
that obey the caster's instruction or perform various
other effects beyond what is possible with magic.
Room - A Room is any immovable structure, or area
within such a structure which has clearly recognized
boundaries such as walls and a doorway through
which to enter the area, which includes a ceiling or
roof. It includes, but is not limited to constructed
buildings, caves, tents which are secured to the
ground, covered wagons which have been rendered
immobile and similar areas. An effect which targets a
Room can never be placed on an area which has
been designated as out of game such as bathrooms.
Skill – This refers to all abilities which are purchased
using Skill Points unless the Skill explicitly states
otherwise.
Skill Points (aka SP) – This refers to the currency
used by Characters to purchase Skills for their
Character.
Spell – This refers to all Techniques which are a form
of Magic. Normally these effects are derived from
Skills but some monsters have the ability to use them
as well.
Town – A small number of effects refer to Town. This
is generally the area where the Players sleep
although the Event Staff may designate, at the start
of the Event, a more specific location. For example,
Staff may designate that Town is the main floor of the
Inn, or the seating area of the firepit. When such a
location is designated, effects which require the
Character to be in Town, such as Resting, require the
Character to enter these areas to benefit from these
effects. Finally, effects, such as Teleport, which return
the Character to Town require the Character to enter
the designated area before resolving them.
